RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/merge_sort.v
// Stable bottom-up merge sort. One merge output or copy-back per cycle.

// =====================================================================
// merge_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
// control[1]: width <= 1;
// control[2]: left <= 0;
// control[3]: middle <= (left + width < N)? left + width : N;
// control[4]: limit <= (left + 2 * width < N)? left + 2 * width : N;
// control[5]: i <= left;
// control[6]: j <= (left + width < N)? left + width : N;
// control[7]: p <= left;
// control[8]: p <= left;
// control[9]: temp[p] <= mem[i];
// control[10]: i <= i + 1;
// control[11]: temp[p] <= mem[j];
// control[12]: j <= j + 1;
// control[13]: p <= p + 1;
// control[14]: mem[p] <= temp[p];
// control[15]: p <= p + 1;
// control[16]: left <= limit;
// control[17]: width <= width * 2;
// control[18]: left <= 0;
// control[19]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module merge_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [6:0] status,
  output reg [19:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);

reg [2:0] state;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg [2:0] next_state;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  state <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  state <= next_state;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_state = state;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      next_state = (N == 1)? 4 : 0;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) case(state)
      0: begin
        control[3] = 1'b1;
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        control[6] = 1'b1;
        control[7] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 1;
      end
      1: begin
        if(status[0]) begin
          control[8] = 1'b1;
          next_state = 2;
        end else begin
          if(status[1] && (status[2] || status[3])) begin
            control[9] = 1'b1;
            control[10] = 1'b1;
          end else begin
            control[11] = 1'b1;
            control[12] = 1'b1;
          end
          control[13] = 1'b1;
        end
      end
      2: begin
        control[14] = 1'b1;
        if(status[4]) next_state = 3;
        else control[15] = 1'b1;
      end
      3: if(status[5]) begin
        control[16] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 0;
      end else if(status[6]) next_state = 4;
      else begin
        control[17] = 1'b1;
        control[18] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 0;
      end
      4: begin
        control[19] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
      default: next_state = 0;
    endcase
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module merge_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [19:0] control,
  output [6:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [W-1:0] mem [0:N-1];
reg [W-1:0] temp [0:N-1];
integer width;
integer left;
integer middle;
integer limit;
integer i;
integer j;
integer p;
integer k;
wire le;
assign le = (SIGNED)? $signed(mem[i]) <= $signed(mem[j]) : mem[i] <= mem[j];

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (p == limit);
assign status[1] = (i < middle);
assign status[2] = (j >= limit);
assign status[3] = le;
assign status[4] = (p == limit - 1);
assign status[5] = (limit < N);
assign status[6] = (2 * width >= N);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  width <= 1;
  left <= 0;
  middle <= 0;
  limit <= 0;
  i <= 0;
  j <= 0;
  p <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) begin
    mem[k] <= 0;
    temp[k] <= 0;
  end
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    width <= 1;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    left <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    middle <= (left + width < N)? left + width : N;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    limit <= (left + 2 * width < N)? left + 2 * width : N;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    i <= left;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    j <= (left + width < N)? left + width : N;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    p <= left;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    p <= left;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    temp[p] <= mem[i];
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    i <= i + 1;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    temp[p] <= mem[j];
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    j <= j + 1;
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
  if(control[14]) begin
    mem[p] <= temp[p];
  end
  if(control[15]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
  if(control[16]) begin
    left <= limit;
  end
  if(control[17]) begin
    width <= width * 2;
  end
  if(control[18]) begin
    left <= 0;
  end
  if(control[19]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module merge_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [19:0] control;
wire [6:0] status;
merge_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
merge_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Sortingمرتب‌سازی

Heap Sortمرتب‌سازی هیپ heap_sort

In-place max-heap sort; one sift-down comparison/swap per cycleمرتب‌سازی هیپ بیشینهٔ درجا؛ در هر کلاک یک مقایسه/جابه‌جایی sift-down.

rtl/heap_sort.v

Explanationتوضیح

In-place max-heap sort; one sift-down comparison/swap per cycle

The controller copies the input array into internal storage, runs the sorting FSM, and drives the sorted array on data_out. Use the start/busy/done handshake.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus heap_sort_controller and heap_sort_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, SIGNED
  • Testbench: Covered by the shared tb/tb_sorting.v testbench (see Selection Sort).

مرتب‌سازی هیپ بیشینهٔ درجا؛ در هر کلاک یک مقایسه/جابه‌جایی sift-down.

کنترلر آرایهٔ ورودی را در حافظهٔ داخلی کپی می‌کند، FSM مرتب‌سازی را اجرا می‌کند و آرایهٔ مرتب را روی data_out می‌گذارد. از handshake start/busy/done استفاده کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه heap_sort_controller و heap_sort_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED
  • تست‌بنچ: توسط تست‌بنچ مشترک tb/tb_sorting.v پوشش داده می‌شود (نمونه: Selection Sort).

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/heap_sort.v
// In-place max-heap sort, one sift-down comparison/swap per cycle.

// =====================================================================
// heap_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
// control[1]: size <= N;
// control[2]: build_root <= N / 2 - 1;
// control[3]: root <= N / 2 - 1;
// control[4]: mem[root] <= mem[child];
// control[5]: mem[child] <= mem[root];
// control[6]: root <= child;
// control[7]: build_root <= build_root - 1;
// control[8]: root <= build_root - 1;
// control[9]: mem[0] <= mem[size - 1];
// control[10]: mem[size - 1] <= mem[0];
// control[11]: size <= size - 1;
// control[12]: root <= 0;
// control[13]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module heap_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [3:0] status,
  output reg [13:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);

reg building;
reg [2:0] state;
reg next_building;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg [2:0] next_state;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  building <= 0;
  state <= 0;
end else begin
  building <= next_building;
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  state <= next_state;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_building = building;
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_state = state;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_building = 1;
      next_state = (N == 1)? 3 : 0;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) case(state)
      0: begin
        if(status[0] && status[1]) begin
          control[4] = 1'b1;
          control[5] = 1'b1;
          control[6] = 1'b1;
        end else next_state = 1;
      end
      1: if(building && status[2]) begin
        control[7] = 1'b1;
        control[8] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 0;
      end else begin
        next_building = 0;
        next_state = 2;
      end
      2: if(status[3]) next_state = 3;
      else begin
        control[9] = 1'b1;
        control[10] = 1'b1;
        control[11] = 1'b1;
        control[12] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 0;
      end
      3: begin
        control[13] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
      default: next_state = 0;
    endcase
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module heap_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [13:0] control,
  output [3:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [W-1:0] mem [0:N-1];
integer build_root;
integer root;
integer size;
integer child;
integer k;

function [0:0] greater;
  input [W-1:0] a;input [W-1:0] b;
  begin
    greater = (SIGNED)? $signed(a) > $signed(b) : a > b;
  end
endfunction


always @(*) begin
  child = 2 * root + 1;
  if(child + 1 < size) if(greater(mem[child + 1], mem[child])) child = child + 1;
end


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (child < size);
assign status[1] = greater(mem[child], mem[root]);
assign status[2] = (build_root > 0);
assign status[3] = (size <= 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  build_root <= 0;
  root <= 0;
  size <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    size <= N;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    build_root <= N / 2 - 1;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    root <= N / 2 - 1;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    mem[root] <= mem[child];
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    mem[child] <= mem[root];
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    root <= child;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    build_root <= build_root - 1;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    root <= build_root - 1;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    mem[0] <= mem[size - 1];
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    mem[size - 1] <= mem[0];
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    size <= size - 1;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    root <= 0;
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module heap_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [13:0] control;
wire [3:0] status;
heap_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
heap_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Shell Sortمرتب‌سازی شل shell_sort

Shell sort with halving gaps and gapped insertionمرتب‌سازی شل با گام‌های نصف‌شونده و درج گامی.

rtl/shell_sort.v

Explanationتوضیح

Shell sort with halving gaps and gapped insertion

The controller copies the input array into internal storage, runs the sorting FSM, and drives the sorted array on data_out. Use the start/busy/done handshake.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus shell_sort_controller and shell_sort_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, SIGNED
  • Testbench: Covered by the shared tb/tb_sorting.v testbench (see Selection Sort).

مرتب‌سازی شل با گام‌های نصف‌شونده و درج گامی.

کنترلر آرایهٔ ورودی را در حافظهٔ داخلی کپی می‌کند، FSM مرتب‌سازی را اجرا می‌کند و آرایهٔ مرتب را روی data_out می‌گذارد. از handshake start/busy/done استفاده کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه shell_sort_controller و shell_sort_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED
  • تست‌بنچ: توسط تست‌بنچ مشترک tb/tb_sorting.v پوشش داده می‌شود (نمونه: Selection Sort).

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: data_in"] --> M["shell_sort"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: data_out"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح
W8Data widthعرض داده
N8Number of elements / sizeتعداد عناصر / اندازه
SIGNED01 = signed two's-complement۱ = علامت‌دار مکمل دو

Portsپورت‌ها

DirجهتNameنامWidthعرضDescriptionتوضیح
inputclk1Clockکلاک
inputrst_n1Reset, active lowریست فعال پایین
inputstart1Start pulseپالس شروع
inputdata_inN*WInput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ ورودی
outputdata_outN*WOutput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ خروجی
outputbusy1Operation in progressدر حال اجرا
outputdone1One-cycle completion pulseپایان (یک کلاک)

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/shell_sort.v
// Shell sort using halving gaps and gapped insertion, one shift per cycle.

// =====================================================================
// shell_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
// control[1]: gap <= N / 2;
// control[2]: i <= N / 2;
// control[3]: key <= mem[i];
// control[4]: j <= i;
// control[5]: mem[j] <= mem[j - gap];
// control[6]: j <= j - gap;
// control[7]: mem[j] <= key;
// control[8]: i <= i + 1;
// control[9]: gap <= gap / 2;
// control[10]: i <= gap / 2;
// control[11]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module shell_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [3:0] status,
  output reg [11:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);

reg [1:0] state;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg [1:0] next_state;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  state <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  state <= next_state;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_state = state;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      next_state = (N == 1)? 3 : 0;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) case(state)
      0: begin
        control[3] = 1'b1;
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 1;
      end
      1: if(status[0] && status[1]) begin
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        control[6] = 1'b1;
      end else begin
        control[7] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 2;
      end
      2: if(status[2]) begin
        control[8] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 0;
      end else if(status[3]) begin
        control[9] = 1'b1;
        control[10] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 0;
      end else next_state = 3;
      3: begin
        control[11] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
      default: next_state = 0;
    endcase
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module shell_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [11:0] control,
  output [3:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [W-1:0] mem [0:N-1];
reg [W-1:0] key;
integer gap;
integer i;
integer j;
integer k;
wire greater;
assign greater = (SIGNED)? $signed(mem[(j >= gap)? j - gap : 0]) > $signed(key) : mem[(j >= gap)? j - gap : 0] > key;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (j >= gap);
assign status[1] = greater;
assign status[2] = (i + 1 < N);
assign status[3] = (gap > 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  gap <= 0;
  i <= 0;
  j <= 0;
  key <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    gap <= N / 2;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    i <= N / 2;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    key <= mem[i];
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    j <= i;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    mem[j] <= mem[j - gap];
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    j <= j - gap;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    mem[j] <= key;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    i <= i + 1;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    gap <= gap / 2;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    i <= gap / 2;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module shell_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [11:0] control;
wire [3:0] status;
shell_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
shell_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Sortingمرتب‌سازی

Gnome Sortمرتب‌سازی گنوم gnome_sort

Gnome sort; walk forward when ordered, swap/backtrack on inversionمرتب‌سازی گنوم؛ جلو رفتن در ترتیب و جابه‌جایی/عقب‌گرد در نابه‌جایی.

rtl/gnome_sort.v

Explanationتوضیح

Gnome sort; walk forward when ordered, swap/backtrack on inversion

The controller copies the input array into internal storage, runs the sorting FSM, and drives the sorted array on data_out. Use the start/busy/done handshake.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus gnome_sort_controller and gnome_sort_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, SIGNED
  • Testbench: Covered by the shared tb/tb_sorting.v testbench (see Selection Sort).

مرتب‌سازی گنوم؛ جلو رفتن در ترتیب و جابه‌جایی/عقب‌گرد در نابه‌جایی.

کنترلر آرایهٔ ورودی را در حافظهٔ داخلی کپی می‌کند، FSM مرتب‌سازی را اجرا می‌کند و آرایهٔ مرتب را روی data_out می‌گذارد. از handshake start/busy/done استفاده کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه gnome_sort_controller و gnome_sort_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED
  • تست‌بنچ: توسط تست‌بنچ مشترک tb/tb_sorting.v پوشش داده می‌شود (نمونه: Selection Sort).

    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: data_out"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/gnome_sort.v
// Gnome sort: walk forward when ordered, swap/backtrack on inversion.

// =====================================================================
// gnome_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
// control[1]: p <= 1;
// control[2]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
// control[3]: p <= p + 1;
// control[4]: mem[p - 1] <= mem[p];
// control[5]: mem[p] <= mem[p - 1];
// control[6]: p <= p - 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module gnome_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [2:0] status,
  output reg [6:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(status[0]) begin
        control[2] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else if(status[1] || status[2]) control[3] = 1'b1;
      else begin
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        control[6] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module gnome_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [6:0] control,
  output [2:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [W-1:0] mem [0:N-1];
integer p;
integer k;
wire ordered;
assign ordered = (SIGNED)? $signed(mem[((p > 0) && (p < N))? p - 1 : 0]) <= $signed(mem[(p < N)? p : 0]) : mem[((p > 0) && (p < N))? p - 1 : 0] <= mem[(p < N)? p : 0];

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (p >= N);
assign status[1] = (p == 0);
assign status[2] = ordered;

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  p <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    p <= 1;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    mem[p - 1] <= mem[p];
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    mem[p] <= mem[p - 1];
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    p <= p - 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module gnome_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [6:0] control;
wire [2:0] status;
gnome_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
gnome_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/cocktail_sort.v
// Bidirectional bubble (cocktail shaker) sort, one adjacent pair per cycle.

// =====================================================================
// cocktail_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
// control[1]: low <= 0;
// control[2]: high <= N - 1;
// control[3]: p <= 0;
// control[4]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
// control[5]: mem[p] <= mem[p + 1];
// control[6]: mem[p + 1] <= mem[p];
// control[7]: high <= high - 1;
// control[8]: p <= high - 2;
// control[9]: p <= p + 1;
// control[10]: low <= low + 1;
// control[11]: p <= low + 1;
// control[12]: p <= p - 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module cocktail_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [4:0] status,
  output reg [12:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);

reg reverse;
reg finish;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg next_finish;
reg next_reverse;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  reverse <= 0;
  finish <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  finish <= next_finish;
  reverse <= next_reverse;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_finish = finish;
  next_reverse = reverse;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_reverse = 0;
      next_finish = N == 1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(finish) begin
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else begin
        if(status[0]) begin
          control[5] = 1'b1;
          control[6] = 1'b1;
        end
        if(!reverse) begin
          if(status[1]) begin
            control[7] = 1'b1;
            next_reverse = 1;
            control[8] = 1'b1;
            if(status[2]) next_finish = 1;
          end else control[9] = 1'b1;
        end else if(status[3]) begin
          control[10] = 1'b1;
          next_reverse = 0;
          control[11] = 1'b1;
          if(status[4]) next_finish = 1;
        end else control[12] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module cocktail_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [12:0] control,
  output [4:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [W-1:0] mem [0:N-1];
integer low;
integer high;
integer p;
integer k;
wire greater;
assign greater = (SIGNED)? $signed(mem[p]) > $signed(mem[(p + 1 < N)? p + 1 : 0]) : mem[p] > mem[(p + 1 < N)? p + 1 : 0];

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = greater;
assign status[1] = (p == high - 1);
assign status[2] = (high - 1 <= low);
assign status[3] = (p == low);
assign status[4] = (low + 1 >= high);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  low <= 0;
  high <= 0;
  p <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    low <= 0;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    high <= N - 1;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    mem[p] <= mem[p + 1];
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    mem[p + 1] <= mem[p];
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    high <= high - 1;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    p <= high - 2;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    low <= low + 1;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    p <= low + 1;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    p <= p - 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module cocktail_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [12:0] control;
wire [4:0] status;
cocktail_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
cocktail_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Sortingمرتب‌سازی

Comb Sortمرتب‌سازی شانه‌ای comb_sort

Comb sort with 1.3 shrink factor; final gap-1 pass repeats until no swapsمرتب‌سازی شانه‌ای با ضریب ۱٫۳ و پاس نهایی گام ۱ تا بدون جابه‌جایی.

rtl/comb_sort.v

Explanationتوضیح

Comb sort with 1.3 shrink factor; final gap-1 pass repeats until no swaps

The controller copies the input array into internal storage, runs the sorting FSM, and drives the sorted array on data_out. Use the start/busy/done handshake.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus comb_sort_controller and comb_sort_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, SIGNED
  • Testbench: Covered by the shared tb/tb_sorting.v testbench (see Selection Sort).

مرتب‌سازی شانه‌ای با ضریب ۱٫۳ و پاس نهایی گام ۱ تا بدون جابه‌جایی.

کنترلر آرایهٔ ورودی را در حافظهٔ داخلی کپی می‌کند، FSM مرتب‌سازی را اجرا می‌کند و آرایهٔ مرتب را روی data_out می‌گذارد. از handshake start/busy/done استفاده کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه comb_sort_controller و comb_sort_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED
  • تست‌بنچ: توسط تست‌بنچ مشترک tb/tb_sorting.v پوشش داده می‌شود (نمونه: Selection Sort).

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: data_in"] --> M["comb_sort"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: data_out"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح
W8Data widthعرض داده
N8Number of elements / sizeتعداد عناصر / اندازه
SIGNED01 = signed two's-complement۱ = علامت‌دار مکمل دو

Portsپورت‌ها

DirجهتNameنامWidthعرضDescriptionتوضیح

inputrst_n1Reset, active lowریست فعال پایین
inputstart1Start pulseپالس شروع
inputdata_inN*WInput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ ورودی
outputdata_outN*WOutput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ خروجی
outputbusy1Operation in progressدر حال اجرا
outputdone1One-cycle completion pulseپایان (یک کلاک)

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/comb_sort.v
// Comb sort with a 1.3 shrink factor. Final gap=1 repeats until no swaps.

// =====================================================================
// comb_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
// control[1]: gap <= (N * 10 / 13 > 0)? N * 10 / 13 : 1;
// control[2]: p <= 0;
// control[3]: changed <= 0;
// control[4]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
// control[5]: mem[p] <= mem[p + gap];
// control[6]: mem[p + gap] <= mem[p];
// control[7]: changed <= 1;
// control[8]: gap <= new_gap;
// control[9]: p <= 0;
// control[10]: changed <= 0;
// control[11]: p <= p + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module comb_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [4:0] status,
  output reg [11:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);

reg finish;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg next_finish;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  finish <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  finish <= next_finish;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_finish = finish;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_finish = N == 1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(finish) begin
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else begin
        if(status[0]) begin
          control[5] = 1'b1;
          control[6] = 1'b1;
          control[7] = 1'b1;
        end
        if(status[1]) begin
          if(status[2] && status[3] && status[4]) next_finish = 1;
          else begin
            control[8] = 1'b1;
            control[9] = 1'b1;
            control[10] = 1'b1;
          end
        end else control[11] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module comb_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [11:0] control,
  output [4:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [W-1:0] mem [0:N-1];
integer gap;
integer p;
integer k;
integer new_gap;
reg changed;
wire greater;
assign greater = (SIGNED)? $signed(mem[p]) > $signed(mem[(p + gap < N)? p + gap : 0]) : mem[p] > mem[(p + gap < N)? p + gap : 0];

always @(*) begin
  new_gap = gap * 10 / 13;
  if(new_gap < 1) new_gap = 1;
end


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = greater;
assign status[1] = (p + gap == N - 1);
assign status[2] = (gap == 1);
assign status[3] = (!changed);
assign status[4] = (!greater);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  gap <= 1;
  p <= 0;
  changed <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    gap <= (N * 10 / 13 > 0)? N * 10 / 13 : 1;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    changed <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    mem[p] <= mem[p + gap];
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    mem[p + gap] <= mem[p];
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    changed <= 1;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    gap <= new_gap;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    changed <= 0;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module comb_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [11:0] control;
wire [4:0] status;
comb_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
comb_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Odd-even transposition sort; alternating even/odd adjacent passesمرتب‌سازی جابه‌جایی فرد-زوج با پاس‌های متناوب.

Odd-even transposition sort; alternating even/odd adjacent passes

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus odd_even_sort_controller and odd_even_sort_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/odd_even_sort.v
// Odd-even transposition sort; N alternating even/odd adjacent passes.

// =====================================================================
// odd_even_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
// control[1]: pass <= 0;
// control[2]: p <= 0;
// control[3]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
// control[4]: pass <= pass + 1;
// control[5]: p <= 1 - pass % 2;
// control[6]: mem[p] <= mem[p + 1];
// control[7]: mem[p + 1] <= mem[p];
// control[8]: p <= p + 2;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module odd_even_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [2:0] status,
  output reg [8:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);

reg finish;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg next_finish;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  finish <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  finish <= next_finish;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_finish = finish;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      next_finish = N == 1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(finish) begin
        control[3] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else if(status[0]) begin
        if(status[1]) next_finish = 1;
        else begin
          control[4] = 1'b1;
          control[5] = 1'b1;
        end
      end else begin
        if(status[2]) begin
          control[6] = 1'b1;
          control[7] = 1'b1;
        end
        control[8] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module odd_even_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [8:0] control,
  output [2:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [W-1:0] mem [0:N-1];
integer pass;
integer p;
integer k;
wire greater;
assign greater = (SIGNED)? $signed(mem[(p < N)? p : 0]) > $signed(mem[(p + 1 < N)? p + 1 : 0]) : mem[(p < N)? p : 0] > mem[(p + 1 < N)? p + 1 : 0];

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (p + 1 >= N);
assign status[1] = (pass == N - 1);
assign status[2] = greater;

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  pass <= 0;
  p <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    pass <= 0;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    pass <= pass + 1;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    p <= 1 - pass % 2;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    mem[p] <= mem[p + 1];
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    mem[p + 1] <= mem[p];
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    p <= p + 2;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module odd_even_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [8:0] control;
wire [2:0] status;
odd_even_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
odd_even_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Sortingمرتب‌سازی

Radix Sortمرتب‌سازی رادیکس radix_sort

Stable binary LSD radix sort; sign bit flipped in the last pass for signed dataمرتب‌سازی رادیکس دودویی LSD پایدار؛ در پاس آخر بیت علامت برای دادهٔ علامت‌دار برعکس می‌شود.

rtl/radix_sort.v

Explanationتوضیح

Stable binary LSD radix sort; sign bit flipped in the last pass for signed data

The controller copies the input array into internal storage, runs the sorting FSM, and drives the sorted array on data_out. Use the start/busy/done handshake.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus radix_sort_controller and radix_sort_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, SIGNED
  • Testbench: Covered by the shared tb/tb_sorting.v testbench (see Selection Sort).

مرتب‌سازی رادیکس دودویی LSD پایدار؛ در پاس آخر بیت علامت برای دادهٔ علامت‌دار برعکس می‌شود.

کنترلر آرایهٔ ورودی را در حافظهٔ داخلی کپی می‌کند، FSM مرتب‌سازی را اجرا می‌کند و آرایهٔ مرتب را روی data_out می‌گذارد. از handshake start/busy/done استفاده کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه radix_sort_controller و radix_sort_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED
  • تست‌بنچ: توسط تست‌بنچ مشترک tb/tb_sorting.v پوشش داده می‌شود (نمونه: Selection Sort).

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: data_in"] --> M["radix_sort"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: data_out"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح
W8Data widthعرض داده
N8Number of elements / sizeتعداد عناصر / اندازه
SIGNED01 = signed two's-complement۱ = علامت‌دار مکمل دو

Portsپورت‌ها

DirجهتNameنامWidthعرضDescriptionتوضیح
inputclk1Clockکلاک
inputrst_n1Reset, active lowریست فعال پایین
inputstart1Start pulseپالس شروع
inputdata_inN*WInput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ ورودی
outputdata_outN*WOutput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ خروجی
outputbusy1Operation in progressدر حال اجرا
outputdone1One-cycle completion pulseپایان (یک کلاک)

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/radix_sort.v
// Stable binary LSD radix sort. Each pass scatters zeros then ones.
// No exponential histogram; signed ordering flips the sign bit in the last pass.

// =====================================================================
// radix_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
// control[1]: bit_index <= 0;
// control[2]: p <= 0;
// control[3]: out_index <= 0;
// control[4]: temp[out_index] <= mem[p];
// control[5]: out_index <= out_index + 1;
// control[6]: p <= 0;
// control[7]: p <= p + 1;
// control[8]: temp[out_index] <= mem[p];
// control[9]: out_index <= out_index + 1;
// control[10]: p <= 0;
// control[11]: p <= p + 1;
// control[12]: mem[p] <= temp[p];
// control[13]: p <= 0;
// control[14]: out_index <= 0;
// control[15]: bit_index <= bit_index + 1;
// control[16]: p <= p + 1;
// control[17]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module radix_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [3:0] status,
  output reg [17:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);

reg [2:0] state;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg [2:0] next_state;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  state <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  state <= next_state;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_state = state;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_state = 0;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) case(state)
      0: begin
        if(status[0]) begin
          control[4] = 1'b1;
          control[5] = 1'b1;
        end
        if(status[1]) begin
          control[6] = 1'b1;
          next_state = 1;
        end else control[7] = 1'b1;
      end
      1: begin
        if(status[2]) begin
          control[8] = 1'b1;
          control[9] = 1'b1;
        end
        if(status[1]) begin
          control[10] = 1'b1;
          next_state = 2;
        end else control[11] = 1'b1;
      end
      2: begin
        control[12] = 1'b1;
        if(status[1]) begin
          control[13] = 1'b1;
          control[14] = 1'b1;
          if(status[3]) next_state = 3;
          else begin
            control[15] = 1'b1;
            next_state = 0;
          end
        end else control[16] = 1'b1;
      end
      3: begin
        control[17] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
      default: next_state = 0;
    endcase
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module radix_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [17:0] control,
  output [3:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [W-1:0] mem [0:N-1];
reg [W-1:0] temp [0:N-1];
integer bit_index;
integer p;
integer out_index;
integer k;
wire digit;
assign digit = mem[p][bit_index] ^ (SIGNED && (bit_index == W - 1));

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (!digit);
assign status[1] = (p == N - 1);
assign status[2] = digit;
assign status[3] = (bit_index == W - 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  bit_index <= 0;
  p <= 0;
  out_index <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) begin
    mem[k] <= 0;
    temp[k] <= 0;
  end
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    bit_index <= 0;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    out_index <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    temp[out_index] <= mem[p];
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    out_index <= out_index + 1;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    temp[out_index] <= mem[p];
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    out_index <= out_index + 1;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    mem[p] <= temp[p];
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[14]) begin
    out_index <= 0;
  end
  if(control[15]) begin
    bit_index <= bit_index + 1;
  end
  if(control[16]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
  if(control[17]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module radix_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [17:0] control;
wire [3:0] status;
radix_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
radix_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Counting Sortمرتب‌سازی شمارشی counting_sort

Counting sort for small keys; histogram grows as 2**Wمرتب‌سازی شمارشی برای کلیدهای کوچک؛ هیستوگرام 2**W.

rtl/counting_sort.v

Explanationتوضیح

Counting sort for small keys; histogram grows as 2**W

The controller copies the input array into internal storage, runs the sorting FSM, and drives the sorted array on data_out. Use the start/busy/done handshake.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus counting_sort_controller and counting_sort_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, SIGNED
  • Testbench: Covered by the shared tb/tb_sorting.v testbench (see Selection Sort).

مرتب‌سازی شمارشی برای کلیدهای کوچک؛ هیستوگرام 2**W.

کنترلر آرایهٔ ورودی را در حافظهٔ داخلی کپی می‌کند، FSM مرتب‌سازی را اجرا می‌کند و آرایهٔ مرتب را روی data_out می‌گذارد. از handshake start/busy/done استفاده کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه counting_sort_controller و counting_sort_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED
  • تست‌بنچ: توسط تست‌بنچ مشترک tb/tb_sorting.v پوشش داده می‌شود (نمونه: Selection Sort).

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/counting_sort.v
// Counting sort for SMALL integer keys (1 <= W <= 10 recommended).
// Histogram storage grows as 2**W. Signed order uses sign-bit biased keys.

// =====================================================================
// counting_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: data <= data_in;
// control[1]: p <= 0;
// control[2]: key <= 0;
// control[3]: out_index <= 0;
// control[4]: histogram[key] <= 0;
// control[5]: key <= 0;
// control[6]: key <= key + 1;
// control[7]: histogram[current] <= histogram[current] + 1;
// control[8]: p <= p + 1;
// control[9]: data_out[(out_index * W) +: W] <= key ^ bias;
// control[10]: histogram[key] <= histogram[key] - 1;
// control[11]: out_index <= out_index + 1;
// control[12]: key <= key + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module counting_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 4,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [3:0] status,
  output reg [12:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);
localparam RANGE = 1 << W;
reg [1:0] state;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg [1:0] next_state;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  state <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  state <= next_state;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_state = state;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
      next_state = 0;
    end else if(busy) case(state)
      0: begin
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        if(status[0]) begin
          control[5] = 1'b1;
          next_state = 1;
        end else control[6] = 1'b1;
      end
      1: begin
        control[7] = 1'b1;
        if(status[1]) next_state = 2;
        else control[8] = 1'b1;
      end
      2: if(status[2]) begin
        control[9] = 1'b1;
        control[10] = 1'b1;
        control[11] = 1'b1;
        if(status[3]) next_state = 3;
      end else control[12] = 1'b1;
      3: begin
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
    endcase
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module counting_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 4,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [12:0] control,
  output [3:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
localparam RANGE = 1 << W;
reg [N*W-1:0] data;
reg signed [31:0] histogram [0:RANGE-1];
integer p;
integer key;
integer out_index;
integer k;
wire [W-1:0] bias;
assign bias = (SIGNED)? 1 << W - 1 : 0;
wire [W-1:0] current;
assign current = data[(p * W) +: W] ^ bias;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (key == RANGE - 1);
assign status[1] = (p == N - 1);
assign status[2] = (histogram[key] > 0);
assign status[3] = (out_index == N - 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  data <= 0;
  p <= 0;
  key <= 0;
  out_index <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    data <= data_in;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    key <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    out_index <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    histogram[key] <= 0;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    key <= 0;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    key <= key + 1;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    histogram[current] <= histogram[current] + 1;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    data_out[(out_index * W) +: W] <= key ^ bias;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    histogram[key] <= histogram[key] - 1;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    out_index <= out_index + 1;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    key <= key + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module counting_sort #
(
  parameter W = 4,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [12:0] control;
wire [3:0] status;
counting_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
counting_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Explanationتوضیح

DirجهتNameنامWidthعرضDescriptionتوضیح

inputrst_n1Reset, active lowریست فعال پایین
inputstart1Start pulseپالس شروع
inputdata_inN*WInput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ ورودی
outputdata_outN*WOutput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ خروجی
outputbusy1Operation in progressدر حال اجرا
outputdone1One-cycle completion pulseپایان (یک کلاک)

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/bucket_sort.v
// Integer bucket sort: high key bits select a bucket; each bucket is insertion
// sorted, then concatenated. 1 <= BITS <= W; storage is N * 2**BITS words.

// =====================================================================
// bucket_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: data <= data_in;
// control[1]: p <= 0;
// control[2]: bucket <= 0;
// control[3]: out_index <= 0;
// control[4]: for(k=0; k<B; k=k+1) counts[k] <= 0;
// control[5]: buckets[destination * N + counts[destination]] <= data[(p * W) +: W];
// control[6]: counts[destination] <= counts[destination] + 1;
// control[7]: i <= 1;
// control[8]: p <= p + 1;
// control[9]: p <= 0;
// control[10]: key <= buckets[bucket * N + i];
// control[11]: j <= i - 1;
// control[12]: buckets[bucket * N + j + 1] <= buckets[bucket * N + j];
// control[13]: j <= j - 1;
// control[14]: buckets[bucket * N + j + 1] <= key;
// control[15]: i <= i + 1;
// control[16]: data_out[(out_index * W) +: W] <= buckets[bucket * N + p];
// control[17]: out_index <= out_index + 1;
// control[18]: p <= p + 1;
// control[19]: bucket <= bucket + 1;
// control[20]: i <= 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module bucket_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter BITS = (W > 1)? 2 : 1
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [5:0] status,
  output reg [20:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);
localparam B = 1 << BITS;
reg [2:0] state;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg [2:0] next_state;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  state <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  state <= next_state;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_state = state;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_state = 0;
      next_busy = 1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
    end else if(busy) case(state)
      0: begin
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        control[6] = 1'b1;
        if(status[0]) begin
          control[7] = 1'b1;
          next_state = 1;
        end else control[8] = 1'b1;
      end
      1: if(status[1]) begin
        control[9] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 4;
      end else begin
        control[10] = 1'b1;
        control[11] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 2;
      end
      2: if(status[2] && status[3]) begin
        control[12] = 1'b1;
        control[13] = 1'b1;
      end else begin
        control[14] = 1'b1;
        control[15] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 1;
      end
      4: if(status[4]) begin
        control[16] = 1'b1;
        control[17] = 1'b1;
        control[18] = 1'b1;
      end else if(status[5]) next_state = 5;
      else begin
        control[19] = 1'b1;
        control[20] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 1;
      end
      5: begin
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
      default: next_state = 0;
    endcase
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module bucket_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter BITS = (W > 1)? 2 : 1
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [20:0] control,
  output [5:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
localparam B = 1 << BITS;
reg [W-1:0] buckets [0:B*N-1];
reg [W-1:0] key;
reg signed [31:0] counts [0:B-1];
reg [N*W-1:0] data;
integer bucket;
integer p;
integer i;
integer j;
integer out_index;
integer k;
wire [W-1:0] biased;
assign biased = data[(p * W) +: W] ^ ((SIGNED)? 1 << W - 1 : 0);
wire [BITS-1:0] destination;
assign destination = biased[(W - 1) -: BITS];
wire greater;
assign greater = (SIGNED)? $signed(buckets[bucket * N + ((j >= 0)? j : 0)]) > $signed(key) : buckets[bucket * N + ((j >= 0)? j : 0)] > key;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (p == N - 1);
assign status[1] = (i >= counts[bucket]);
assign status[2] = (j >= 0);
assign status[3] = greater;
assign status[4] = (p < counts[bucket]);
assign status[5] = (bucket == B - 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  data <= 0;
  key <= 0;
  bucket <= 0;
  p <= 0;
  i <= 0;
  j <= 0;
  out_index <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<B; k=k+1) counts[k] <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<B*N; k=k+1) buckets[k] <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    data <= data_in;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    bucket <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    out_index <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    for(k=0; k<B; k=k+1) counts[k] <= 0;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    buckets[destination * N + counts[destination]] <= data[(p * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    counts[destination] <= counts[destination] + 1;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    i <= 1;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    key <= buckets[bucket * N + i];
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    j <= i - 1;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    buckets[bucket * N + j + 1] <= buckets[bucket * N + j];
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    j <= j - 1;
  end
  if(control[14]) begin
    buckets[bucket * N + j + 1] <= key;
  end
  if(control[15]) begin
    i <= i + 1;
  end
  if(control[16]) begin
    data_out[(out_index * W) +: W] <= buckets[bucket * N + p];
  end
  if(control[17]) begin
    out_index <= out_index + 1;
  end
  if(control[18]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
  if(control[19]) begin
    bucket <= bucket + 1;
  end
  if(control[20]) begin
    i <= 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module bucket_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter BITS = (W > 1)? 2 : 1
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [20:0] control;
wire [5:0] status;
bucket_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .BITS(BITS)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
bucket_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .BITS(BITS)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Sortingمرتب‌سازی

Bitonic Sortمرتب‌سازی بیتونیک bitonic_sort

Iterative bitonic comparator network; N must be a power of twoشبکهٔ مقایسه‌گر بیتونیک تکراری؛ N باید توان دو باشد.

rtl/bitonic_sort.v

Explanationتوضیح

Iterative bitonic comparator network; N must be a power of two

The controller copies the input array into internal storage, runs the sorting FSM, and drives the sorted array on data_out. Use the start/busy/done handshake.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus bitonic_sort_controller and bitonic_sort_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, SIGNED
  • Testbench: Covered by the shared tb/tb_sorting.v testbench (see Selection Sort).

شبکهٔ مقایسه‌گر بیتونیک تکراری؛ N باید توان دو باشد.

کنترلر آرایهٔ ورودی را در حافظهٔ داخلی کپی می‌کند، FSM مرتب‌سازی را اجرا می‌کند و آرایهٔ مرتب را روی data_out می‌گذارد. از handshake start/busy/done استفاده کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه bitonic_sort_controller و bitonic_sort_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED
  • تست‌بنچ: توسط تست‌بنچ مشترک tb/tb_sorting.v پوشش داده می‌شود (نمونه: Selection Sort).

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: data_in"] --> M["bitonic_sort"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: data_out"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/bitonic_sort.v
// Iterative bitonic comparator network schedule. N MUST be a power of two.
// One pair is visited per cycle; ascending output, signed/unsigned keys.

// =====================================================================
// bitonic_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
// control[1]: span <= 2;
// control[2]: distance <= 1;
// control[3]: p <= 0;
// control[4]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
// control[5]: mem[p] <= mem[peer];
// control[6]: mem[peer] <= mem[p];
// control[7]: p <= 0;
// control[8]: distance <= distance / 2;
// control[9]: span <= span * 2;
// control[10]: distance <= span;
// control[11]: p <= p + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module bitonic_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [8:0] status,
  output reg [11:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);

reg finish;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg next_finish;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  finish <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  finish <= next_finish;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_finish = finish;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_finish = N == 1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(finish) begin
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else begin
        if(status[0] && status[1]) begin
          if(status[2] && status[3] || status[4] && status[5]) begin
            control[5] = 1'b1;
            control[6] = 1'b1;
          end
        end
        if(status[6]) begin
          control[7] = 1'b1;
          if(status[7]) control[8] = 1'b1;
          else if(status[8]) next_finish = 1;
          else begin
            control[9] = 1'b1;
            control[10] = 1'b1;
          end
        end else control[11] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module bitonic_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [11:0] control,
  output [8:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [W-1:0] mem [0:N-1];
integer span;
integer distance;
integer p;
integer peer;
integer k;
wire greater;
assign greater = (SIGNED)? $signed(mem[p]) > $signed(mem[peer]) : mem[p] > mem[peer];
wire less;
assign less = (SIGNED)? $signed(mem[p]) < $signed(mem[peer]) : mem[p] < mem[peer];

always @(*) peer = p ^ distance;


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (peer > p);
assign status[1] = (peer < N);
assign status[2] = ((p & span) == 0);
assign status[3] = greater;
assign status[4] = ((p & span) != 0);
assign status[5] = less;
assign status[6] = (p == N - 1);
assign status[7] = (distance > 1);
assign status[8] = (span == N);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  span <= 2;
  distance <= 1;
  p <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    span <= 2;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    distance <= 1;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    mem[p] <= mem[peer];
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    mem[peer] <= mem[p];
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    distance <= distance / 2;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    span <= span * 2;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    distance <= span;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module bitonic_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [11:0] control;
wire [8:0] status;
bitonic_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
bitonic_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Rank Sortمرتب‌سازی رتبه‌ای rank_sort

Stable enumeration/rank sort; counts smaller keys plus earlier equal keysمرتب‌سازی رتبه‌ای/شمارشی پایدار؛ تعداد کلیدهای کوچک‌تر و مساوی‌های قبلی.

Explanationتوضیح

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus rank_sort_controller and rank_sort_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, SIGNED
  • Testbench: Covered by the shared tb/tb_sorting.v testbench (see Selection Sort).

مرتب‌سازی رتبه‌ای/شمارشی پایدار؛ تعداد کلیدهای کوچک‌تر و مساوی‌های قبلی.

کنترلر آرایهٔ ورودی را در حافظهٔ داخلی کپی می‌کند، FSM مرتب‌سازی را اجرا می‌کند و آرایهٔ مرتب را روی data_out می‌گذارد. از handshake start/busy/done استفاده کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه rank_sort_controller و rank_sort_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED
  • تست‌بنچ: توسط تست‌بنچ مشترک tb/tb_sorting.v پوشش داده می‌شود (نمونه: Selection Sort).
