RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/rank_sort.v
// Stable enumeration/rank sort: count smaller keys plus earlier equal keys.
// N comparisons per item, then place it at its unique rank.

// =====================================================================
// rank_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: data <= data_in;
// control[1]: i <= 0;
// control[2]: j <= 0;
// control[3]: rank_value <= 0;
// control[4]: data_out[(rank_value * W) +: W] <= b;
// control[5]: i <= i + 1;
// control[6]: j <= 0;
// control[7]: rank_value <= 0;
// control[8]: rank_value <= rank_value + 1;
// control[9]: j <= j + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module rank_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [4:0] status,
  output reg [9:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);

reg placing;
reg finish;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg next_finish;
reg next_placing;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  placing <= 0;
  finish <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  finish <= next_finish;
  placing <= next_placing;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_finish = finish;
  next_placing = placing;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_placing = 0;
      next_finish = 0;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(finish) begin
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else if(placing) begin
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        if(status[0]) next_finish = 1;
        else begin
          control[5] = 1'b1;
          control[6] = 1'b1;
          control[7] = 1'b1;
          next_placing = 0;
        end
      end else begin
        if(status[1] || status[2] && status[3]) control[8] = 1'b1;
        if(status[4]) next_placing = 1;
        else control[9] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module rank_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [9:0] control,
  output [4:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [N*W-1:0] data;
integer i;
integer j;
integer rank_value;
integer k;
wire [W-1:0] a;
assign a = data[(j * W) +: W];
wire [W-1:0] b;
assign b = data[(i * W) +: W];
wire smaller;
assign smaller = (SIGNED)? $signed(a) < $signed(b) : a < b;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (i == N - 1);
assign status[1] = smaller;
assign status[2] = (a == b);
assign status[3] = (j < i);
assign status[4] = (j == N - 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  data <= 0;
  i <= 0;
  j <= 0;
  rank_value <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    data <= data_in;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    i <= 0;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    j <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    rank_value <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    data_out[(rank_value * W) +: W] <= b;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    i <= i + 1;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    j <= 0;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    rank_value <= 0;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    rank_value <= rank_value + 1;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    j <= j + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module rank_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [9:0] control;
wire [4:0] status;
rank_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
rank_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Testbenchتست‌بنچ

No dedicated testbench is committed for this module.تست‌بنچ اختصاصی برای این ماژول در مخزن موجود نیست.

Sortingمرتب‌سازی

Intro Sortمرتب‌سازی درون‌نگر intro_sort

Introspective quicksort with depth budget and heapsort fallbackکوییک‌سورت درون‌نگر با بودجهٔ عمق و بازگشت به heapsort.

rtl/intro_sort.v

Explanationتوضیح

Introspective quicksort with depth budget and heapsort fallback

The controller copies the input array into internal storage, runs the sorting FSM, and drives the sorted array on data_out. Use the start/busy/done handshake.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus intro_sort_controller and intro_sort_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, SIGNED, DEPTH
  • Testbench: Covered by the shared tb/tb_sorting.v testbench (see Selection Sort).

کوییک‌سورت درون‌نگر با بودجهٔ عمق و بازگشت به heapsort.

کنترلر آرایهٔ ورودی را در حافظهٔ داخلی کپی می‌کند، FSM مرتب‌سازی را اجرا می‌کند و آرایهٔ مرتب را روی data_out می‌گذارد. از handshake start/busy/done استفاده کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه intro_sort_controller و intro_sort_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED, DEPTH
  • تست‌بنچ: توسط تست‌بنچ مشترک tb/tb_sorting.v پوشش داده می‌شود (نمونه: Selection Sort).

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: data_in"] --> M["intro_sort"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: data_out"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح

N8Number of elements / sizeتعداد عناصر / اندازه
SIGNED01 = signed two's-complement۱ = علامت‌دار مکمل دو
DEPTH2 * $clog2(N)Storage depthعمق حافظه

Portsپورت‌ها

DirجهتNameنامWidthعرضDescriptionتوضیح

inputrst_n1Reset, active lowریست فعال پایین

outputdata_outN*WOutput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ خروجی
outputbusy1Operation in progressدر حال اجرا
outputdone1One-cycle completion pulseپایان (یک کلاک)

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/intro_sort.v
// Introspective quicksort: Lomuto partitioning with a per-partition depth budget;
// switches to in-place heapsort when the budget is exhausted. No recursion.
// DEPTH=0 directly exercises the heap fallback. N-entry explicit range stack.

// =====================================================================
// intro_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
// control[1]: slo[0] <= 0;
// control[2]: shi[0] <= N - 1;
// control[3]: sd[0] <= DEPTH;
// control[4]: sp <= 1;
// control[5]: lo <= slo[sp - 1];
// control[6]: hi <= shi[sp - 1];
// control[7]: depth <= sd[sp - 1];
// control[8]: sp <= sp - 1;
// control[9]: pivot <= mem[hi];
// control[10]: i <= lo;
// control[11]: j <= lo;
// control[12]: mem[i] <= mem[j];
// control[13]: mem[j] <= mem[i];
// control[14]: i <= i + 1;
// control[15]: j <= j + 1;
// control[16]: mem[i] <= mem[hi];
// control[17]: mem[hi] <= mem[i];
// control[18]: slo[sp] <= lo;
// control[19]: shi[sp] <= i - 1;
// control[20]: sd[sp] <= depth - 1;
// control[21]: slo[sp + 1] <= i + 1;
// control[22]: shi[sp + 1] <= hi;
// control[23]: sd[sp + 1] <= depth - 1;
// control[24]: sp <= sp + 2;
// control[25]: slo[sp] <= lo;
// control[26]: shi[sp] <= i - 1;
// control[27]: sd[sp] <= depth - 1;
// control[28]: sp <= sp + 1;
// control[29]: slo[sp] <= i + 1;
// control[30]: shi[sp] <= hi;
// control[31]: sd[sp] <= depth - 1;
// control[32]: sp <= sp + 1;
// control[33]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
// control[34]: size <= hi - lo + 1;
// control[35]: root <= (hi - lo + 1) / 2 - 1;
// control[36]: build_root <= (hi - lo + 1) / 2 - 1;
// control[37]: mem[lo + root] <= mem[lo + child];
// control[38]: mem[lo + child] <= mem[lo + root];
// control[39]: root <= child;
// control[40]: root <= build_root - 1;
// control[41]: build_root <= build_root - 1;
// control[42]: mem[lo] <= mem[lo + size - 1];
// control[43]: mem[lo + size - 1] <= mem[lo];
// control[44]: size <= size - 1;
// control[45]: root <= 0;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module intro_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter DEPTH = 2 * $clog2(N)
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [10:0] status,
  output reg [45:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);

reg building;
reg [3:0] state;
reg next_building;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg [3:0] next_state;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  building <= 0;
  state <= 0;
end else begin
  building <= next_building;
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  state <= next_state;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_building = building;
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_state = state;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
      next_state = 0;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) case(state)
      0: if(status[0]) next_state = 4;
      else begin
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        control[6] = 1'b1;
        control[7] = 1'b1;
        control[8] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 1;
      end
      1: if(status[1]) next_state = 0;
      else if(status[2]) next_state = 5;
      else begin
        control[9] = 1'b1;
        control[10] = 1'b1;
        control[11] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 2;
      end
      2: if(status[3]) next_state = 3;
      else begin
        if(status[4]) begin
          control[12] = 1'b1;
          control[13] = 1'b1;
          control[14] = 1'b1;
        end
        control[15] = 1'b1;
      end
      3: begin
        control[16] = 1'b1;
        control[17] = 1'b1;
        if(status[5] && status[6]) begin
          control[18] = 1'b1;
          control[19] = 1'b1;
          control[20] = 1'b1;
          control[21] = 1'b1;
          control[22] = 1'b1;
          control[23] = 1'b1;
          control[24] = 1'b1;
        end else if(status[5]) begin
          control[25] = 1'b1;
          control[26] = 1'b1;
          control[27] = 1'b1;
          control[28] = 1'b1;
        end else if(status[6]) begin
          control[29] = 1'b1;
          control[30] = 1'b1;
          control[31] = 1'b1;
          control[32] = 1'b1;
        end
        next_state = 0;
      end
      4: begin
        control[33] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
      5: begin
        control[34] = 1'b1;
        control[35] = 1'b1;
        control[36] = 1'b1;
        next_building = 1;
        next_state = 6;
      end
      6: if(status[7] && status[8]) begin
        control[37] = 1'b1;
        control[38] = 1'b1;
        control[39] = 1'b1;
      end else next_state = 7;
      7: if(building && status[9]) begin
        control[40] = 1'b1;
        control[41] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 6;
      end else begin
        next_building = 0;
        next_state = 8;
      end
      8: if(status[10]) next_state = 0;
      else begin
        control[42] = 1'b1;
        control[43] = 1'b1;
        control[44] = 1'b1;
        control[45] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 6;
      end
      default: next_state = 0;
    endcase
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module intro_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter DEPTH = 2 * $clog2(N)
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [45:0] control,
  output [10:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [W-1:0] mem [0:N-1];
reg [W-1:0] pivot;
reg signed [31:0] slo [0:N-1];
reg signed [31:0] shi [0:N-1];
reg signed [31:0] sd [0:N-1];
integer sp;
integer lo;
integer hi;
integer depth;
integer i;
integer j;
integer k;
integer size;
integer root;
integer build_root;
integer child;

function [0:0] greater;
  input [W-1:0] a;input [W-1:0] b;
  begin
    greater = (SIGNED)? $signed(a) > $signed(b) : a > b;
  end
endfunction


always @(*) begin
  child = 2 * root + 1;
  if(child + 1 < size) if(greater(mem[lo + child + 1], mem[lo + child])) child = child + 1;
end


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (sp == 0);
assign status[1] = (lo >= hi);
assign status[2] = (depth == 0);
assign status[3] = (j == hi);
assign status[4] = (!greater(mem[j], pivot));
assign status[5] = (lo < i - 1);
assign status[6] = (i + 1 < hi);
assign status[7] = (child < size);
assign status[8] = greater(mem[lo + child], mem[lo + root]);
assign status[9] = (build_root > 0);
assign status[10] = (size <= 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  pivot <= 0;
  sp <= 0;
  lo <= 0;
  hi <= 0;
  depth <= 0;
  i <= 0;
  j <= 0;
  size <= 0;
  root <= 0;
  build_root <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) begin
    mem[k] <= 0;
    slo[k] <= 0;
    shi[k] <= 0;
    sd[k] <= 0;
  end
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    slo[0] <= 0;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    shi[0] <= N - 1;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    sd[0] <= DEPTH;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    sp <= 1;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    lo <= slo[sp - 1];
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    hi <= shi[sp - 1];
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    depth <= sd[sp - 1];
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    sp <= sp - 1;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    pivot <= mem[hi];
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    i <= lo;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    j <= lo;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    mem[i] <= mem[j];
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    mem[j] <= mem[i];
  end
  if(control[14]) begin
    i <= i + 1;
  end
  if(control[15]) begin
    j <= j + 1;
  end
  if(control[16]) begin
    mem[i] <= mem[hi];
  end
  if(control[17]) begin
    mem[hi] <= mem[i];
  end
  if(control[18]) begin
    slo[sp] <= lo;
  end
  if(control[19]) begin
    shi[sp] <= i - 1;
  end
  if(control[20]) begin
    sd[sp] <= depth - 1;
  end
  if(control[21]) begin
    slo[sp + 1] <= i + 1;
  end
  if(control[22]) begin
    shi[sp + 1] <= hi;
  end
  if(control[23]) begin
    sd[sp + 1] <= depth - 1;
  end
  if(control[24]) begin
    sp <= sp + 2;
  end
  if(control[25]) begin
    slo[sp] <= lo;
  end
  if(control[26]) begin
    shi[sp] <= i - 1;
  end
  if(control[27]) begin
    sd[sp] <= depth - 1;
  end
  if(control[28]) begin
    sp <= sp + 1;
  end
  if(control[29]) begin
    slo[sp] <= i + 1;
  end
  if(control[30]) begin
    shi[sp] <= hi;
  end
  if(control[31]) begin
    sd[sp] <= depth - 1;
  end
  if(control[32]) begin
    sp <= sp + 1;
  end
  if(control[33]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
  end
  if(control[34]) begin
    size <= hi - lo + 1;
  end
  if(control[35]) begin
    root <= (hi - lo + 1) / 2 - 1;
  end
  if(control[36]) begin
    build_root <= (hi - lo + 1) / 2 - 1;
  end
  if(control[37]) begin
    mem[lo + root] <= mem[lo + child];
  end
  if(control[38]) begin
    mem[lo + child] <= mem[lo + root];
  end
  if(control[39]) begin
    root <= child;
  end
  if(control[40]) begin
    root <= build_root - 1;
  end
  if(control[41]) begin
    build_root <= build_root - 1;
  end
  if(control[42]) begin
    mem[lo] <= mem[lo + size - 1];
  end
  if(control[43]) begin
    mem[lo + size - 1] <= mem[lo];
  end
  if(control[44]) begin
    size <= size - 1;
  end
  if(control[45]) begin
    root <= 0;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module intro_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter DEPTH = 2 * $clog2(N)
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [45:0] control;
wire [10:0] status;
intro_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .DEPTH(DEPTH)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
intro_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .DEPTH(DEPTH)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Testbenchتست‌بنچ

No dedicated testbench is committed for this module.تست‌بنچ اختصاصی برای این ماژول در مخزن موجود نیست.

Sortingمرتب‌سازی

Tim Sortمرتب‌سازی تیم tim_sort

Educational fixed-run TimSort-style insertion + bottom-up mergeتیم‌سورت آموزشی با runهای ثابت: insertion + ادغام پایین‌به‌بالا.

rtl/tim_sort.v

Explanationتوضیح

Educational fixed-run TimSort-style insertion + bottom-up merge

The controller copies the input array into internal storage, runs the sorting FSM, and drives the sorted array on data_out. Use the start/busy/done handshake.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus tim_sort_controller and tim_sort_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, SIGNED, RUN
  • Testbench: Covered by the shared tb/tb_sorting.v testbench (see Selection Sort).

تیم‌سورت آموزشی با runهای ثابت: insertion + ادغام پایین‌به‌بالا.

کنترلر آرایهٔ ورودی را در حافظهٔ داخلی کپی می‌کند، FSM مرتب‌سازی را اجرا می‌کند و آرایهٔ مرتب را روی data_out می‌گذارد. از handshake start/busy/done استفاده کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه tim_sort_controller و tim_sort_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED, RUN
  • تست‌بنچ: توسط تست‌بنچ مشترک tb/tb_sorting.v پوشش داده می‌شود (نمونه: Selection Sort).

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: data_in"] --> M["tim_sort"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: data_out"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح
W8Data widthعرض داده
N8Number of elements / sizeتعداد عناصر / اندازه
SIGNED01 = signed two's-complement۱ = علامت‌دار مکمل دو
RUN4Run lengthطول run

Portsپورت‌ها

DirجهتNameنامWidthعرضDescriptionتوضیح
inputclk1Clockکلاک
inputrst_n1Reset, active lowریست فعال پایین

inputdata_inN*WInput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ ورودی
outputdata_outN*WOutput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ خروجی
outputbusy1Operation in progressدر حال اجرا
outputdone1One-cycle completion pulseپایان (یک کلاک)

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/tim_sort.v
// Educational fixed-run TimSort-style hybrid: insertion-sort RUN-sized chunks,
// then stably merge them bottom-up. This is NOT CPython's adaptive natural-run
// stack/galloping implementation. RUN>=1; one shift/merge/copy per cycle.

// =====================================================================
// tim_sort: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
// control[1]: run_start <= 0;
// control[2]: run_end <= (RUN < N)? RUN : N;
// control[3]: i <= 1;
// control[4]: width <= RUN;
// control[5]: left <= 0;
// control[6]: run_start <= run_end;
// control[7]: run_end <= (run_end + RUN < N)? run_end + RUN : N;
// control[8]: i <= run_end + 1;
// control[9]: key <= mem[i];
// control[10]: j <= i - 1;
// control[11]: mem[j + 1] <= mem[j];
// control[12]: j <= j - 1;
// control[13]: mem[j + 1] <= key;
// control[14]: i <= i + 1;
// control[15]: middle <= (left + width < N)? left + width : N;
// control[16]: limit <= (left + 2 * width < N)? left + 2 * width : N;
// control[17]: i <= left;
// control[18]: j <= (left + width < N)? left + width : N;
// control[19]: p <= left;
// control[20]: p <= left;
// control[21]: temp[p] <= mem[i];
// control[22]: i <= i + 1;
// control[23]: temp[p] <= mem[j];
// control[24]: j <= j + 1;
// control[25]: p <= p + 1;
// control[26]: mem[p] <= temp[p];
// control[27]: p <= p + 1;
// control[28]: left <= limit;
// control[29]: width <= width * 2;
// control[30]: left <= 0;
// control[31]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module tim_sort_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter RUN = 4
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [10:0] status,
  output reg [31:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);

reg [3:0] state;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg [3:0] next_state;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  state <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  state <= next_state;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_state = state;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
      control[5] = 1'b1;
      next_state = 0;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) case(state)
      0: if(status[0]) begin
        if(status[1]) begin
          if(RUN >= N) next_state = 7;
          else next_state = 3;
        end else begin
          control[6] = 1'b1;
          control[7] = 1'b1;
          control[8] = 1'b1;
        end
      end else begin
        control[9] = 1'b1;
        control[10] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 1;
      end
      1: if(status[2] && status[3]) begin
        control[11] = 1'b1;
        control[12] = 1'b1;
      end else begin
        control[13] = 1'b1;
        control[14] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 0;
      end
      3: begin
        control[15] = 1'b1;
        control[16] = 1'b1;
        control[17] = 1'b1;
        control[18] = 1'b1;
        control[19] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 4;
      end
      4: if(status[4]) begin
        control[20] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 5;
      end else begin
        if(status[5] && (status[6] || status[7])) begin
          control[21] = 1'b1;
          control[22] = 1'b1;
        end else begin
          control[23] = 1'b1;
          control[24] = 1'b1;
        end
        control[25] = 1'b1;
      end
      5: begin
        control[26] = 1'b1;
        if(status[8]) next_state = 6;
        else control[27] = 1'b1;
      end
      6: if(status[9]) begin
        control[28] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 3;
      end else if(status[10]) next_state = 7;
      else begin
        control[29] = 1'b1;
        control[30] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 3;
      end
      7: begin
        control[31] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
      default: next_state = 0;
    endcase
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module tim_sort_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter RUN = 4
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [31:0] control,
  output [10:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [W-1:0] mem [0:N-1];
reg [W-1:0] temp [0:N-1];
reg [W-1:0] key;
integer run_start;
integer run_end;
integer i;
integer j;
integer width;
integer left;
integer middle;
integer limit;
integer p;
integer k;

function [0:0] greater;
  input [W-1:0] a;input [W-1:0] b;
  begin
    greater = (SIGNED)? $signed(a) > $signed(b) : a > b;
  end
endfunction


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (i >= run_end);
assign status[1] = (run_end == N);
assign status[2] = (j >= run_start);
assign status[3] = greater(mem[(j >= 0)? j : 0], key);
assign status[4] = (p == limit);
assign status[5] = (i < middle);
assign status[6] = (j >= limit);
assign status[7] = (!greater(mem[(i < N)? i : 0], mem[(j < N)? j : 0]));
assign status[8] = (p == limit - 1);
assign status[9] = (limit < N);
assign status[10] = (2 * width >= N);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data_out <= 0;
  key <= 0;
  run_start <= 0;
  run_end <= 0;
  i <= 0;
  j <= 0;
  width <= RUN;
  left <= 0;
  middle <= 0;
  limit <= 0;
  p <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) begin
    mem[k] <= 0;
    temp[k] <= 0;
  end
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) mem[k] <= data_in[(k * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    run_start <= 0;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    run_end <= (RUN < N)? RUN : N;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    i <= 1;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    width <= RUN;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    left <= 0;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    run_start <= run_end;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    run_end <= (run_end + RUN < N)? run_end + RUN : N;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    i <= run_end + 1;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    key <= mem[i];
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    j <= i - 1;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    mem[j + 1] <= mem[j];
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    j <= j - 1;
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    mem[j + 1] <= key;
  end
  if(control[14]) begin
    i <= i + 1;
  end
  if(control[15]) begin
    middle <= (left + width < N)? left + width : N;
  end
  if(control[16]) begin
    limit <= (left + 2 * width < N)? left + 2 * width : N;
  end
  if(control[17]) begin
    i <= left;
  end
  if(control[18]) begin
    j <= (left + width < N)? left + width : N;
  end
  if(control[19]) begin
    p <= left;
  end
  if(control[20]) begin
    p <= left;
  end
  if(control[21]) begin
    temp[p] <= mem[i];
  end
  if(control[22]) begin
    i <= i + 1;
  end
  if(control[23]) begin
    temp[p] <= mem[j];
  end
  if(control[24]) begin
    j <= j + 1;
  end
  if(control[25]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
  if(control[26]) begin
    mem[p] <= temp[p];
  end
  if(control[27]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
  if(control[28]) begin
    left <= limit;
  end
  if(control[29]) begin
    width <= width * 2;
  end
  if(control[30]) begin
    left <= 0;
  end
  if(control[31]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) data_out[(k * W) +: W] <= mem[k];
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module tim_sort #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter RUN = 4
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [31:0] control;
wire [10:0] status;
tim_sort_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .RUN(RUN)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
tim_sort_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .RUN(RUN)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Search & Array Algorithmsجستجو و الگوریتم‌های آرایه

Linear Searchجستجوی خطی linear_search

Sequential linear search; first match wins; index=0 on missجستجوی خطی ترتیبی؛ اولین تطابق برنده است؛ در عدم تطابق index=0.

rtl/linear_search.v

Explanationتوضیح

Sequential linear search; first match wins; index=0 on miss

The controller sequences the array operation; results and status flags are valid when done pulses.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus linear_search_controller and linear_search_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, I

جستجوی خطی ترتیبی؛ اولین تطابق برنده است؛ در عدم تطابق index=0.

کنترلر عملیات آرایه را ترتیب می‌دهد؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌های وضعیت وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه linear_search_controller و linear_search_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, I

    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: index"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: found, busy (+1)"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/linear_search.v
// Sequential linear search; first match wins. index=0 when not found.

// =====================================================================
// linear_search: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: data <= data_in;
// control[1]: key <= target;
// control[2]: p <= 0;
// control[3]: found <= 1;
// control[4]: index <= p;
// control[5]: found <= 0;
// control[6]: index <= 0;
// control[7]: p <= p + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module linear_search_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter I = (N > 1)? $clog2(N) : 1
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [1:0] status,
  output reg [7:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(status[0]) begin
        control[3] = 1'b1;
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else if(status[1]) begin
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        control[6] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else control[7] = 1'b1;
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module linear_search_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter I = (N > 1)? $clog2(N) : 1
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [W-1:0] target,
  input [7:0] control,
  output [1:0] status,
  output reg found,
  output reg [I-1:0] index);
reg [N*W-1:0] data;
reg [W-1:0] key;
integer p;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (data[(p * W) +: W] == key);
assign status[1] = (p == N - 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  found <= 0;
  index <= 0;
  data <= 0;
  key <= 0;
  p <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    data <= data_in;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    key <= target;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    found <= 1;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    index <= p;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    found <= 0;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    index <= 0;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module linear_search #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter I = (N > 1)? $clog2(N) : 1
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [W-1:0] target,
  output found,
  output [I-1:0] index,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [7:0] control;
wire [1:0] status;
linear_search_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .I(I)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
linear_search_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .I(I)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .target(target),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .found(found),
    .index(index)
);
endmodule

Binary Searchجستجوی دودویی binary_search

Lower-bound binary search on an ascending sorted array; first match winsجستجوی دودویی lower-bound روی آرایهٔ صعودی؛ اولین تطابق برنده است.

rtl/binary_search.v

Explanationتوضیح

Lower-bound binary search on an ascending sorted array; first match wins

The controller sequences the array operation; results and status flags are valid when done pulses.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus binary_search_controller and binary_search_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, SIGNED, I

جستجوی دودویی lower-bound روی آرایهٔ صعودی؛ اولین تطابق برنده است.

کنترلر عملیات آرایه را ترتیب می‌دهد؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌های وضعیت وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه binary_search_controller و binary_search_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED, I

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: data_in, target"] --> M["binary_search"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: index"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: found, busy (+1)"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح
W8Data widthعرض داده
N8Number of elements / sizeتعداد عناصر / اندازه
SIGNED01 = signed two's-complement۱ = علامت‌دار مکمل دو
I(N > 1)? $clog2(N) : 1Index widthعرض اندیس

Portsپورت‌ها

DirجهتNameنامWidthعرضDescriptionتوضیح
inputclk1Clockکلاک
inputrst_n1Reset, active lowریست فعال پایین
inputstart1Start pulseپالس شروع
inputdata_inN*WInput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ ورودی
inputtargetWSearch targetمقدار هدف
outputfound1Match foundپیدا شد
outputindexIMatch indexاندیس
outputbusy1Operation in progressدر حال اجرا
outputdone1One-cycle completion pulseپایان (یک کلاک)

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/binary_search.v
// Lower-bound binary search on an ASCENDING sorted array; first match wins.

// =====================================================================
// binary_search: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: data <= data_in;
// control[1]: key <= target;
// control[2]: low <= 0;
// control[3]: high <= N;
// control[4]: found <= (low < N) && (current == key);
// control[5]: index <= ((low < N) && (current == key))? low : 0;
// control[6]: low <= middle + 1;
// control[7]: high <= middle;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module binary_search_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter I = (N > 1)? $clog2(N) : 1
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [1:0] status,
  output reg [7:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(status[0]) begin
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else if(status[1]) control[6] = 1'b1;
      else control[7] = 1'b1;
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module binary_search_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter I = (N > 1)? $clog2(N) : 1
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [W-1:0] target,
  input [7:0] control,
  output [1:0] status,
  output reg found,
  output reg [I-1:0] index);
reg [N*W-1:0] data;
reg [W-1:0] key;
integer low;
integer high;
integer middle;
wire [W-1:0] current;
assign current = data[(((middle < N)? middle : 0) * W) +: W];
wire less;
assign less = (SIGNED)? $signed(current) < $signed(key) : current < key;

always @(*) middle = (low + high) / 2;


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (low == high);
assign status[1] = less;

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  found <= 0;
  index <= 0;
  data <= 0;
  key <= 0;
  low <= 0;
  high <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    data <= data_in;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    key <= target;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    low <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    high <= N;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    found <= (low < N) && (current == key);
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    index <= ((low < N) && (current == key))? low : 0;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    low <= middle + 1;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    high <= middle;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module binary_search #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter I = (N > 1)? $clog2(N) : 1
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [W-1:0] target,
  output found,
  output [I-1:0] index,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [7:0] control;
wire [1:0] status;
binary_search_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .I(I)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
binary_search_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .I(I)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .target(target),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .found(found),
    .index(index)
);
endmodule

Array Max / Minبیشینه/کمینهٔ آرایه array_max_min

Controller + datapath + top max/min scanner; legacy async active-high reset and direct data_in scanبیشینه و کمینه را در یک اسکن پیدا می‌کند.

rtl/array_max_min.v

Explanationتوضیح

Controller + datapath + top max/min scanner; legacy async active-high reset and direct data_in scan

The controller sequences the array operation; results and status flags are valid when done pulses.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus array_max_min_controller and array_max_min_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Asynchronous active-high reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: N, WIDTH

بیشینه و کمینه را در یک اسکن پیدا می‌کند.

کنترلر عملیات آرایه را ترتیب می‌دهد؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌های وضعیت وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه array_max_min_controller و array_max_min_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست غیرهمگام فعال بالا
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: N, WIDTH

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: data_in"] --> M["array_max_min"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: max_val, min_val"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: done, busy"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

SCAN compares one element per cycle against the running maximum and minimum. When all elements have been visited, FIN pulses done.

StateMeaningExitNext
IDLEInitialize max and min with element 0startSCAN / FIN
SCANCompare one element per cyclek == NSCAN / FIN
FINAssert done for one cyclealwaysIDLE

SCAN در هر کلاک یک عنصر را با بیشینه و کمینهٔ جاری مقایسه می‌کند. وقتی همهٔ عناصر دیده شدند، FIN پالس done می‌دهد.

حالتمعنیخروجبعدی
IDLEمقداردهی max و min با عنصر ۰startSCAN / FIN
SCANمقایسهٔ یک عنصر در هر کلاکk == NSCAN / FIN
FINفعال‌کردن done برای یک کلاکهمیشهIDLE
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> SCAN: start, multi-element
    IDLE --> FIN: start, N=1
    SCAN --> SCAN: more elements
    SCAN --> FIN: scan complete
    FIN --> IDLE: done

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح
N8Number of elements / sizeتعداد عناصر / اندازه
WIDTH8Data widthعرض داده

Portsپورت‌ها

DirجهتNameنامWidthعرضDescriptionتوضیح
inputclk1Clockکلاک
inputrst1Resetریست
inputstart1Start pulseپالس شروع
inputdata_inN*WIDTHInput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ ورودی
outputmax_valWIDTHMaximum valueبیشینه
outputmin_valWIDTHMinimum valueکمینه
outputdone1One-cycle completion pulseپایان (یک کلاک)
outputbusy1Operation in progressدر حال اجرا

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/array_max_min.v
// =====================================================================
// array_max_min: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Keep data_in stable until done (legacy API).
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: max_val <= data_in[0 +: WIDTH];
// control[1]: min_val <= data_in[0 +: WIDTH];
// control[2]: k <= 1;
// control[3]: max_val <= data_in[(k * WIDTH) +: WIDTH];
// control[4]: min_val <= data_in[(k * WIDTH) +: WIDTH];
// control[5]: k <= k + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module array_max_min_controller #
(
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter WIDTH = 8
)
(
  input wire clk,
  input wire rst,
  input wire start,
  input [2:0] status,
  output reg [5:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);
localparam IDLE = 2'd0;
localparam SCAN = 2'd1;
localparam FIN = 2'd2;
reg [1:0] state;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg [1:0] next_state;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) if(rst) begin
  state <= IDLE;
  done <= 1'b0;
  busy <= 1'b0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  state <= next_state;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_state = state;
  control = 0;
  begin
    case(state)
      IDLE: begin
        next_done = 1'b0;
        next_busy = 1'b0;
        if(start) begin
          control[0] = 1'b1;
          control[1] = 1'b1;
          control[2] = 1'b1;
          if(N <= 1) begin
            next_state = FIN;
          end else begin
            next_busy = 1'b1;
            next_state = SCAN;
          end
        end
      end
      SCAN: begin
        if(status[0]) begin
          if(status[1]) control[3] = 1'b1;
          if(status[2]) control[4] = 1'b1;
          control[5] = 1'b1;
        end else begin
          next_state = FIN;
        end
      end
      FIN: begin
        next_busy = 1'b0;
        next_done = 1'b1;
        next_state = IDLE;
      end
      default: next_state = IDLE;
    endcase
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module array_max_min_datapath #
(
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter WIDTH = 8
)
(
  input wire clk,
  input wire rst,
  input wire [N*WIDTH-1:0] data_in,
  input [5:0] control,
  output [2:0] status,
  output reg [WIDTH-1:0] max_val,
  output reg [WIDTH-1:0] min_val);
localparam IDLE = 2'd0;
localparam SCAN = 2'd1;
localparam FIN = 2'd2;
integer k;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (k < N);
assign status[1] = (data_in[(k * WIDTH) +: WIDTH] > max_val);
assign status[2] = (data_in[(k * WIDTH) +: WIDTH] < min_val);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) if(rst) begin
  max_val <= { WIDTH{ 1'b0 } };
  min_val <= { WIDTH{ 1'b0 } };
  k <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    max_val <= data_in[0 +: WIDTH];
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    min_val <= data_in[0 +: WIDTH];
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    k <= 1;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    max_val <= data_in[(k * WIDTH) +: WIDTH];
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    min_val <= data_in[(k * WIDTH) +: WIDTH];
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    k <= k + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module array_max_min #
(
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter WIDTH = 8
)
(
  input wire clk,
  input wire rst,
  input wire start,
  input wire [N*WIDTH-1:0] data_in,
  output [WIDTH-1:0] max_val,
  output [WIDTH-1:0] min_val,
  output done,
  output busy);
wire [5:0] control;
wire [2:0] status;
array_max_min_controller #(.N(N), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst(rst),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
array_max_min_datapath #(.N(N), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst(rst),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .max_val(max_val),
    .min_val(min_val)
);
endmodule
Testbench: tb/tb_array_max_min.vتست‌بنچ: tb/tb_array_max_min.v
tb/tb_array_max_min.v
// Plain Verilog test for the preserved array_max_min interface.
// iverilog -g2005 -s tb_array_max_min -o minmax_sim rtl/array_max_min.v tb/tb_array_max_min.v
// vvp minmax_sim
`timescale 1ns/1ps
module minmax_verilog_check #(parameter W=8,N=8)(output reg finished);
    reg clk,rst,start;reg [N*W-1:0] data_in;
    wire [W-1:0] maximum,minimum;wire busy,done;
    integer trial,k,value,expected_max,expected_min,cycles,seed;
    array_max_min #(.N(N),.WIDTH(W)) dut(
        .clk(clk),.rst(rst),.start(start),.data_in(data_in),
        .max_val(maximum),.min_val(minimum),.busy(busy),.done(done)
    );
    always #5 clk=~clk;
    task tick;begin @(posedge clk);#1;@(negedge clk);end endtask
    initial begin
        clk=0;rst=1;start=0;data_in=0;finished=0;seed=134+W+N;
        tick();rst=0;
        for(trial=0;trial<100;trial=trial+1)begin
            expected_max=0;expected_min=(1<<W)-1;
            for(k=0;k<N;k=k+1)begin
                data_in[k*W+:W]=$random(seed);
                value=data_in[k*W+:W];
                if(value>expected_max)expected_max=value;
                if(value<expected_min)expected_min=value;
            end
            start=1;tick();start=0;cycles=0;
            // Legacy module scans data_in directly: keep it stable until done.
            while(!done)begin tick();cycles=cycles+1;
                if(cycles>N+10)begin $display("FAIL max/min timeout");$finish;end
            end
            if(maximum!==expected_max || minimum!==expected_min)begin
                $display("FAIL max/min W=%0d N=%0d trial=%0d",W,N,trial);$finish;
            end
            tick();tick();
        end
        $display("PASS max/min W=%0d N=%0d cases=100",W,N);finished=1;
    end
endmodule
module tb_array_max_min;
    wire a,b;
    minmax_verilog_check #(1,1) small_case(a);
    minmax_verilog_check #(8,8) array_case(b);
    initial begin wait(a&&b);$display("PASS tb_array_max_min (pure Verilog-2005)");$finish;end
    initial begin #1000000;$display("FAIL timeout");$finish;end
endmodule

Array Extremaاکسترمم آرایه array_extrema

Combinational min/max/argmax; ties retain the first elementکمینه/بیشینه/argmax ترکیبی؛ در تساوی اولین عنصر می‌ماند.

rtl/array_extrema.v

Explanationتوضیح

Combinational min/max/argmax; ties retain the first element

The controller sequences the array operation; results and status flags are valid when done pulses.

  • Source structure: This file contains a single module.
  • Reset: No reset
  • Handshake: No start/busy/done handshake; drive the control inputs directly.
  • Parameters: W, N, SIGNED, I

کمینه/بیشینه/argmax ترکیبی؛ در تساوی اولین عنصر می‌ماند.

کنترلر عملیات آرایه را ترتیب می‌دهد؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌های وضعیت وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل فقط یک ماژول دارد.
  • ریست: بدون ریست
  • Handshake: handshake start/busy/done ندارد؛ ورودی‌های کنترل را مستقیم بدهید.
  • پارامترها: W, N, SIGNED, I

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: data"] --> M["array_extrema"]
    M --> OUT["data out: minimum, maximum (+1)"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

This module is purely combinational; it has no state and therefore no FSM. It is a datapath operator controlled by signals from the surrounding controller.

این ماژول کاملاً ترکیبی است؛ حالت ندارد و بنابراین FSM ندارد. یک عملگر دیتاپدیت است که با سیگنال‌های کنترلر اطراف کنترل می‌شود.

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح

N8Number of elements / sizeتعداد عناصر / اندازه
SIGNED01 = signed two's-complement۱ = علامت‌دار مکمل دو
I(N>1?$clog2(N):1)Index widthعرض اندیس

Portsپورت‌ها

DirجهتNameنامWidthعرضDescriptionتوضیح

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/array_extrema.v
// Module: array_extrema. Original group: algorithms.
// Combinational min/max/argmax. Ties retain the first (lowest-index) element.
module array_extrema #(parameter W=8,N=8,SIGNED=0,I=(N>1?$clog2(N):1)) (
    input [N*W-1:0] data,output reg [W-1:0] minimum,maximum,output reg [I-1:0] argmax
);
    integer k;reg [W-1:0] v;
    always @* begin
        minimum=data[0 +: W];maximum=data[0 +: W];argmax=0;v=0;
        for(k=1;k<N;k=k+1) begin
            v=data[k*W +: W];
            if(SIGNED ? ($signed(v)<$signed(minimum)) : (v<minimum)) minimum=v;
            if(SIGNED ? ($signed(v)>$signed(maximum)) : (v>maximum)) begin maximum=v;argmax=k;end
        end
    end
endmodule

    IN["data in: data_in"] --> M["array_reverse"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح
W8Data widthعرض داده
N8Number of elements / sizeتعداد عناصر / اندازه

Portsپورت‌ها

DirجهتNameنامWidthعرضDescriptionتوضیح
inputclk1Clockکلاک
inputrst_n1Reset, active lowریست فعال پایین
inputstart1Start pulseپالس شروع
inputdata_inN*WInput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ ورودی
outputdata_outN*WOutput data/arrayداده/آرایهٔ خروجی
outputbusy1Operation in progressدر حال اجرا
outputdone1One-cycle completion pulseپایان (یک کلاک)

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/array_reverse.v
// Copy one element per cycle into the reversed position.

// =====================================================================
// array_reverse: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: data <= data_in;
// control[1]: p <= 0;
// control[2]: data_out[((N - 1 - p) * W) +: W] <= data[(p * W) +: W];
// control[3]: p <= p + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module array_reverse_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [0:0] status,
  output reg [3:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      if(status[0]) begin
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else control[3] = 1'b1;
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module array_reverse_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [3:0] control,
  output [0:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] data_out);
reg [N*W-1:0] data;
integer p;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (p == N - 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data <= 0;
  data_out <= 0;
  p <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    data <= data_in;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    data_out[((N - 1 - p) * W) +: W] <= data[(p * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module array_reverse #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [3:0] control;
wire [0:0] status;
array_reverse_controller #(.W(W), .N(N)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
array_reverse_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

Search & Array Algorithmsجستجو و الگوریتم‌های آرایه

Array Sumجمع آرایه array_sum

Controller + datapath + top; accumulates one element per RUN cycleکنترلر + دیتاپدیت + top؛ در هر چرخهٔ RUN یک عنصر را جمع می‌کند.

rtl/array_sum.v

Explanationتوضیح

Controller + datapath + top; accumulates one element per RUN cycle

The controller sequences the array operation; results and status flags are valid when done pulses.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus array_sum_controller and array_sum_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, N, AW, I

کنترلر + دیتاپدیت + top؛ در هر چرخهٔ RUN یک عنصر را جمع می‌کند.

کنترلر عملیات آرایه را ترتیب می‌دهد؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌های وضعیت وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه array_sum_controller و array_sum_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, N, AW, I

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: data_in"] --> M["array_sum"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: sum"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.

    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/array_sum.v
// Module: array_sum_controller. Original group: fsm.
// Controller template used by array_sum. One element is accumulated per RUN cycle.
module array_sum_controller #(parameter N=8,I=(N>1?$clog2(N):1)) (
    input clk,rst_n,start,output reg clear,step,busy,done,output reg [I-1:0] index
);
    localparam IDLE=0,RUN=1,REPORT=2;reg [1:0] state;
    always @* begin clear=0;step=0;busy=(state!=IDLE);done=(state==REPORT);
        if(state==IDLE && start) clear=1;
        if(state==RUN) step=1;
    end
    always @(posedge clk)
        if(!rst_n) begin state<=IDLE;index<=0;end
        else case(state)
            IDLE: if(start) begin index<=0;state<=RUN;end
            RUN: if(index==N-1) state<=REPORT;else index<=index+1'b1;
            REPORT: state<=IDLE;
            default: state<=IDLE;
        endcase
endmodule

// Module: array_sum_datapath. Original group: fsm.
module array_sum_datapath #(parameter W=8,N=8,AW=W+$clog2(N),I=(N>1?$clog2(N):1)) (
    input clk,rst_n,clear,step,input [I-1:0] index,input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
    output reg [AW-1:0] sum
);
    reg [N*W-1:0] data;
    always @(posedge clk)
        if(!rst_n) begin sum<=0;data<=0;end
        else if(clear) begin sum<=0;data<=data_in;end
        else if(step) sum<=sum+data[index*W +: W];
endmodule

// Module: array_sum. Original group: fsm.
// Complete controller/datapath example. Data is latched when start is accepted.
module array_sum #(parameter W=8,N=8,AW=W+$clog2(N),I=(N>1?$clog2(N):1)) (
    input clk,rst_n,start,input [N*W-1:0] data_in,output [AW-1:0] sum,output busy,done
);
    wire clear,step;wire [I-1:0] index;
    array_sum_controller #(N,I) ctrl(clk,rst_n,start,clear,step,busy,done,index);
    array_sum_datapath #(W,N,AW,I) dp(clk,rst_n,clear,step,index,data_in,sum);
endmodule

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/prefix_sum.v
// Inclusive unsigned prefix sum. AW defaults to sufficient full precision.

// =====================================================================
// prefix_sum: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: data <= data_in;
// control[1]: total <= 0;
// control[2]: p <= 0;
// control[3]: total <= next_total;
// control[4]: data_out[(p * AW) +: AW] <= next_total;
// control[5]: p <= p + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module prefix_sum_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter AW = W + $clog2(N)
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [0:0] status,
  output reg [5:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
      if(status[0]) begin
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else control[5] = 1'b1;
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module prefix_sum_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter AW = W + $clog2(N)
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  input [5:0] control,
  output [0:0] status,
  output reg [N*AW-1:0] data_out);
reg [N*W-1:0] data;
reg [AW-1:0] total;
integer p;
wire [AW-1:0] next_total;
assign next_total = total + data[(p * W) +: W];

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (p == N - 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  data <= 0;
  data_out <= 0;
  total <= 0;
  p <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    data <= data_in;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    total <= 0;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    total <= next_total;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    data_out[(p * AW) +: AW] <= next_total;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module prefix_sum #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter AW = W + $clog2(N)
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] data_in,
  output [N*AW-1:0] data_out,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [5:0] control;
wire [0:0] status;
prefix_sum_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .AW(AW)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
prefix_sum_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .AW(AW)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .data_in(data_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .data_out(data_out)
);
endmodule

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/dot_product.v
// Sequential dot product. AW>=2*W+ceil(log2(N)) retains full precision.

// =====================================================================
// dot_product: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: sa <= a;
// control[1]: sb <= b;
// control[2]: total <= 0;
// control[3]: p <= 0;
// control[4]: total <= total + extended;
// control[5]: result <= total + extended;
// control[6]: p <= p + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module dot_product_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter AW = 2 * W + $clog2(N)
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [0:0] status,
  output reg [6:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      control[4] = 1'b1;
      if(status[0]) begin
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else control[6] = 1'b1;
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module dot_product_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter AW = 2 * W + $clog2(N)
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] a,
  input [N*W-1:0] b,
  input [6:0] control,
  output [0:0] status,
  output reg [AW-1:0] result);
reg [N*W-1:0] sa;
reg [N*W-1:0] sb;
reg [AW-1:0] total;
integer p;
wire [W-1:0] x;
assign x = sa[(p * W) +: W];
wire [W-1:0] y;
assign y = sb[(p * W) +: W];
wire [2*W-1:0] product;
wire [AW-1:0] extended;

generate if(SIGNED) begin : signed_math
  assign product = $signed(x) * $signed(y);
  assign extended = $signed(product);
end else begin : unsigned_math
  assign product = x * y;
  assign extended = product;
end
endgenerate


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (p == N - 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  sa <= 0;
  sb <= 0;
  total <= 0;
  result <= 0;
  p <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    sa <= a;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    sb <= b;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    total <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    total <= total + extended;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    result <= total + extended;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    p <= p + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module dot_product #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter AW = 2 * W + $clog2(N)
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] a,
  input [N*W-1:0] b,
  output [AW-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [6:0] control;
wire [0:0] status;
dot_product_controller #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .AW(AW)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
dot_product_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .AW(AW)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .a(a),
    .b(b),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result)
);
endmodule

Search & Array Algorithmsجستجو و الگوریتم‌های آرایه

Matrix Multiplyضرب ماتریس matrix_multiply

Row-major A[R,K] * B[K,C]; one multiply-accumulate per clockضرب ماتریس A[R,K] * B[K,C] سطری؛ در هر کلاک یک MAC.

rtl/matrix_multiply.v

Explanationتوضیح

Row-major A[R,K] * B[K,C]; one multiply-accumulate per clock

The controller sequences the array operation; results and status flags are valid when done pulses.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus matrix_multiply_controller and matrix_multiply_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, R, K, C, SIGNED, AW

ضرب ماتریس A[R,K] * B[K,C] سطری؛ در هر کلاک یک MAC.

کنترلر عملیات آرایه را ترتیب می‌دهد؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌های وضعیت وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه matrix_multiply_controller و matrix_multiply_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, R, K, C, SIGNED, AW

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: a, b"] --> M["matrix_multiply"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: result"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح
W8Data widthعرض داده
R2Matrix row countبعد سطرهای ماتریس
K3Matrix inner dimensionبعد مشترک ماتریس
C2Count widthعرض شمارنده
SIGNED01 = signed two's-complement۱ = علامت‌دار مکمل دو
AW2 * W + $clog2(K)Accumulator/result widthعرض انباره/نتیجه

Portsپورت‌ها

DirجهتNameنامWidthعرضDescriptionتوضیح

inputrst_n1Reset, active lowریست فعال پایین
inputstart1Start pulseپالس شروع
inputaR*K*WOperand aعملوند اول
inputbK*C*WOperand bعملوند دوم
outputresultR*C*AWResultنتیجه
outputbusy1Operation in progressدر حال اجرا

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/matrix_multiply.v
// Row-major A[R,K] times B[K,C]. One multiply-accumulate per clock.
// Signed/unsigned full-precision output by default; lane (r*C+c) is result[r,c].

// =====================================================================
// matrix_multiply: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: sa <= a;
// control[1]: sb <= b;
// control[2]: row <= 0;
// control[3]: col <= 0;
// control[4]: term <= 0;
// control[5]: total <= 0;
// control[6]: result[((row * C + col) * AW) +: AW] <= total + extended;
// control[7]: total <= 0;
// control[8]: term <= 0;
// control[9]: col <= 0;
// control[10]: row <= row + 1;
// control[11]: col <= col + 1;
// control[12]: total <= total + extended;
// control[13]: term <= term + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module matrix_multiply_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter R = 2,
  parameter K = 3,
  parameter C = 2,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter AW = 2 * W + $clog2(K)
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [2:0] status,
  output reg [13:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
      control[5] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(status[0]) begin
        control[6] = 1'b1;
        control[7] = 1'b1;
        control[8] = 1'b1;
        if(status[1]) begin
          control[9] = 1'b1;
          if(status[2]) begin
            next_busy = 0;
            next_done = 1;
          end else control[10] = 1'b1;
        end else control[11] = 1'b1;
      end else begin
        control[12] = 1'b1;
        control[13] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module matrix_multiply_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter R = 2,
  parameter K = 3,
  parameter C = 2,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter AW = 2 * W + $clog2(K)
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [R*K*W-1:0] a,
  input [K*C*W-1:0] b,
  input [13:0] control,
  output [2:0] status,
  output reg [R*C*AW-1:0] result);
reg [R*K*W-1:0] sa;
reg [K*C*W-1:0] sb;
integer row;
integer col;
integer term;
reg [AW-1:0] total;
wire [W-1:0] x;
assign x = sa[((row * K + term) * W) +: W];
wire [W-1:0] y;
assign y = sb[((term * C + col) * W) +: W];
wire [2*W-1:0] product;
wire [AW-1:0] extended;

generate if(SIGNED) begin : signed_math
  assign product = $signed(x) * $signed(y);
  assign extended = $signed(product);
end else begin : unsigned_math
  assign product = x * y;
  assign extended = product;
end
endgenerate


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (term == K - 1);
assign status[1] = (col == C - 1);
assign status[2] = (row == R - 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  sa <= 0;
  sb <= 0;
  result <= 0;
  total <= 0;
  row <= 0;
  col <= 0;
  term <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    sa <= a;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    sb <= b;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    row <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    col <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    term <= 0;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    total <= 0;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    result[((row * C + col) * AW) +: AW] <= total + extended;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    total <= 0;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    term <= 0;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    col <= 0;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    row <= row + 1;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    col <= col + 1;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    total <= total + extended;
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    term <= term + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module matrix_multiply #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter R = 2,
  parameter K = 3,
  parameter C = 2,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter AW = 2 * W + $clog2(K)
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [R*K*W-1:0] a,
  input [K*C*W-1:0] b,
  output [R*C*AW-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [13:0] control;
wire [2:0] status;
matrix_multiply_controller #(.W(W), .R(R), .K(K), .C(C), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .AW(AW)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
matrix_multiply_datapath #(.W(W), .R(R), .K(K), .C(C), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .AW(AW)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .a(a),
    .b(b),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result)
);
endmodule

Convolutionکانولوشن convolution

Full linear convolution; output length NA+NB-1; one valid MAC per cycleکانولوشن خطی کامل؛ طول خروجی NA+NB-1؛ در هر کلاک یک MAC معتبر.

rtl/convolution.v

Explanationتوضیح

Full linear convolution; output length NA+NB-1; one valid MAC per cycle

The controller sequences the array operation; results and status flags are valid when done pulses.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus convolution_controller and convolution_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, NA, NB, SIGNED, AW

کانولوشن خطی کامل؛ طول خروجی NA+NB-1؛ در هر کلاک یک MAC معتبر.

کنترلر عملیات آرایه را ترتیب می‌دهد؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌های وضعیت وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه convolution_controller و convolution_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, NA, NB, SIGNED, AW

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
