RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/convolution.v
// Full linear convolution y[t]=sum_k a[k]*b[t-k], length NA+NB-1.
// Unsigned or signed integer inputs, one valid MAC per cycle.

// =====================================================================
// convolution: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: sa <= a;
// control[1]: sb <= b;
// control[2]: total <= 0;
// control[3]: t <= 0;
// control[4]: k <= 0;
// control[5]: result[(t * AW) +: AW] <= total + extended;
// control[6]: total <= 0;
// control[7]: k <= 0;
// control[8]: t <= t + 1;
// control[9]: total <= total + extended;
// control[10]: k <= k + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module convolution_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter NA = 4,
  parameter NB = 3,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter AW = 2 * W + $clog2(((NA < NB)? NA : NB))
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [1:0] status,
  output reg [10:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(status[0]) begin
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        control[6] = 1'b1;
        control[7] = 1'b1;
        if(status[1]) begin
          next_busy = 0;
          next_done = 1;
        end else control[8] = 1'b1;
      end else begin
        control[9] = 1'b1;
        control[10] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module convolution_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter NA = 4,
  parameter NB = 3,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter AW = 2 * W + $clog2(((NA < NB)? NA : NB))
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [NA*W-1:0] a,
  input [NB*W-1:0] b,
  input [10:0] control,
  output [1:0] status,
  output reg [(NA+NB-1)*AW-1:0] result);
reg [NA*W-1:0] sa;
reg [NB*W-1:0] sb;
reg [AW-1:0] total;
integer t;
integer k;
wire valid_pair;
assign valid_pair = (t >= k) && (t - k < NB);
wire [W-1:0] x;
assign x = sa[(k * W) +: W];
wire [W-1:0] y;
assign y = sb[(((valid_pair)? t - k : 0) * W) +: W];
wire [2*W-1:0] product;
wire [AW-1:0] extended;

generate if(SIGNED) begin : signed_math
  wire signed [AW-1:0] sign_extended;
  assign product = $signed(x) * $signed(y);
  assign sign_extended = $signed(product);
  assign extended = (valid_pair)? sign_extended : { AW{ 1'b0 } };
end else begin : unsigned_math
  assign product = x * y;
  assign extended = (valid_pair)? product : { AW{ 1'b0 } };
end
endgenerate


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (k == NA - 1);
assign status[1] = (t == NA + NB - 2);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  sa <= 0;
  sb <= 0;
  result <= 0;
  total <= 0;
  t <= 0;
  k <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    sa <= a;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    sb <= b;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    total <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    t <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    k <= 0;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    result[(t * AW) +: AW] <= total + extended;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    total <= 0;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    k <= 0;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    t <= t + 1;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    total <= total + extended;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    k <= k + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module convolution #
(
  parameter W = 8,
  parameter NA = 4,
  parameter NB = 3,
  parameter SIGNED = 0,
  parameter AW = 2 * W + $clog2(((NA < NB)? NA : NB))
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [NA*W-1:0] a,
  input [NB*W-1:0] b,
  output [(NA+NB-1)*AW-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [10:0] control;
wire [1:0] status;
convolution_controller #(.W(W), .NA(NA), .NB(NB), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .AW(AW)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
convolution_datapath #(.W(W), .NA(NA), .NB(NB), .SIGNED(SIGNED), .AW(AW)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .a(a),
    .b(b),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result)
);
endmodule

Numerical, Integer & Fixed-Pointعددی، صحیح و ممیز ثابت

Numerical, Integer & Fixed-Pointعددی، صحیح و ممیز ثابت

Taylor Seriesسری تیلور taylor_series

Generic fixed-point Taylor engine; MODE=0 exp, 1 sin, 2 cosموتور تیلور fixed-point عمومی؛ MODE=0 exp، 1 sin، 2 cos.

rtl/taylor_series.v

Explanationتوضیح

Generic fixed-point Taylor engine; MODE=0 exp, 1 sin, 2 cos

The controller iterates the fixed-point/integer algorithm; results and flags are valid when done pulses. Check the overflow and out-of-range outputs.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus taylor_series_controller and taylor_series_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, Q, TERMS, MODE

موتور تیلور fixed-point عمومی؛ MODE=0 exp، 1 sin، 2 cos.

کنترلر الگوریتم fixed-point/صحیح را تکرار می‌کند؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌ها وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند. خروجی‌های overflow و out_of_range را بررسی کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه taylor_series_controller و taylor_series_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, Q, TERMS, MODE

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: x"] --> M["taylor_series"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: result"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done (+2)"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

Q20Fractional bits (fixed-point)بیت‌های کسری (fixed-point)
TERMS12Number of Taylor termsتعداد جمله‌های تیلور

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/taylor_series.v
// Fixed-point Taylor series, signed value / 2**Q. MODE: 0 exp, 1 sin, 2 cos.
// Supported: 8<=W<=32, 4<=Q<=24, W>=Q+4, 2<=TERMS<=16.
// Domain: exp |x|<=1; sin/cos |x|<=pi. out_of_range reports invalid domain.
// One term per cycle, truncation after products/divisions; output saturates.

// =====================================================================
// taylor_series: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: argument <= x;
// control[1]: square <= $signed(x) * $signed({ { W{ x[W - 1] } }, x }) >>> Q;
// control[2]: term <= (MODE == 1)? $signed(x) : ONE;
// control[3]: total <= (MODE == 1)? $signed(x) : ONE;
// control[4]: k <= 1;
// control[5]: overflow <= 0;
// control[6]: out_of_range <= ($signed(x) > ((MODE == 0)? ONE : PI_Q)) || ($signed(x) < -((MODE == 0)? ONE : PI_Q));
// control[7]: term <= next_term;
// control[8]: total <= next_total;
// control[9]: k <= k + 1;
// control[10]: result <= MAXIMUM;
// control[11]: overflow <= 1;
// control[12]: result <= MINIMUM;
// control[13]: overflow <= 1;
// control[14]: result <= next_total;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module taylor_series_controller #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter TERMS = 12,
  parameter MODE = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [2:0] status,
  output reg [14:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);
localparam signed [63:0] ONE = 64'sd1 << Q;
localparam signed [63:0] PI_Q = 64'sd3373259426 >> 30 - Q;
localparam signed [63:0] MAXIMUM = (64'sd1 << W - 1) - 1;
localparam signed [63:0] MINIMUM = -(64'sd1 << W - 1);

reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
      control[5] = 1'b1;
      control[6] = 1'b1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      control[7] = 1'b1;
      control[8] = 1'b1;
      control[9] = 1'b1;
      if(status[0]) begin
        if(status[1]) begin
          control[10] = 1'b1;
          control[11] = 1'b1;
        end else if(status[2]) begin
          control[12] = 1'b1;
          control[13] = 1'b1;
        end else control[14] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module taylor_series_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter TERMS = 12,
  parameter MODE = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input signed [W-1:0] x,
  input [14:0] control,
  output [2:0] status,
  output reg signed [W-1:0] result,
  output reg out_of_range,
  output reg overflow);
localparam signed [63:0] ONE = 64'sd1 << Q;
localparam signed [63:0] PI_Q = 64'sd3373259426 >> 30 - Q;
localparam signed [63:0] MAXIMUM = (64'sd1 << W - 1) - 1;
localparam signed [63:0] MINIMUM = -(64'sd1 << W - 1);
reg signed [63:0] argument;
reg signed [63:0] square;
reg signed [63:0] term;
reg signed [63:0] total;
reg signed [63:0] next_term;
reg signed [63:0] next_total;
reg signed [127:0] product;
reg signed [127:0] scaled;
integer k;
integer denominator;

always @(*) begin
  denominator = k;
  if(MODE == 0) product = term * argument;
  else begin
    product = -term * square;
    denominator = (MODE == 1)? 2 * k * (2 * k + 1) : (2 * k - 1) * (2 * k);
  end
  scaled = product >>> Q;
  next_term = scaled / ((denominator > 0)? denominator : 1);
  next_total = total + next_term;
end


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (k == TERMS - 1);
assign status[1] = (next_total > MAXIMUM);
assign status[2] = (next_total < MINIMUM);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  result <= 0;
  out_of_range <= 0;
  overflow <= 0;
  argument <= 0;
  square <= 0;
  term <= 0;
  total <= 0;
  k <= 1;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    argument <= x;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    square <= $signed(x) * $signed({ { W{ x[W - 1] } }, x }) >>> Q;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    term <= (MODE == 1)? $signed(x) : ONE;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    total <= (MODE == 1)? $signed(x) : ONE;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    k <= 1;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    overflow <= 0;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    out_of_range <= ($signed(x) > ((MODE == 0)? ONE : PI_Q)) || ($signed(x) < -((MODE == 0)? ONE : PI_Q));
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    term <= next_term;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    total <= next_total;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    k <= k + 1;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    result <= MAXIMUM;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    result <= MINIMUM;
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
  if(control[14]) begin
    result <= next_total;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module taylor_series #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter TERMS = 12,
  parameter MODE = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input signed [W-1:0] x,
  output signed [W-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done,
  output out_of_range,
  output overflow);
wire [14:0] control;
wire [2:0] status;
taylor_series_controller #(.W(W), .Q(Q), .TERMS(TERMS), .MODE(MODE)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
taylor_series_datapath #(.W(W), .Q(Q), .TERMS(TERMS), .MODE(MODE)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .x(x),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result),
    .out_of_range(out_of_range),
    .overflow(overflow)
);
endmodule

exp(x) Taylor wrapper (MODE=0)

The controller iterates the fixed-point/integer algorithm; results and flags are valid when done pulses. Check the overflow and out-of-range outputs.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus exp_taylor_controller and exp_taylor_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, Q, TERMS

(
  parameter W = 32,

  input rst_n,

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/sin_taylor.v
// Fixed-point Taylor series, signed value / 2**Q. MODE: 0 exp, 1 sin, 2 cos.
// Supported: 8<=W<=32, 4<=Q<=24, W>=Q+4, 2<=TERMS<=16.
// Domain: exp |x|<=1; sin/cos |x|<=pi. out_of_range reports invalid domain.
// One term per cycle, truncation after products/divisions; output saturates.

// =====================================================================
// sin_taylor: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: argument <= x;
// control[1]: square <= $signed(x) * $signed({ { W{ x[W - 1] } }, x }) >>> Q;
// control[2]: term <= (MODE == 1)? $signed(x) : ONE;
// control[3]: total <= (MODE == 1)? $signed(x) : ONE;
// control[4]: k <= 1;
// control[5]: overflow <= 0;
// control[6]: out_of_range <= ($signed(x) > ((MODE == 0)? ONE : PI_Q)) || ($signed(x) < -((MODE == 0)? ONE : PI_Q));
// control[7]: term <= next_term;
// control[8]: total <= next_total;
// control[9]: k <= k + 1;
// control[10]: result <= MAXIMUM;
// control[11]: overflow <= 1;
// control[12]: result <= MINIMUM;
// control[13]: overflow <= 1;
// control[14]: result <= next_total;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module sin_taylor_controller #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter TERMS = 12
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [2:0] status,
  output reg [14:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);
localparam MODE = 1;
localparam signed [63:0] ONE = 64'sd1 << Q;
localparam signed [63:0] PI_Q = 64'sd3373259426 >> 30 - Q;
localparam signed [63:0] MAXIMUM = (64'sd1 << W - 1) - 1;
localparam signed [63:0] MINIMUM = -(64'sd1 << W - 1);

reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
      control[5] = 1'b1;
      control[6] = 1'b1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      control[7] = 1'b1;
      control[8] = 1'b1;
      control[9] = 1'b1;
      if(status[0]) begin
        if(status[1]) begin
          control[10] = 1'b1;
          control[11] = 1'b1;
        end else if(status[2]) begin
          control[12] = 1'b1;
          control[13] = 1'b1;
        end else control[14] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module sin_taylor_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter TERMS = 12
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input signed [W-1:0] x,
  input [14:0] control,
  output [2:0] status,
  output reg signed [W-1:0] result,
  output reg out_of_range,
  output reg overflow);
localparam MODE = 1;
localparam signed [63:0] ONE = 64'sd1 << Q;
localparam signed [63:0] PI_Q = 64'sd3373259426 >> 30 - Q;
localparam signed [63:0] MAXIMUM = (64'sd1 << W - 1) - 1;
localparam signed [63:0] MINIMUM = -(64'sd1 << W - 1);
reg signed [63:0] argument;
reg signed [63:0] square;
reg signed [63:0] term;
reg signed [63:0] total;
reg signed [63:0] next_term;
reg signed [63:0] next_total;
reg signed [127:0] product;
reg signed [127:0] scaled;
integer k;
integer denominator;

always @(*) begin
  denominator = k;
  if(MODE == 0) product = term * argument;
  else begin
    product = -term * square;
    denominator = (MODE == 1)? 2 * k * (2 * k + 1) : (2 * k - 1) * (2 * k);
  end
  scaled = product >>> Q;
  next_term = scaled / ((denominator > 0)? denominator : 1);
  next_total = total + next_term;
end


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (k == TERMS - 1);
assign status[1] = (next_total > MAXIMUM);
assign status[2] = (next_total < MINIMUM);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  result <= 0;
  out_of_range <= 0;
  overflow <= 0;
  argument <= 0;
  square <= 0;
  term <= 0;
  total <= 0;
  k <= 1;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    argument <= x;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    square <= $signed(x) * $signed({ { W{ x[W - 1] } }, x }) >>> Q;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    term <= (MODE == 1)? $signed(x) : ONE;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    total <= (MODE == 1)? $signed(x) : ONE;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    k <= 1;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    overflow <= 0;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    out_of_range <= ($signed(x) > ((MODE == 0)? ONE : PI_Q)) || ($signed(x) < -((MODE == 0)? ONE : PI_Q));
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    term <= next_term;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    total <= next_total;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    k <= k + 1;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    result <= MAXIMUM;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    result <= MINIMUM;
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
  if(control[14]) begin
    result <= next_total;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module sin_taylor #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter TERMS = 12
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input signed [W-1:0] x,
  output signed [W-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done,
  output out_of_range,
  output overflow);
wire [14:0] control;
wire [2:0] status;
sin_taylor_controller #(.W(W), .Q(Q), .TERMS(TERMS)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
sin_taylor_datapath #(.W(W), .Q(Q), .TERMS(TERMS)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .x(x),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result),
    .out_of_range(out_of_range),
    .overflow(overflow)
);
endmodule

Cos Taylorتیلور cos cos_taylor

cos(x) Taylor wrapper (MODE=2)پوشش cos(x) با تیلور (MODE=2).

rtl/cos_taylor.v

Explanationتوضیح

cos(x) Taylor wrapper (MODE=2)

The controller iterates the fixed-point/integer algorithm; results and flags are valid when done pulses. Check the overflow and out-of-range outputs.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus cos_taylor_controller and cos_taylor_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, Q, TERMS

پوشش cos(x) با تیلور (MODE=2).

کنترلر الگوریتم fixed-point/صحیح را تکرار می‌کند؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌ها وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند. خروجی‌های overflow و out_of_range را بررسی کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه cos_taylor_controller و cos_taylor_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, Q, TERMS

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: x"] --> M["cos_taylor"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: result"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done (+2)"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.

    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/exponential_fixed.v
// exp(x) for [-4,4]: evaluate exp(x/4), then square twice.
// W<=32, Q<=20, W>=Q+4; four internal guard bits; saturating signed output.

// =====================================================================
// exponential_fixed: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: child_start <= 0;
// control[1]: reduced <= $signed(x) <<< 2;
// control[2]: child_start <= 1;
// control[3]: overflow <= 0;
// control[4]: out_of_range <= ($signed(x) > (64'sd4 << Q)) || ($signed(x) < -(64'sd4 << Q));
// control[5]: value <= base;
// control[6]: squares <= 0;
// control[7]: value <= MAXIMUM;
// control[8]: overflow <= 1;
// control[9]: value <= product;
// control[10]: squares <= squares + 1;
// control[11]: result <= value >>> 4;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module exponential_fixed_controller #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter TERMS = 12
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [2:0] status,
  output reg [11:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);
localparam signed [127:0] MAXIMUM = (128'sd1 << W - 1) - 1 << 4;
reg [1:0] state;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg [1:0] next_state;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  state <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  state <= next_state;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_state = state;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    control[0] = 1'b1;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
      next_state = 0;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
    end else if(busy) case(state)
      0: if(status[0]) begin
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        control[6] = 1'b1;
        next_state = 1;
      end
      1: begin
        if(status[1]) begin
          control[7] = 1'b1;
          control[8] = 1'b1;
        end else control[9] = 1'b1;
        control[10] = 1'b1;
        if(status[2]) next_state = 2;
      end
      2: begin
        control[11] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
      default: next_state = 0;
    endcase
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module exponential_fixed_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter TERMS = 12
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input signed [W-1:0] x,
  input [11:0] control,
  output [2:0] status,
  output reg signed [W-1:0] result,
  output reg out_of_range,
  output reg overflow);
localparam signed [127:0] MAXIMUM = (128'sd1 << W - 1) - 1 << 4;
reg signed [31:0] reduced;
reg child_start;
wire child_busy;
wire child_done;
wire child_range;
wire child_overflow;
wire signed [31:0] base;
reg signed [63:0] value;
reg signed [127:0] product;
integer squares;

exponential_fixed_helper_exp_taylor
#(
  32,
  Q + 4,
  TERMS
)
core
(
  clk,
  rst_n,
  child_start,
  reduced,
  base,
  child_busy,
  child_done,
  child_range,
  child_overflow
);


always @(*) product = value * value >>> Q + 4;


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = child_done;
assign status[1] = (product > MAXIMUM);
assign status[2] = (squares == 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  result <= 0;
  out_of_range <= 0;
  overflow <= 0;
  reduced <= 0;
  child_start <= 0;
  value <= 0;
  squares <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    child_start <= 0;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    reduced <= $signed(x) <<< 2;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    child_start <= 1;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    overflow <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    out_of_range <= ($signed(x) > (64'sd4 << Q)) || ($signed(x) < -(64'sd4 << Q));
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    value <= base;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    squares <= 0;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    value <= MAXIMUM;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    value <= product;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    squares <= squares + 1;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    result <= value >>> 4;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module exponential_fixed #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter TERMS = 12
)
(
  input clk,

  input start,
  input signed [W-1:0] x,
  output signed [W-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done,
  output out_of_range,
  output overflow);

wire [2:0] status;
exponential_fixed_controller #(.W(W), .Q(Q), .TERMS(TERMS)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);

    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .x(x),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result),
    .out_of_range(out_of_range),
    .overflow(overflow)
);
endmodule

module exponential_fixed_helper_exp_taylor_controller #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter TERMS = 12
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [2:0] status,
  output reg [14:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);
localparam MODE = 0;
localparam signed [63:0] ONE = 64'sd1 << Q;
localparam signed [63:0] PI_Q = 64'sd3373259426 >> 30 - Q;
localparam signed [63:0] MAXIMUM = (64'sd1 << W - 1) - 1;
localparam signed [63:0] MINIMUM = -(64'sd1 << W - 1);

reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
      control[5] = 1'b1;
      control[6] = 1'b1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      control[7] = 1'b1;
      control[8] = 1'b1;
      control[9] = 1'b1;
      if(status[0]) begin
        if(status[1]) begin
          control[10] = 1'b1;
          control[11] = 1'b1;
        end else if(status[2]) begin
          control[12] = 1'b1;
          control[13] = 1'b1;
        end else control[14] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

module exponential_fixed_helper_exp_taylor_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter TERMS = 12
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input signed [W-1:0] x,
  input [14:0] control,
  output [2:0] status,
  output reg signed [W-1:0] result,
  output reg out_of_range,
  output reg overflow);
localparam MODE = 0;
localparam signed [63:0] ONE = 64'sd1 << Q;
localparam signed [63:0] PI_Q = 64'sd3373259426 >> 30 - Q;
localparam signed [63:0] MAXIMUM = (64'sd1 << W - 1) - 1;
localparam signed [63:0] MINIMUM = -(64'sd1 << W - 1);
reg signed [63:0] argument;
reg signed [63:0] square;
reg signed [63:0] term;
reg signed [63:0] total;
reg signed [63:0] next_term;
reg signed [63:0] next_total;
reg signed [127:0] product;
reg signed [127:0] scaled;
integer k;
integer denominator;

always @(*) begin
  denominator = k;
  if(MODE == 0) product = term * argument;
  else begin
    product = -term * square;
    denominator = (MODE == 1)? 2 * k * (2 * k + 1) : (2 * k - 1) * (2 * k);
  end
  scaled = product >>> Q;
  next_term = scaled / ((denominator > 0)? denominator : 1);
  next_total = total + next_term;
end


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (k == TERMS - 1);
assign status[1] = (next_total > MAXIMUM);
assign status[2] = (next_total < MINIMUM);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  result <= 0;
  out_of_range <= 0;
  overflow <= 0;
  argument <= 0;
  square <= 0;
  term <= 0;
  total <= 0;
  k <= 1;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    argument <= x;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    square <= $signed(x) * $signed({ { W{ x[W - 1] } }, x }) >>> Q;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    term <= (MODE == 1)? $signed(x) : ONE;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    total <= (MODE == 1)? $signed(x) : ONE;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    k <= 1;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    overflow <= 0;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    out_of_range <= ($signed(x) > ((MODE == 0)? ONE : PI_Q)) || ($signed(x) < -((MODE == 0)? ONE : PI_Q));
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    term <= next_term;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    total <= next_total;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    k <= k + 1;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    result <= MAXIMUM;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    result <= MINIMUM;
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
  if(control[14]) begin
    result <= next_total;
  end
end

endmodule

Numerical, Integer & Fixed-Pointعددی، صحیح و ممیز ثابت

Fourier Seriesسری فوریه fourier_series

Finite real Fourier series on theta=2*pi*phase/64سری فوریهٔ حقیقی متناهی روی theta=2*pi*phase/64.

rtl/fourier_series.v

Explanationتوضیح

Finite real Fourier series on theta=2*pi*phase/64

The controller iterates the fixed-point/integer algorithm; results and flags are valid when done pulses. Check the overflow and out-of-range outputs.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus fourier_series_controller and fourier_series_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, Q, H

سری فوریهٔ حقیقی متناهی روی theta=2*pi*phase/64.

کنترلر الگوریتم fixed-point/صحیح را تکرار می‌کند؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌ها وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند. خروجی‌های overflow و out_of_range را بررسی کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه fourier_series_controller و fourier_series_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, Q, H

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: phase, cosine_coefficients (+1)"] --> M["fourier_series"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: result"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done (+1)"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/fourier_series.v
// Finite real Fourier series on theta=2*pi*phase/64:
// y=c[0]+sum(h=1..H){c[h]*cos(h*theta)+s[h]*sin(h*theta)}.
// c[0] is the actual constant term (predivide a0 by 2 if using that convention).
// Signed fixed-point coefficients/results; H>=0; sine coefficient s[0] ignored.
// One harmonic MAC per cycle; output saturates and sets overflow.

// =====================================================================
// fourier_series: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: c <= cosine_coefficients;
// control[1]: s <= sine_coefficients;
// control[2]: saved_phase <= phase;
// control[3]: harmonic <= 1;
// control[4]: total <= $signed(cosine_coefficients[0 +: W]);
// control[5]: overflow <= 0;
// control[6]: result <= MAXIMUM;
// control[7]: overflow <= 1;
// control[8]: result <= MINIMUM;
// control[9]: overflow <= 1;
// control[10]: result <= (H == 0)? total : next_total;
// control[11]: total <= next_total;
// control[12]: harmonic <= harmonic + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module fourier_series_controller #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter H = 3
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [2:0] status,
  output reg [12:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);
localparam signed [63:0] MAXIMUM = (64'sd1 << W - 1) - 1;
localparam signed [63:0] MINIMUM = -(64'sd1 << W - 1);

reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
      control[5] = 1'b1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if((H == 0) || status[0]) begin
        if(status[1]) begin
          control[6] = 1'b1;
          control[7] = 1'b1;
        end else if(status[2]) begin
          control[8] = 1'b1;
          control[9] = 1'b1;
        end else control[10] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else begin
        control[11] = 1'b1;
        control[12] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module fourier_series_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter H = 3
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [5:0] phase,
  input [(H+1)*W-1:0] cosine_coefficients,
  input [(H+1)*W-1:0] sine_coefficients,
  input [12:0] control,
  output [2:0] status,
  output reg signed [W-1:0] result,
  output reg overflow);
localparam signed [63:0] MAXIMUM = (64'sd1 << W - 1) - 1;
localparam signed [63:0] MINIMUM = -(64'sd1 << W - 1);
reg [(H+1)*W-1:0] c;
reg [(H+1)*W-1:0] s;
reg [5:0] saved_phase;
integer harmonic;
reg signed [63:0] total;
reg signed [63:0] next_total;
reg signed [127:0] product;
wire [5:0] angle;
assign angle = saved_phase * harmonic;
wire signed [W-1:0] cosine;
wire signed [W-1:0] negative_sine;
wire signed [W-1:0] ch;
assign ch = c[(((harmonic <= H)? harmonic : 0) * W) +: W];
wire signed [W-1:0] sh;
assign sh = s[(((harmonic <= H)? harmonic : 0) * W) +: W];

fourier_series_helper_twiddle_rom
#(
  W,
  Q
)
table_rom
(
  angle,
  cosine,
  negative_sine
);


always @(*) begin
  product = $signed(ch) * $signed(cosine) - $signed(sh) * $signed(negative_sine);
  next_total = total + (product >>> Q);
end


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (harmonic == H);
assign status[1] = (((H == 0)? total : next_total) > MAXIMUM);
assign status[2] = (((H == 0)? total : next_total) < MINIMUM);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  c <= 0;
  s <= 0;
  saved_phase <= 0;
  harmonic <= 1;
  total <= 0;
  result <= 0;
  overflow <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    c <= cosine_coefficients;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    s <= sine_coefficients;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    saved_phase <= phase;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    harmonic <= 1;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    total <= $signed(cosine_coefficients[0 +: W]);
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    overflow <= 0;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    result <= MAXIMUM;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    result <= MINIMUM;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    result <= (H == 0)? total : next_total;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    total <= next_total;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    harmonic <= harmonic + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module fourier_series #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter H = 3
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [5:0] phase,
  input [(H+1)*W-1:0] cosine_coefficients,
  input [(H+1)*W-1:0] sine_coefficients,
  output signed [W-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done,
  output overflow);
wire [12:0] control;
wire [2:0] status;
fourier_series_controller #(.W(W), .Q(Q), .H(H)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
fourier_series_datapath #(.W(W), .Q(Q), .H(H)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .phase(phase),
    .cosine_coefficients(cosine_coefficients),
    .sine_coefficients(sine_coefficients),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result),
    .overflow(overflow)
);
endmodule


// ---------------- PRIVATE HELPERS ----------------
module fourier_series_helper_twiddle_rom #(parameter W=32,Q=20)(input [5:0] phase,
    output reg signed [W-1:0] real_part,imag_part);
    reg signed [63:0] r,i;
    always @*begin
        r=0;i=0;
        case(phase)
            6'd0:begin r=64'sd1073741824;i=64'sd0;end
            6'd1:begin r=64'sd1068571464;i=-64'sd105245103;end
            6'd2:begin r=64'sd1053110176;i=-64'sd209476638;end
            6'd3:begin r=64'sd1027506862;i=-64'sd311690799;end
            6'd4:begin r=64'sd992008094;i=-64'sd410903207;end
            6'd5:begin r=64'sd946955747;i=-64'sd506158392;end
            6'd6:begin r=64'sd892783698;i=-64'sd596538995;end
            6'd7:begin r=64'sd830013654;i=-64'sd681174602;end
            6'd8:begin r=64'sd759250125;i=-64'sd759250125;end
            6'd9:begin r=64'sd681174602;i=-64'sd830013654;end
            6'd10:begin r=64'sd596538995;i=-64'sd892783698;end
            6'd11:begin r=64'sd506158392;i=-64'sd946955747;end
            6'd12:begin r=64'sd410903207;i=-64'sd992008094;end
            6'd13:begin r=64'sd311690799;i=-64'sd1027506862;end
            6'd14:begin r=64'sd209476638;i=-64'sd1053110176;end

Numerical, Integer & Fixed-Pointعددی، صحیح و ممیز ثابت

Fixed-Point DFTDFT fixed-point dft_fixed

Direct complex DFT/IDFT; N divides 64; forward unnormalized, inverse /NDFT/IDFT مختلط مستقیم؛ N بر 64 بخش‌پذیر است؛ رفت بدون نرمال‌سازی، برگشت /N.

rtl/dft_fixed.v

Explanationتوضیح

Direct complex DFT/IDFT; N divides 64; forward unnormalized, inverse /N

The controller iterates the fixed-point/integer algorithm; results and flags are valid when done pulses. Check the overflow and out-of-range outputs.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus dft_fixed_controller and dft_fixed_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, Q, N, INVERSE

DFT/IDFT مختلط مستقیم؛ N بر 64 بخش‌پذیر است؛ رفت بدون نرمال‌سازی، برگشت /N.

کنترلر الگوریتم fixed-point/صحیح را تکرار می‌کند؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌ها وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند. خروجی‌های overflow و out_of_range را بررسی کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه dft_fixed_controller و dft_fixed_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, Q, N, INVERSE

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: real_in, imag_in"] --> M["dft_fixed"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: real_out, imag_out"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done (+1)"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/dft_fixed.v
// Complex direct DFT/IDFT. N must divide 64 (1,2,4,8,16,32,64).
// Same fixed-point scale on input/output; forward is unnormalized, inverse /N.
// One complex multiply-accumulate per cycle; W<=32,Q<=24,W>=Q+4.
// Accumulation uses 64 bits; choose signal headroom to avoid internal overflow.

// =====================================================================
// dft_fixed: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: saved_real <= real_in;
// control[1]: saved_imag <= imag_in;
// control[2]: bin <= 0;
// control[3]: sample <= 0;
// control[4]: sum_real <= 0;
// control[5]: sum_imag <= 0;
// control[6]: overflow <= 0;
// control[7]: real_out[(bin * W) +: W] <= clip(normalized_real);
// control[8]: imag_out[(bin * W) +: W] <= clip(normalized_imag);
// control[9]: overflow <= 1;
// control[10]: sample <= 0;
// control[11]: sum_real <= 0;
// control[12]: sum_imag <= 0;
// control[13]: bin <= bin + 1;
// control[14]: sum_real <= next_real;
// control[15]: sum_imag <= next_imag;
// control[16]: sample <= sample + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module dft_fixed_controller #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter INVERSE = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [5:0] status,
  output reg [16:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);
localparam signed [63:0] MAXIMUM = (64'sd1 << W - 1) - 1;
localparam signed [63:0] MINIMUM = -(64'sd1 << W - 1);
reg reporting;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg next_reporting;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  reporting <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  reporting <= next_reporting;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_reporting = reporting;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
      control[5] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
      control[6] = 1'b1;
      next_reporting = 0;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(reporting) begin
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else if(status[0]) begin
        control[7] = 1'b1;
        control[8] = 1'b1;
        if(status[1] || status[2] || status[3] || status[4]) control[9] = 1'b1;
        control[10] = 1'b1;
        control[11] = 1'b1;
        control[12] = 1'b1;
        if(status[5]) next_reporting = 1;
        else control[13] = 1'b1;
      end else begin
        control[14] = 1'b1;
        control[15] = 1'b1;
        control[16] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module dft_fixed_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter INVERSE = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] real_in,
  input [N*W-1:0] imag_in,
  input [16:0] control,
  output [5:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] real_out,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] imag_out,
  output reg overflow);
localparam signed [63:0] MAXIMUM = (64'sd1 << W - 1) - 1;
localparam signed [63:0] MINIMUM = -(64'sd1 << W - 1);
reg [N*W-1:0] saved_real;
reg [N*W-1:0] saved_imag;
integer bin;
integer sample;
integer k;
wire [5:0] phase;
assign phase = bin * sample % N * (64 / N);
wire signed [W-1:0] wr;
wire signed [W-1:0] wi_forward;
wire signed [W-1:0] wi;
assign wi = (INVERSE)? -wi_forward : wi_forward;
wire signed [W-1:0] xr;
assign xr = saved_real[(sample * W) +: W];
wire signed [W-1:0] xi;
assign xi = saved_imag[(sample * W) +: W];
reg signed [63:0] sum_real;
reg signed [63:0] sum_imag;
reg signed [63:0] next_real;
reg signed [63:0] next_imag;
reg signed [63:0] normalized_real;
reg signed [63:0] normalized_imag;
reg signed [127:0] pr;
reg signed [127:0] pi;

dft_fixed_helper_twiddle_rom
#(
  W,
  Q
)
table_rom
(
  phase,
  wr,
  wi_forward
);


function [W-1:0] clip;
  input signed [63:0] value;
  begin
    if(value > MAXIMUM) clip = MAXIMUM;
    else if(value < MINIMUM) clip = MINIMUM;
    else clip = value;
  end
endfunction


always @(*) begin
  pr = $signed(xr) * $signed(wr) - $signed(xi) * $signed(wi);
  pi = $signed(xr) * $signed(wi) + $signed(xi) * $signed(wr);
  next_real = sum_real + (pr >>> Q);
  next_imag = sum_imag + (pi >>> Q);
  normalized_real = (INVERSE)? next_real / N : next_real;
  normalized_imag = (INVERSE)? next_imag / N : next_imag;
end


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (sample == N - 1);
assign status[1] = (normalized_real > MAXIMUM);
assign status[2] = (normalized_real < MINIMUM);
assign status[3] = (normalized_imag > MAXIMUM);
assign status[4] = (normalized_imag < MINIMUM);
assign status[5] = (bin == N - 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  saved_real <= 0;
  saved_imag <= 0;
  real_out <= 0;
  imag_out <= 0;
  overflow <= 0;
  bin <= 0;
  sample <= 0;
  sum_real <= 0;
  sum_imag <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    saved_real <= real_in;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    saved_imag <= imag_in;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    bin <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    sample <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    sum_real <= 0;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    sum_imag <= 0;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    overflow <= 0;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    real_out[(bin * W) +: W] <= clip(normalized_real);
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    imag_out[(bin * W) +: W] <= clip(normalized_imag);
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    sample <= 0;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    sum_real <= 0;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    sum_imag <= 0;
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    bin <= bin + 1;
  end
  if(control[14]) begin
    sum_real <= next_real;
  end
  if(control[15]) begin
    sum_imag <= next_imag;
  end
  if(control[16]) begin
    sample <= sample + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module dft_fixed #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter INVERSE = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] real_in,
  input [N*W-1:0] imag_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] real_out,
  output [N*W-1:0] imag_out,
  output busy,
  output done,
  output overflow);
wire [16:0] control;
wire [5:0] status;
dft_fixed_controller #(.W(W), .Q(Q), .N(N), .INVERSE(INVERSE)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
dft_fixed_datapath #(.W(W), .Q(Q), .N(N), .INVERSE(INVERSE)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .real_in(real_in),
    .imag_in(imag_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .real_out(real_out),
    .imag_out(imag_out),
    .overflow(overflow)
);
endmodule


// ---------------- PRIVATE HELPERS ----------------
module dft_fixed_helper_twiddle_rom #(parameter W=32,Q=20)(input [5:0] phase,
    output reg signed [W-1:0] real_part,imag_part);
    reg signed [63:0] r,i;

        r=0;i=0;

Radix-2 FFTFFT رادیکس-۲ fft_radix2

Iterative radix-2 DIT FFT/IFFT; bit-reversed load, natural outputFFT/IFFT رادیکس-۲ DIT تکراری؛ بارگذاری بیت‌معکوس و خروجی طبیعی.

rtl/fft_radix2.v

Explanationتوضیح

Iterative radix-2 DIT FFT/IFFT; bit-reversed load, natural output

The controller iterates the fixed-point/integer algorithm; results and flags are valid when done pulses. Check the overflow and out-of-range outputs.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus fft_radix2_controller and fft_radix2_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, Q, N, INVERSE

FFT/IFFT رادیکس-۲ DIT تکراری؛ بارگذاری بیت‌معکوس و خروجی طبیعی.

کنترلر الگوریتم fixed-point/صحیح را تکرار می‌کند؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌ها وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند. خروجی‌های overflow و out_of_range را بررسی کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه fft_radix2_controller و fft_radix2_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, Q, N, INVERSE

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: real_in, imag_in"] --> M["fft_radix2"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: real_out, imag_out"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done (+1)"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/fft_radix2.v
// Iterative radix-2 DIT FFT/IFFT; bit-reversed input loading, natural output.
// N in {1,2,4,8,16,32,64}; one butterfly per cycle; fixed-point W/Q.
// Forward is unnormalized. Inverse divides the final result by N.
// Intermediate butterfly overflow SATURATES and sets sticky overflow; inputs
// need sufficient headroom because inverse normalization happens at the end.

// =====================================================================
// fft_radix2: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) begin re[reversed(k)] <= real_in[(k * W) +: W]; im[reversed(k)] <= imag_in[(k * W) +: W]; end
// control[1]: span <= 2;
// control[2]: base <= 0;
// control[3]: j <= 0;
// control[4]: overflow <= 0;
// control[5]: for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) begin real_out[(k * W) +: W] <= (INVERSE)? $signed(re[k]) / N : re[k]; imag_out[(k * W) +: W] <= (INVERSE)? $signed(im[k]) / N : im[k]; end
// control[6]: re[upper] <= clip(ar);
// control[7]: im[upper] <= clip(ai);
// control[8]: re[lower] <= clip(br);
// control[9]: im[lower] <= clip(bi);
// control[10]: overflow <= 1;
// control[11]: j <= 0;
// control[12]: base <= 0;
// control[13]: span <= span * 2;
// control[14]: base <= base + span;
// control[15]: j <= j + 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module fft_radix2_controller #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter INVERSE = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [10:0] status,
  output reg [15:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);
localparam LOGN = (N > 1)? $clog2(N) : 0;
localparam signed [63:0] MAXIMUM = (64'sd1 << W - 1) - 1;
localparam signed [63:0] MINIMUM = -(64'sd1 << W - 1);
reg reporting;
reg next_busy;
reg next_done;
reg next_reporting;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
  reporting <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
  reporting <= next_reporting;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  next_reporting = reporting;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_reporting = N == 1;
      next_busy = 1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(reporting) begin
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else begin
        control[6] = 1'b1;
        control[7] = 1'b1;
        control[8] = 1'b1;
        control[9] = 1'b1;
        if(status[0] || status[1] || status[2] || status[3] || status[4] || status[5] || status[6] || status[7]) control[10] = 1'b1;
        if(status[8]) begin
          control[11] = 1'b1;
          if(status[9]) begin
            control[12] = 1'b1;
            if(status[10]) next_reporting = 1;
            else control[13] = 1'b1;
          end else control[14] = 1'b1;
        end else control[15] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module fft_radix2_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter INVERSE = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [N*W-1:0] real_in,
  input [N*W-1:0] imag_in,
  input [15:0] control,
  output [10:0] status,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] real_out,
  output reg [N*W-1:0] imag_out,
  output reg overflow);
localparam LOGN = (N > 1)? $clog2(N) : 0;
localparam signed [63:0] MAXIMUM = (64'sd1 << W - 1) - 1;
localparam signed [63:0] MINIMUM = -(64'sd1 << W - 1);
reg signed [W-1:0] re [0:N-1];
reg signed [W-1:0] im [0:N-1];
integer span;
integer base;
integer j;
integer k;
integer upper;
integer lower;
wire [5:0] phase;
assign phase = j * (64 / span);
wire signed [W-1:0] wr;
wire signed [W-1:0] wi_forward;
wire signed [W-1:0] wi;
assign wi = (INVERSE)? -wi_forward : wi_forward;
reg signed [127:0] pr;
reg signed [127:0] pi;
reg signed [63:0] tr;
reg signed [63:0] ti;
reg signed [63:0] ar;
reg signed [63:0] ai;
reg signed [63:0] br;
reg signed [63:0] bi;

fft_radix2_helper_twiddle_rom
#(
  W,
  Q
)
table_rom
(
  phase,
  wr,
  wi_forward
);


function [31:0] reversed;
  input [31:0] value;
  integer bit_index;
  begin
    reversed = 0;
    for(bit_index=0; bit_index<LOGN; bit_index=bit_index+1) reversed = (reversed << 1) | (value >> bit_index) & 1;
  end
endfunction


function [W-1:0] clip;
  input signed [63:0] value;
  begin
    if(value > MAXIMUM) clip = MAXIMUM;
    else if(value < MINIMUM) clip = MINIMUM;
    else clip = value;
  end
endfunction


always @(*) begin
  upper = base + j;
  lower = base + j + span / 2;
  pr = 0;
  pi = 0;
  tr = 0;
  ti = 0;
  ar = 0;
  ai = 0;
  br = 0;
  bi = 0;
  if((upper < N) && (lower < N)) begin
    pr = $signed(re[lower]) * $signed(wr) - $signed(im[lower]) * $signed(wi);
    pi = $signed(re[lower]) * $signed(wi) + $signed(im[lower]) * $signed(wr);
    tr = pr >>> Q;
    ti = pi >>> Q;
    ar = $signed(re[upper]) + tr;
    ai = $signed(im[upper]) + ti;
    br = $signed(re[upper]) - tr;
    bi = $signed(im[upper]) - ti;
  end
end


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (ar > MAXIMUM);
assign status[1] = (ar < MINIMUM);
assign status[2] = (ai > MAXIMUM);
assign status[3] = (ai < MINIMUM);
assign status[4] = (br > MAXIMUM);
assign status[5] = (br < MINIMUM);
assign status[6] = (bi > MAXIMUM);
assign status[7] = (bi < MINIMUM);
assign status[8] = (j == span / 2 - 1);
assign status[9] = (base + span >= N);
assign status[10] = (span == N);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  real_out <= 0;
  imag_out <= 0;
  overflow <= 0;
  span <= 2;
  base <= 0;
  j <= 0;
  for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) begin
    re[k] <= 0;
    im[k] <= 0;
  end
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) begin
      re[reversed(k)] <= real_in[(k * W) +: W];
      im[reversed(k)] <= imag_in[(k * W) +: W];
    end
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    span <= 2;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    base <= 0;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    j <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    overflow <= 0;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    for(k=0; k<N; k=k+1) begin
      real_out[(k * W) +: W] <= (INVERSE)? $signed(re[k]) / N : re[k];
      imag_out[(k * W) +: W] <= (INVERSE)? $signed(im[k]) / N : im[k];
    end
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    re[upper] <= clip(ar);
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    im[upper] <= clip(ai);
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    re[lower] <= clip(br);
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    im[lower] <= clip(bi);
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    j <= 0;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    base <= 0;
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    span <= span * 2;
  end
  if(control[14]) begin
    base <= base + span;
  end
  if(control[15]) begin
    j <= j + 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module fft_radix2 #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter Q = 20,
  parameter N = 8,
  parameter INVERSE = 0
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [N*W-1:0] real_in,
  input [N*W-1:0] imag_in,
  output [N*W-1:0] real_out,
  output [N*W-1:0] imag_out,
  output busy,
  output done,
  output overflow);
wire [15:0] control;
wire [10:0] status;
fft_radix2_controller #(.W(W), .Q(Q), .N(N), .INVERSE(INVERSE)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
fft_radix2_datapath #(.W(W), .Q(Q), .N(N), .INVERSE(INVERSE)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .real_in(real_in),
    .imag_in(imag_in),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .real_out(real_out),
    .imag_out(imag_out),
    .overflow(overflow)
);
endmodule


// ---------------- PRIVATE HELPERS ----------------
module fft_radix2_helper_twiddle_rom #(parameter W=32,Q=20)(input [5:0] phase,
    output reg signed [W-1:0] real_part,imag_part);
    reg signed [63:0] r,i;
    always @*begin
        r=0;i=0;

Twiddle ROMROM ضرایب twiddle twiddle_rom

rtl/twiddle_rom.v

Explanationتوضیح

Constant exp(-j*2*pi*phase/64) coefficients, Q30 rescaled to Q

The controller iterates the fixed-point/integer algorithm; results and flags are valid when done pulses. Check the overflow and out-of-range outputs.

  • Source structure: This file contains a single module.
  • Reset: No reset
  • Handshake: No start/busy/done handshake; drive the control inputs directly.
  • Parameters: W, Q

ضرایب ثابت exp(-j*2*pi*phase/64) با Q30 و مقیاس Q.

کنترلر الگوریتم fixed-point/صحیح را تکرار می‌کند؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌ها وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند. خروجی‌های overflow و out_of_range را بررسی کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل فقط یک ماژول دارد.
  • ریست: بدون ریست
  • Handshake: handshake start/busy/done ندارد؛ ورودی‌های کنترل را مستقیم بدهید.
  • پارامترها: W, Q

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: phase"] --> M["twiddle_rom"]
    M --> OUT["data out: real_part, imag_part"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

This module is purely combinational; it has no state and therefore no FSM. It is a datapath operator controlled by signals from the surrounding controller.

این ماژول کاملاً ترکیبی است؛ حالت ندارد و بنابراین FSM ندارد. یک عملگر دیتاپدیت است که با سیگنال‌های کنترلر اطراف کنترل می‌شود.

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/twiddle_rom.v
// exp(-j*2*pi*phase/64), constant Q30 coefficients rescaled to Q.
// 8<=W<=32, 4<=Q<=24, W>=Q+2. No runtime real arithmetic.
module twiddle_rom #(parameter W=32,Q=20)(input [5:0] phase,
    output reg signed [W-1:0] real_part,imag_part);
    reg signed [63:0] r,i;
    always @*begin
        r=0;i=0;
        case(phase)
            6'd0:begin r=64'sd1073741824;i=64'sd0;end
            6'd1:begin r=64'sd1068571464;i=-64'sd105245103;end
            6'd2:begin r=64'sd1053110176;i=-64'sd209476638;end
            6'd3:begin r=64'sd1027506862;i=-64'sd311690799;end
            6'd4:begin r=64'sd992008094;i=-64'sd410903207;end
            6'd5:begin r=64'sd946955747;i=-64'sd506158392;end
            6'd6:begin r=64'sd892783698;i=-64'sd596538995;end
            6'd7:begin r=64'sd830013654;i=-64'sd681174602;end
            6'd8:begin r=64'sd759250125;i=-64'sd759250125;end
            6'd9:begin r=64'sd681174602;i=-64'sd830013654;end
            6'd10:begin r=64'sd596538995;i=-64'sd892783698;end
            6'd11:begin r=64'sd506158392;i=-64'sd946955747;end
            6'd12:begin r=64'sd410903207;i=-64'sd992008094;end
            6'd13:begin r=64'sd311690799;i=-64'sd1027506862;end
            6'd14:begin r=64'sd209476638;i=-64'sd1053110176;end
            6'd15:begin r=64'sd105245103;i=-64'sd1068571464;end

Numerical, Integer & Fixed-Pointعددی، صحیح و ممیز ثابت

Integer Powerتوان عدد صحیح integer_power

Exponentiation by squaring, modulo 2W; defines a0=1توان‌رسانی با مربع‌سازی، پیمانهٔ 2**W؛ a**0=1.

rtl/integer_power.v

Explanationتوضیح

Exponentiation by squaring, modulo 2W; defines a0=1

The controller iterates the fixed-point/integer algorithm; results and flags are valid when done pulses. Check the overflow and out-of-range outputs.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus integer_power_controller and integer_power_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, E

توان‌رسانی با مربع‌سازی، پیمانهٔ 2**W؛ a**0=1.

کنترلر الگوریتم fixed-point/صحیح را تکرار می‌کند؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌ها وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند. خروجی‌های overflow و out_of_range را بررسی کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه integer_power_controller و integer_power_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, E

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: a, exponent"] --> M["integer_power"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: result"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

E8Exponent widthعرض توان

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/integer_power.v
// Exponentiation by squaring, modulo 2**W. Defines a**0=1, including 0**0.

// =====================================================================
// integer_power: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: base <= a;
// control[1]: total <= 1;
// control[2]: power_left <= exponent;
// control[3]: result <= total;
// control[4]: total <= total * base;
// control[5]: base <= base * base;
// control[6]: power_left <= power_left >> 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module integer_power_controller #
(
  parameter W = 16,
  parameter E = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [1:0] status,
  output reg [6:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(status[0]) begin
        control[3] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else begin
        if(status[1]) control[4] = 1'b1;
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        control[6] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module integer_power_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 16,
  parameter E = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [W-1:0] a,
  input [E-1:0] exponent,
  input [6:0] control,
  output [1:0] status,
  output reg [W-1:0] result);
reg [W-1:0] base;
reg [W-1:0] total;
reg [E-1:0] power_left;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (power_left == 0);
assign status[1] = power_left[0];

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  result <= 0;
  base <= 0;
  total <= 0;
  power_left <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    base <= a;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    total <= 1;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    power_left <= exponent;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    result <= total;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    total <= total * base;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    base <= base * base;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    power_left <= power_left >> 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module integer_power #
(
  parameter W = 16,
  parameter E = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [W-1:0] a,
  input [E-1:0] exponent,
  output [W-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [6:0] control;
wire [1:0] status;
integer_power_controller #(.W(W), .E(E)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
integer_power_datapath #(.W(W), .E(E)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .a(a),
    .exponent(exponent),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result)
);
endmodule

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/integer_sqrt.v
// Floor(sqrt(x)) by trial bits, one candidate square per cycle.

// =====================================================================
// integer_sqrt: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: value <= x;
// control[1]: root <= 0;
// control[2]: bit_index <= (W - 1) / 2;
// control[3]: root <= candidate;
// control[4]: result <= (square <= value)? candidate : root;
// control[5]: bit_index <= bit_index - 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module integer_sqrt_controller #
(
  parameter W = 32
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [1:0] status,
  output reg [5:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(status[0]) control[3] = 1'b1;
      if(status[1]) begin
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else control[5] = 1'b1;
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module integer_sqrt_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 32
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [W-1:0] x,
  input [5:0] control,
  output [1:0] status,
  output reg [W-1:0] result);
reg [W-1:0] value;
reg [W-1:0] root;
integer bit_index;
wire [W-1:0] candidate;
assign candidate = root | ({ { W - 1{ 1'b0 } }, 1'b1 } << bit_index);
wire [2*W-1:0] square;
assign square = candidate * candidate;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (square <= value);
assign status[1] = (bit_index == 0);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  result <= 0;
  value <= 0;
  root <= 0;
  bit_index <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    value <= x;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    root <= 0;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    bit_index <= (W - 1) / 2;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    root <= candidate;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    result <= (square <= value)? candidate : root;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    bit_index <= bit_index - 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module integer_sqrt #
(
  parameter W = 32
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [W-1:0] x,
  output [W-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [5:0] control;
wire [1:0] status;
integer_sqrt_controller #(.W(W)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
integer_sqrt_datapath #(.W(W)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .x(x),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result)
);
endmodule

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/factorial.v
// Unsigned n!; wraps modulo 2**W and reports any overflow. 0! = 1.

// =====================================================================
// factorial: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: total <= 1;
// control[1]: limit <= n;
// control[2]: p <= 1;
// control[3]: overflow <= 0;
// control[4]: result <= total;
// control[5]: total <= product[W-1:0];
// control[6]: overflow <= 1;
// control[7]: p <= p + 1'b1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module factorial_controller #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter B = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [1:0] status,
  output reg [7:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(status[0]) begin
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else begin
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        if(status[1]) control[6] = 1'b1;
        control[7] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module factorial_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter B = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [B-1:0] n,
  input [7:0] control,
  output [1:0] status,
  output reg [W-1:0] result,
  output reg overflow);
reg [W-1:0] total;
reg [B-1:0] limit;
reg [B:0] p;
wire [W+B:0] product;
assign product = total * p;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (p > limit);
assign status[1] = (|product[W+B:W]);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  result <= 0;
  overflow <= 0;
  total <= 1;
  limit <= 0;
  p <= 1;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    total <= 1;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    limit <= n;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    p <= 1;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    overflow <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    result <= total;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    total <= product[W-1:0];
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    p <= p + 1'b1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module factorial #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter B = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [B-1:0] n,
  output [W-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done,
  output overflow);
wire [7:0] control;
wire [1:0] status;
factorial_controller #(.W(W), .B(B)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
factorial_datapath #(.W(W), .B(B)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .n(n),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result),
    .overflow(overflow)
);
endmodule

Numerical, Integer & Fixed-Pointعددی، صحیح و ممیز ثابت

Fibonacciفیبوناچی fibonacci

F(0)=0, F(1)=1; result wraps modulo 2**W; sticky overflowF(0)=0، F(1)=1؛ نتیجه پیمانهٔ 2**W و overflow چسبنده.

rtl/fibonacci.v

Explanationتوضیح

F(0)=0, F(1)=1; result wraps modulo 2**W; sticky overflow

The controller iterates the fixed-point/integer algorithm; results and flags are valid when done pulses. Check the overflow and out-of-range outputs.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus fibonacci_controller and fibonacci_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W, B

F(0)=0، F(1)=1؛ نتیجه پیمانهٔ 2**W و overflow چسبنده.

کنترلر الگوریتم fixed-point/صحیح را تکرار می‌کند؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌ها وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند. خروجی‌های overflow و out_of_range را بررسی کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه fibonacci_controller و fibonacci_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W, B

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: n"] --> M["fibonacci"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: result"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done (+1)"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح
W32Data widthعرض داده
B8Input index widthعرض اندیس ورودی

Portsپورت‌ها
