RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/fibonacci.v
// F(0)=0,F(1)=1. Unsigned result wraps modulo 2**W; overflow is sticky.

// =====================================================================
// fibonacci: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: a <= 0;
// control[1]: b <= 1;
// control[2]: limit <= n;
// control[3]: p <= 0;
// control[4]: overflow <= 0;
// control[5]: result <= a;
// control[6]: a <= b;
// control[7]: b <= sum[W-1:0];
// control[8]: p <= p + 1'b1;
// control[9]: overflow <= 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module fibonacci_controller #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter B = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [2:0] status,
  output reg [9:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
      control[4] = 1'b1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(status[0]) begin
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else begin
        control[6] = 1'b1;
        control[7] = 1'b1;
        control[8] = 1'b1;
        if(status[1] && status[2]) control[9] = 1'b1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module fibonacci_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter B = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [B-1:0] n,
  input [9:0] control,
  output [2:0] status,
  output reg [W-1:0] result,
  output reg overflow);
reg [W-1:0] a;
reg [W-1:0] b;
reg [B-1:0] limit;
reg [B-1:0] p;
wire [W:0] sum;
assign sum = { 1'b0, a } + { 1'b0, b };

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (p == limit);
assign status[1] = sum[W];
assign status[2] = (p + 1 < limit);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  result <= 0;
  overflow <= 0;
  a <= 0;
  b <= 1;
  limit <= 0;
  p <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    a <= 0;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    b <= 1;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    limit <= n;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    p <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    overflow <= 0;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    result <= a;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    a <= b;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    b <= sum[W-1:0];
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    p <= p + 1'b1;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    overflow <= 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module fibonacci #
(
  parameter W = 32,
  parameter B = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [B-1:0] n,
  output [W-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done,
  output overflow);
wire [9:0] control;
wire [2:0] status;
fibonacci_controller #(.W(W), .B(B)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
fibonacci_datapath #(.W(W), .B(B)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .n(n),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result),
    .overflow(overflow)
);
endmodule

Horner Polynomialچندجمله‌ای هورنر polynomial_horner

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/polynomial_horner.v
// Integer polynomial c[0] + c[1]*x + ... + c[N-1]*x**(N-1).
// Horner evaluation, one coefficient per cycle. Arithmetic wraps modulo 2**W.

// =====================================================================
// polynomial_horner: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: saved_x <= x;
// control[1]: coeff <= coefficients;
// control[2]: p <= N - 1;
// control[3]: total <= 0;
// control[4]: total <= total * saved_x + coeff[(p * W) +: W];
// control[5]: result <= total * saved_x + coeff[(p * W) +: W];
// control[6]: p <= p - 1;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module polynomial_horner_controller #
(
  parameter W = 16,
  parameter N = 4
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [0:0] status,
  output reg [6:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      control[4] = 1'b1;
      if(status[0]) begin
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else control[6] = 1'b1;
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module polynomial_horner_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 16,
  parameter N = 4
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [W-1:0] x,
  input [N*W-1:0] coefficients,
  input [6:0] control,
  output [0:0] status,
  output reg [W-1:0] result);
reg [W-1:0] saved_x;
reg [W-1:0] total;
reg [N*W-1:0] coeff;
integer p;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (p == 0);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  result <= 0;
  saved_x <= 0;
  total <= 0;
  coeff <= 0;
  p <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    saved_x <= x;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    coeff <= coefficients;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    p <= N - 1;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    total <= 0;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    total <= total * saved_x + coeff[(p * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    result <= total * saved_x + coeff[(p * W) +: W];
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    p <= p - 1;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module polynomial_horner #
(
  parameter W = 16,
  parameter N = 4
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [W-1:0] x,
  input [N*W-1:0] coefficients,
  output [W-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [6:0] control;
wire [0:0] status;
polynomial_horner_controller #(.W(W), .N(N)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
polynomial_horner_datapath #(.W(W), .N(N)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .x(x),
    .coefficients(coefficients),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result)
);
endmodule

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/gcd.v
// Module: gcd. Original group: algorithms.
// Euclidean subtraction GCD: one subtraction per cycle, no divider inferred.

// =====================================================================
// gcd: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: x <= a;
// control[1]: y <= b;
// control[2]: result <= (x == 0)? y : x;
// control[3]: x <= x - y;
// control[4]: y <= y - x;

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module gcd_controller #
(
  parameter W = 16
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [3:0] status,
  output reg [4:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(status[0] || status[1] || status[2]) begin
        control[2] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end else if(status[3]) control[3] = 1'b1;
      else control[4] = 1'b1;
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module gcd_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 16
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [W-1:0] a,
  input [W-1:0] b,
  input [4:0] control,
  output [3:0] status,
  output reg [W-1:0] result);
reg [W-1:0] x;
reg [W-1:0] y;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (x == 0);
assign status[1] = (y == 0);
assign status[2] = (x == y);
assign status[3] = (x > y);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  x <= 0;
  y <= 0;
  result <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    x <= a;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    y <= b;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    result <= (x == 0)? y : x;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    x <= x - y;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    y <= y - x;
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module gcd #
(
  parameter W = 16
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [W-1:0] a,
  input [W-1:0] b,
  output [W-1:0] result,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [4:0] control;
wire [3:0] status;
gcd_controller #(.W(W)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
gcd_datapath #(.W(W)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .a(a),
    .b(b),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .result(result)
);
endmodule

Numerical, Integer & Fixed-Pointعددی، صحیح و ممیز ثابت

Iterative Dividerتقسیم‌کنندهٔ تکراری iterative_divider

W-step restoring divider; divide-by-zero completes with quotient all onesتقسیم‌کنندهٔ بازگردانندهٔ W مرحله‌ای؛ تقسیم بر صفر با خارج‌قسمت همه‌یک کامل می‌شود.

rtl/iterative_divider.v

Explanationتوضیح

W-step restoring divider; divide-by-zero completes with quotient all ones

The controller iterates the fixed-point/integer algorithm; results and flags are valid when done pulses. Check the overflow and out-of-range outputs.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus iterative_divider_controller and iterative_divider_datapath; the top wires the control/status bus between them.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Assert start for at least one clock; busy stays high while the operation runs; done pulses for one cycle when the result is valid.
  • Parameters: W

تقسیم‌کنندهٔ بازگردانندهٔ W مرحله‌ای؛ تقسیم بر صفر با خارج‌قسمت همه‌یک کامل می‌شود.

کنترلر الگوریتم fixed-point/صحیح را تکرار می‌کند؛ نتیجه و فلگ‌ها وقتی done پالس می‌زند معتبرند. خروجی‌های overflow و out_of_range را بررسی کنید.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه iterative_divider_controller و iterative_divider_datapath است؛ top باس کنترل/وضعیت را بین آن‌ها وصل می‌کند.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: start را حداقل یک کلاک فعال کنید؛ busy در طول اجرا بالا می‌ماند و done وقتی نتیجه معتبر است یک پالس می‌دهد.
  • پارامترها: W

flowchart LR

    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: quotient, remainder"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: busy, done (+1)"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The controller waits in IDLE for start, runs the datapath while busy is high, and pulses done when the result is valid. start is ignored while busy.
کنترلر در IDLE منتظر start می‌ماند، دیتاپدیت را تا وقتی busy بالاست اجرا می‌کند و وقتی نتیجه معتبر شد done را پالس می‌دهد. در زمان busy، start نادیده گرفته می‌شود.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> IDLE
    IDLE --> RUN: start
    RUN --> DONE: complete
    DONE --> IDLE: done

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/iterative_divider.v
// Module: iterative_divider. Original group: algorithms.
// W restoring steps. /0 completes immediately with quotient all ones.

// =====================================================================
// iterative_divider: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: divide_by_zero <= b == 0;
// control[1]: quotient <= { W{ 1'b1 } };
// control[2]: remainder <= a;
// control[3]: dividend <= a;
// control[4]: divisor <= b;
// control[5]: q <= 0;
// control[6]: rem_reg <= 0;
// control[7]: left <= W;
// control[8]: dividend <= dividend << 1;
// control[9]: q <= next_q;
// control[10]: rem_reg <= next_rem;
// control[11]: left <= left - 1;
// control[12]: quotient <= next_q;
// control[13]: remainder <= next_rem[W-1:0];

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module iterative_divider_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [1:0] status,
  output reg [13:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      if(status[0]) begin
        control[1] = 1'b1;
        control[2] = 1'b1;
        next_done = 1;
      end else begin
        control[3] = 1'b1;
        control[4] = 1'b1;
        control[5] = 1'b1;
        control[6] = 1'b1;
        control[7] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 1;
      end
    end else if(busy) begin
      control[8] = 1'b1;
      control[9] = 1'b1;
      control[10] = 1'b1;
      control[11] = 1'b1;
      if(status[1]) begin
        control[12] = 1'b1;
        control[13] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module iterative_divider_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [W-1:0] a,
  input [W-1:0] b,
  input [13:0] control,
  output [1:0] status,
  output reg [W-1:0] quotient,
  output reg [W-1:0] remainder,
  output reg divide_by_zero);
reg [W-1:0] dividend;
reg [W-1:0] divisor;
reg [W-1:0] q;
reg [W:0] rem_reg;
reg [W:0] next_rem;
reg [W-1:0] next_q;
integer left;

always @(*) begin
  next_rem = { rem_reg[W-1:0], dividend[W - 1] };
  next_q = q << 1;
  if(next_rem >= { 1'b0, divisor }) begin
    next_rem = next_rem - divisor;
    next_q[0] = 1;
  end
end


// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = (b == 0);
assign status[1] = (left == 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  quotient <= 0;
  remainder <= 0;
  divide_by_zero <= 0;
  dividend <= 0;
  divisor <= 0;
  q <= 0;
  rem_reg <= 0;
  left <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    divide_by_zero <= b == 0;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    quotient <= { W{ 1'b1 } };
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    remainder <= a;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    dividend <= a;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    divisor <= b;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    q <= 0;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    rem_reg <= 0;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    left <= W;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    dividend <= dividend << 1;
  end
  if(control[9]) begin
    q <= next_q;
  end
  if(control[10]) begin
    rem_reg <= next_rem;
  end
  if(control[11]) begin
    left <= left - 1;
  end
  if(control[12]) begin
    quotient <= next_q;
  end
  if(control[13]) begin
    remainder <= next_rem[W-1:0];
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module iterative_divider #
(
  parameter W = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [W-1:0] a,
  input [W-1:0] b,
  output [W-1:0] quotient,
  output [W-1:0] remainder,
  output busy,
  output done,
  output divide_by_zero);
wire [13:0] control;
wire [1:0] status;
iterative_divider_controller #(.W(W)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
iterative_divider_datapath #(.W(W)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .a(a),
    .b(b),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .quotient(quotient),
    .remainder(remainder),
    .divide_by_zero(divide_by_zero)
);
endmodule

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/shift_add_multiplier.v
// Module: shift_add_multiplier. Original group: algorithms.

// =====================================================================
// shift_add_multiplier: complete controller + datapath + top in one Verilog file.
// Pulse start while busy=0. Inputs are latched on the accepting edge.
// Reset and port conventions are preserved from the original module.
// Packed array lane i occupies bits [i*W +: W], unless stated otherwise.
// =====================================================================
// control[0]: acc <= 0;
// control[1]: multiplicand <= a;
// control[2]: multiplier <= b;
// control[3]: bits_left <= W;
// control[4]: acc <= acc + multiplicand;
// control[5]: multiplicand <= multiplicand << 1;
// control[6]: multiplier <= multiplier >> 1;
// control[7]: bits_left <= bits_left - 1;
// control[8]: product <= acc + ((multiplier[0])? multiplicand : { 2 * W{ 1'b0 } });

// ---------------- CONTROLLER ----------------
module shift_add_multiplier_controller #
(
  parameter W = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [1:0] status,
  output reg [8:0] control,
  output reg busy,
  output reg done);


reg next_busy;
reg next_done;

// FSM state/handshake registers; all data writes are control strobes.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  busy <= 0;
  done <= 0;
end else begin
  busy <= next_busy;
  done <= next_done;
end


always @(*) begin
  next_busy = busy;
  next_done = done;
  control = 0;
  begin
    next_done = 0;
    if(start && !busy) begin
      control[0] = 1'b1;
      control[1] = 1'b1;
      control[2] = 1'b1;
      control[3] = 1'b1;
      next_busy = 1;
    end else if(busy) begin
      if(status[0]) control[4] = 1'b1;
      control[5] = 1'b1;
      control[6] = 1'b1;
      control[7] = 1'b1;
      if(status[1]) begin
        control[8] = 1'b1;
        next_busy = 0;
        next_done = 1;
      end
    end
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- DATAPATH ----------------
module shift_add_multiplier_datapath #
(
  parameter W = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input [W-1:0] a,
  input [W-1:0] b,
  input [8:0] control,
  output [1:0] status,
  output reg [2*W-1:0] product);
reg [2*W-1:0] acc;
reg [2*W-1:0] multiplicand;
reg [W-1:0] multiplier;
integer bits_left;

// Status predicates are generated by the datapath, never by the FSM.
assign status[0] = multiplier[0];
assign status[1] = (bits_left == 1);

// Register-transfer operations. Later strobes retain nonblocking
// assignment priority when the same destination is written twice.

always @(posedge clk) if(!rst_n) begin
  acc <= 0;
  multiplicand <= 0;
  multiplier <= 0;
  product <= 0;
  bits_left <= 0;
end else begin
  if(control[0]) begin
    acc <= 0;
  end
  if(control[1]) begin
    multiplicand <= a;
  end
  if(control[2]) begin
    multiplier <= b;
  end
  if(control[3]) begin
    bits_left <= W;
  end
  if(control[4]) begin
    acc <= acc + multiplicand;
  end
  if(control[5]) begin
    multiplicand <= multiplicand << 1;
  end
  if(control[6]) begin
    multiplier <= multiplier >> 1;
  end
  if(control[7]) begin
    bits_left <= bits_left - 1;
  end
  if(control[8]) begin
    product <= acc + ((multiplier[0])? multiplicand : { 2 * W{ 1'b0 } });
  end
end

endmodule

// ---------------- TOP ----------------
module shift_add_multiplier #
(
  parameter W = 8
)
(
  input clk,
  input rst_n,
  input start,
  input [W-1:0] a,
  input [W-1:0] b,
  output [2*W-1:0] product,
  output busy,
  output done);
wire [8:0] control;
wire [1:0] status;
shift_add_multiplier_controller #(.W(W)) controller (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .start(start),
    .status(status),
    .control(control),
    .busy(busy),
    .done(done)
);
shift_add_multiplier_datapath #(.W(W)) datapath (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .a(a),
    .b(b),
    .control(control),
    .status(status),
    .product(product)
);
endmodule

Floating Pointممیز شناور

Floating-Point Arithmeticحساب ممیز شناور fp_arithmetic

Combinational binary FP add/sub/mul/div; E/F parameters; round-to-nearest-even; subnormal/Inf/NaNجمع/تفریق/ضرب/تقسیم FP دودویی ترکیبی؛ E/F، گرد به نزدیک‌ترین زوج، subnormal/Inf/NaN.

rtl/fp_arithmetic.v

Explanationتوضیح

Combinational binary FP add/sub/mul/div; E/F parameters; round-to-nearest-even; subnormal/Inf/NaN

IEEE-754-style floating-point datapath with parameterized exponent/fraction widths; special values are handled explicitly.

  • Source structure: This file contains a single module.
  • Reset: No reset
  • Handshake: No start/busy/done handshake; drive the control inputs directly.
  • Parameters: E, F, OP, W

جمع/تفریق/ضرب/تقسیم FP دودویی ترکیبی؛ E/F، گرد به نزدیک‌ترین زوج، subnormal/Inf/NaN.

دیتاپدیت ممیز شناور به سبک IEEE-754 با عرض‌های پارامتریک توان/کسر؛ مقادیر خاص به‌صورت صریح مدیریت می‌شوند.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل فقط یک ماژول دارد.
  • ریست: بدون ریست
  • Handshake: handshake start/busy/done ندارد؛ ورودی‌های کنترل را مستقیم بدهید.
  • پارامترها: E, F, OP, W

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: a, b"] --> M["fp_arithmetic"]
    M --> OUT["data out: result"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

This module is purely combinational; it has no state and therefore no FSM. It is a datapath operator controlled by signals from the surrounding controller.

این ماژول کاملاً ترکیبی است؛ حالت ندارد و بنابراین FSM ندارد. یک عملگر دیتاپدیت است که با سیگنال‌های کنترلر اطراف کنترل می‌شود.

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح
E5Exponent widthعرض توان
F10Fraction widthعرض کسر
OP0Operation select (0=add, 1=sub, 2=mul, 3=div)انتخاب عملیات (۰=جمع، ۱=تفریق، ۲=ضرب، ۳=تقسیم)
W1+E+FData widthعرض داده

Portsپورت‌ها

DirجهتNameنامWidthعرضDescriptionتوضیح

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/fp_arithmetic.v
// Module: fp_arithmetic. Original group: floating_point.
// Educational synthesizable binary floating-point arithmetic.
// Supported parameter range: 3 <= E <= 8, 2 <= F <= 23 (binary16/binary32).
// Round-to-nearest, ties-to-even; gradual underflow; canonical quiet NaN.
// No exception flags or alternate rounding modes. Arithmetic is combinational;
// division uses / and % on integer significands and can infer a large circuit.
module fp_arithmetic #(parameter E=5,F=10,OP=0,W=1+E+F)(
    input [W-1:0] a,b,output reg [W-1:0] result
);
    localparam BIAS=(1<<(E-1))-1;
    localparam EMAX=(1<<E)-1;
    localparam [W-1:0] QNAN=((64'd1*((1<<E)-1))<<F) | (64'd1<<(F-1));
    reg sa,sb,sign_out,na,nb,ia,ib,za,zb;
    integer ea,eb,xa,xb,common_exp,diff,n;
    reg [63:0] ma,mb,va,vb,magnitude,quotient,remainder;

    // Right shift with sticky bit; preserves all discarded information needed
    // by the final rounding operation when at least three guard bits remain.
    function [63:0] shift_jam;
        input [63:0] value;input integer amount;
        reg sticky;
        begin
            if(amount<=0) shift_jam=value;
            else if(amount>=64) shift_jam=(value!=0);
            else begin sticky=((value & ((64'd1<<amount)-1))!=0);
                shift_jam=(value>>amount)|sticky;
            end
        end
    endfunction

    // Pack integer magnitude * 2**exp2, including subnormal rounding.
    function [W-1:0] pack;
        input sign;input [63:0] mag;input integer exp2;
        integer msb,i,unbiased,shift,target_exp;
        reg [63:0] rounded,discarded,half;
        reg [E-1:0] encoded;
        begin
            pack={sign,{(E+F){1'b0}}}; msb=-1;rounded=0;discarded=0;half=0;encoded=0;
            for(i=0;i<64;i=i+1) if(mag[i]) msb=i;
            if(msb>=0) begin
                unbiased=msb+exp2;
                // Subnormal values use a fixed quantum 2**(1-BIAS-F).
                target_exp=(unbiased<1-BIAS)?(1-BIAS):unbiased;
                shift=target_exp-F-exp2;
                if(shift>64) rounded=0;
                else if(shift==64) begin
                    rounded=(mag>64'h8000000000000000)?1:0;
                end else if(shift>0) begin
                    rounded=mag>>shift;
                    discarded=mag & ((64'd1<<shift)-1);
                    half=64'd1<<(shift-1);
                    if(discarded>half || (discarded==half && rounded[0])) rounded=rounded+1;
                end else rounded=mag<<(-shift);
                if(rounded>=(64'd1<<(F+1))) begin rounded=rounded>>1;target_exp=target_exp+1;end
                if(target_exp>BIAS) pack={sign,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
                else if(rounded<(64'd1<<F)) pack={sign,{E{1'b0}},rounded[F-1:0]};
                else begin encoded=target_exp+BIAS;pack={sign,encoded,rounded[F-1:0]};end
            end
        end
    endfunction

    always @* begin
        sa=a[W-1];sb=b[W-1]^(OP==1);
        ea=a[F +: E];eb=b[F +: E];
        ma={1'b0,a[F-1:0]};mb={1'b0,b[F-1:0]};
        na=(ea==EMAX && ma!=0);nb=(eb==EMAX && mb!=0);
        ia=(ea==EMAX && ma==0);ib=(eb==EMAX && mb==0);
        za=(ea==0 && ma==0);zb=(eb==0 && mb==0);
        if(ea!=0) ma=ma|(64'd1<<F);
        if(eb!=0) mb=mb|(64'd1<<F);
        xa=(ea==0?1:ea)-BIAS-F;xb=(eb==0?1:eb)-BIAS-F;
        result=0;va=0;vb=0;magnitude=0;quotient=0;remainder=0;
        common_exp=0;diff=0;sign_out=sa^sb;
        if(na || nb) result=QNAN;
        else if(OP==0 || OP==1) begin
            if(ia && ib && sa!=sb) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia) result={sa,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else if(ib) result={sb,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else begin
                // Align around the larger exponent with F+4 low guard bits.
                common_exp=(xa>xb?xa:xb);
                va=shift_jam(ma<<(F+4),common_exp-xa);
                vb=shift_jam(mb<<(F+4),common_exp-xb);
                if(sa==sb) begin magnitude=va+vb;sign_out=sa;end
                else if(va>=vb) begin magnitude=va-vb;sign_out=sa;end
                else begin magnitude=vb-va;sign_out=sb;end
                // Exact cancellation rounds to +0, except -0 + -0.
                if(magnitude==0) sign_out=sa & sb;
                result=pack(sign_out,magnitude,common_exp-(F+4));
            end
        end else if(OP==2) begin
            if((ia&&zb)||(ib&&za)) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia||ib) result={sign_out,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else result=pack(sign_out,ma*mb,xa+xb);
        end else begin
            if((ia&&ib)||(za&&zb)) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia||zb) result={sign_out,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else if(ib||za) result={sign_out,{(E+F){1'b0}}};
            else begin
                // F+5 fractional quotient bits plus jammed remainder provide
                // guard/round/sticky information even for subnormal operands.
                for(n=0;n<F;n=n+1) begin
                    if(!ma[F]) begin ma=ma<<1;xa=xa-1;end
                    if(!mb[F]) begin mb=mb<<1;xb=xb-1;end
                end
                va=ma<<(F+5);quotient=va/mb;remainder=va%mb;
                if(remainder!=0) quotient=quotient|1;
                result=pack(sign_out,quotient,xa-xb-(F+5));
            end
        end
    end
endmodule

Floating-Point Classifierدسته‌بندی ممیز شناور fp_classify

Classifies sign, zero, subnormal, normal, infinity, and NaNدسته‌بندی sign، zero، subnormal، normal، infinity و NaN.

rtl/fp_classify.v

Explanationتوضیح

Classifies sign, zero, subnormal, normal, infinity, and NaN

IEEE-754-style floating-point datapath with parameterized exponent/fraction widths; special values are handled explicitly.

  • Source structure: This file contains a single module.
  • Reset: No reset
  • Handshake: No start/busy/done handshake; drive the control inputs directly.
  • Parameters: E, F, W

دسته‌بندی sign، zero، subnormal، normal، infinity و NaN.

دیتاپدیت ممیز شناور به سبک IEEE-754 با عرض‌های پارامتریک توان/کسر؛ مقادیر خاص به‌صورت صریح مدیریت می‌شوند.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل فقط یک ماژول دارد.
  • ریست: بدون ریست
  • Handshake: handshake start/busy/done ندارد؛ ورودی‌های کنترل را مستقیم بدهید.
  • پارامترها: E, F, W

rtl/fp_classify.v
// Module: fp_classify. Original group: floating_point.
module fp_classify #(parameter E=5,F=10,W=1+E+F)(input [W-1:0] x,
    output sign,is_zero,is_subnormal,is_normal,is_inf,is_nan);
    wire exp_zero=(x[F +: E]==0);wire exp_ones=(&x[F +: E]);
    wire frac_zero=(x[F-1:0]==0);
    assign sign=x[W-1];assign is_zero=exp_zero&&frac_zero;
    assign is_subnormal=exp_zero&&!frac_zero;assign is_normal=!exp_zero&&!exp_ones;
    assign is_inf=exp_ones&&frac_zero;assign is_nan=exp_ones&&!frac_zero;
endmodule

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/fp_compare.v
// Module: fp_compare. Original group: floating_point.
// Ordered comparison: NaN makes unordered=1 and lt/eq/gt=0; signed zeros compare equal.
module fp_compare #(parameter E=5,F=10,W=1+E+F)(input [W-1:0] a,b,
    output reg lt,eq,gt,output unordered);
    wire na=(&a[F +: E]) && (|a[F-1:0]);
    wire nb=(&b[F +: E]) && (|b[F-1:0]);
    assign unordered=na||nb;
    always @* begin
        lt=0;eq=0;gt=0;
        if(!unordered) begin
            if(a==b || ((a[W-2:0]==0)&&(b[W-2:0]==0))) eq=1;
            else begin
                if(a[W-1]!=b[W-1]) lt=a[W-1];
                else if(a[W-1]) lt=a[W-2:0]>b[W-2:0];
                else lt=a[W-2:0]<b[W-2:0];
                gt=!lt;
            end
        end
    end
endmodule

Floating-Point Stream Wrapperجریان ممیز شناور fp_stream

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/fp_stream.v
// Module: fp_stream. Original group: floating_point.
// One-entry elastic output register: accepts and retires together if ready.
module fp_stream #(parameter E=5,F=10,OP=0,W=1+E+F)(
    input clk,rst_n,in_valid,output in_ready,input [W-1:0] a,b,
    output reg out_valid,input out_ready,output reg [W-1:0] result
);
    wire [W-1:0] computed;
    fp_stream_helper_fp_arithmetic #(E,F,OP) arithmetic(a,b,computed);
    assign in_ready=!out_valid || out_ready;
    always @(posedge clk)
        if(!rst_n) begin out_valid<=0;result<=0;end
        else if(in_ready) begin out_valid<=in_valid;if(in_valid) result<=computed;end
endmodule


// ---------------- PRIVATE HELPERS ----------------
module fp_stream_helper_fp_arithmetic #(parameter E=5,F=10,OP=0,W=1+E+F)(
    input [W-1:0] a,b,output reg [W-1:0] result
);
    localparam BIAS=(1<<(E-1))-1;
    localparam EMAX=(1<<E)-1;
    localparam [W-1:0] QNAN=((64'd1*((1<<E)-1))<<F) | (64'd1<<(F-1));
    reg sa,sb,sign_out,na,nb,ia,ib,za,zb;
    integer ea,eb,xa,xb,common_exp,diff,n;
    reg [63:0] ma,mb,va,vb,magnitude,quotient,remainder;

    // Right shift with sticky bit; preserves all discarded information needed
    // by the final rounding operation when at least three guard bits remain.
    function [63:0] shift_jam;
        input [63:0] value;input integer amount;
        reg sticky;
        begin
            if(amount<=0) shift_jam=value;
            else if(amount>=64) shift_jam=(value!=0);
            else begin sticky=((value & ((64'd1<<amount)-1))!=0);
                shift_jam=(value>>amount)|sticky;
            end
        end
    endfunction

    // Pack integer magnitude * 2**exp2, including subnormal rounding.
    function [W-1:0] pack;
        input sign;input [63:0] mag;input integer exp2;
        integer msb,i,unbiased,shift,target_exp;
        reg [63:0] rounded,discarded,half;
        reg [E-1:0] encoded;
        begin
            pack={sign,{(E+F){1'b0}}}; msb=-1;rounded=0;discarded=0;half=0;encoded=0;
            for(i=0;i<64;i=i+1) if(mag[i]) msb=i;
            if(msb>=0) begin
                unbiased=msb+exp2;
                // Subnormal values use a fixed quantum 2**(1-BIAS-F).
                target_exp=(unbiased<1-BIAS)?(1-BIAS):unbiased;
                shift=target_exp-F-exp2;
                if(shift>64) rounded=0;
                else if(shift==64) begin
                    rounded=(mag>64'h8000000000000000)?1:0;
                end else if(shift>0) begin
                    rounded=mag>>shift;
                    discarded=mag & ((64'd1<<shift)-1);
                    half=64'd1<<(shift-1);
                    if(discarded>half || (discarded==half && rounded[0])) rounded=rounded+1;
                end else rounded=mag<<(-shift);
                if(rounded>=(64'd1<<(F+1))) begin rounded=rounded>>1;target_exp=target_exp+1;end
                if(target_exp>BIAS) pack={sign,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
                else if(rounded<(64'd1<<F)) pack={sign,{E{1'b0}},rounded[F-1:0]};
                else begin encoded=target_exp+BIAS;pack={sign,encoded,rounded[F-1:0]};end
            end
        end
    endfunction

    always @* begin
        sa=a[W-1];sb=b[W-1]^(OP==1);
        ea=a[F +: E];eb=b[F +: E];
        ma={1'b0,a[F-1:0]};mb={1'b0,b[F-1:0]};
        na=(ea==EMAX && ma!=0);nb=(eb==EMAX && mb!=0);
        ia=(ea==EMAX && ma==0);ib=(eb==EMAX && mb==0);
        za=(ea==0 && ma==0);zb=(eb==0 && mb==0);
        if(ea!=0) ma=ma|(64'd1<<F);
        if(eb!=0) mb=mb|(64'd1<<F);
        xa=(ea==0?1:ea)-BIAS-F;xb=(eb==0?1:eb)-BIAS-F;
        result=0;va=0;vb=0;magnitude=0;quotient=0;remainder=0;
        common_exp=0;diff=0;sign_out=sa^sb;
        if(na || nb) result=QNAN;
        else if(OP==0 || OP==1) begin
            if(ia && ib && sa!=sb) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia) result={sa,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else if(ib) result={sb,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else begin
                // Align around the larger exponent with F+4 low guard bits.
                common_exp=(xa>xb?xa:xb);
                va=shift_jam(ma<<(F+4),common_exp-xa);
                vb=shift_jam(mb<<(F+4),common_exp-xb);
                if(sa==sb) begin magnitude=va+vb;sign_out=sa;end
                else if(va>=vb) begin magnitude=va-vb;sign_out=sa;end
                else begin magnitude=vb-va;sign_out=sb;end
                // Exact cancellation rounds to +0, except -0 + -0.
                if(magnitude==0) sign_out=sa & sb;
                result=pack(sign_out,magnitude,common_exp-(F+4));
            end
        end else if(OP==2) begin
            if((ia&&zb)||(ib&&za)) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia||ib) result={sign_out,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else result=pack(sign_out,ma*mb,xa+xb);
        end else begin
            if((ia&&ib)||(za&&zb)) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia||zb) result={sign_out,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else if(ib||za) result={sign_out,{(E+F){1'b0}}};
            else begin
                // F+5 fractional quotient bits plus jammed remainder provide
                // guard/round/sticky information even for subnormal operands.
                for(n=0;n<F;n=n+1) begin
                    if(!ma[F]) begin ma=ma<<1;xa=xa-1;end
                    if(!mb[F]) begin mb=mb<<1;xb=xb-1;end
                end
                va=ma<<(F+5);quotient=va/mb;remainder=va%mb;
                if(remainder!=0) quotient=quotient|1;
                result=pack(sign_out,quotient,xa-xb-(F+5));
            end
        end
    end
endmodule

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/fp16_adder.v
// Module: fp16_adder. Original group: floating_point.
// Binary16 convenience wrappers use the same elastic ready/valid interface.
module fp16_adder(input clk,rst_n,in_valid,output in_ready,input [15:0] a,b,
    output out_valid,input out_ready,output [15:0] result);
    fp16_adder_helper_fp_stream #(5,10,0) core(clk,rst_n,in_valid,in_ready,a,b,out_valid,out_ready,result);
endmodule


// ---------------- PRIVATE HELPERS ----------------
module fp16_adder_helper_fp_arithmetic #(parameter E=5,F=10,OP=0,W=1+E+F)(
    input [W-1:0] a,b,output reg [W-1:0] result
);
    localparam BIAS=(1<<(E-1))-1;
    localparam EMAX=(1<<E)-1;
    localparam [W-1:0] QNAN=((64'd1*((1<<E)-1))<<F) | (64'd1<<(F-1));
    reg sa,sb,sign_out,na,nb,ia,ib,za,zb;
    integer ea,eb,xa,xb,common_exp,diff,n;
    reg [63:0] ma,mb,va,vb,magnitude,quotient,remainder;

    // Right shift with sticky bit; preserves all discarded information needed
    // by the final rounding operation when at least three guard bits remain.
    function [63:0] shift_jam;
        input [63:0] value;input integer amount;
        reg sticky;
        begin
            if(amount<=0) shift_jam=value;
            else if(amount>=64) shift_jam=(value!=0);
            else begin sticky=((value & ((64'd1<<amount)-1))!=0);
                shift_jam=(value>>amount)|sticky;
            end
        end
    endfunction

    // Pack integer magnitude * 2**exp2, including subnormal rounding.
    function [W-1:0] pack;
        input sign;input [63:0] mag;input integer exp2;
        integer msb,i,unbiased,shift,target_exp;
        reg [63:0] rounded,discarded,half;
        reg [E-1:0] encoded;
        begin
            pack={sign,{(E+F){1'b0}}}; msb=-1;rounded=0;discarded=0;half=0;encoded=0;
            for(i=0;i<64;i=i+1) if(mag[i]) msb=i;
            if(msb>=0) begin
                unbiased=msb+exp2;
                // Subnormal values use a fixed quantum 2**(1-BIAS-F).
                target_exp=(unbiased<1-BIAS)?(1-BIAS):unbiased;
                shift=target_exp-F-exp2;
                if(shift>64) rounded=0;
                else if(shift==64) begin
                    rounded=(mag>64'h8000000000000000)?1:0;
                end else if(shift>0) begin
                    rounded=mag>>shift;
                    discarded=mag & ((64'd1<<shift)-1);
                    half=64'd1<<(shift-1);
                    if(discarded>half || (discarded==half && rounded[0])) rounded=rounded+1;
                end else rounded=mag<<(-shift);
                if(rounded>=(64'd1<<(F+1))) begin rounded=rounded>>1;target_exp=target_exp+1;end
                if(target_exp>BIAS) pack={sign,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
                else if(rounded<(64'd1<<F)) pack={sign,{E{1'b0}},rounded[F-1:0]};
                else begin encoded=target_exp+BIAS;pack={sign,encoded,rounded[F-1:0]};end
            end
        end
    endfunction

    always @* begin
        sa=a[W-1];sb=b[W-1]^(OP==1);
        ea=a[F +: E];eb=b[F +: E];
        ma={1'b0,a[F-1:0]};mb={1'b0,b[F-1:0]};
        na=(ea==EMAX && ma!=0);nb=(eb==EMAX && mb!=0);
        ia=(ea==EMAX && ma==0);ib=(eb==EMAX && mb==0);
        za=(ea==0 && ma==0);zb=(eb==0 && mb==0);
        if(ea!=0) ma=ma|(64'd1<<F);
        if(eb!=0) mb=mb|(64'd1<<F);
        xa=(ea==0?1:ea)-BIAS-F;xb=(eb==0?1:eb)-BIAS-F;
        result=0;va=0;vb=0;magnitude=0;quotient=0;remainder=0;
        common_exp=0;diff=0;sign_out=sa^sb;
        if(na || nb) result=QNAN;
        else if(OP==0 || OP==1) begin
            if(ia && ib && sa!=sb) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia) result={sa,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else if(ib) result={sb,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else begin
                // Align around the larger exponent with F+4 low guard bits.
                common_exp=(xa>xb?xa:xb);
                va=shift_jam(ma<<(F+4),common_exp-xa);
                vb=shift_jam(mb<<(F+4),common_exp-xb);
                if(sa==sb) begin magnitude=va+vb;sign_out=sa;end
                else if(va>=vb) begin magnitude=va-vb;sign_out=sa;end
                else begin magnitude=vb-va;sign_out=sb;end
                // Exact cancellation rounds to +0, except -0 + -0.
                if(magnitude==0) sign_out=sa & sb;
                result=pack(sign_out,magnitude,common_exp-(F+4));
            end
        end else if(OP==2) begin
            if((ia&&zb)||(ib&&za)) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia||ib) result={sign_out,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else result=pack(sign_out,ma*mb,xa+xb);
        end else begin
            if((ia&&ib)||(za&&zb)) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia||zb) result={sign_out,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else if(ib||za) result={sign_out,{(E+F){1'b0}}};
            else begin
                // F+5 fractional quotient bits plus jammed remainder provide
                // guard/round/sticky information even for subnormal operands.
                for(n=0;n<F;n=n+1) begin
                    if(!ma[F]) begin ma=ma<<1;xa=xa-1;end
                    if(!mb[F]) begin mb=mb<<1;xb=xb-1;end
                end
                va=ma<<(F+5);quotient=va/mb;remainder=va%mb;
                if(remainder!=0) quotient=quotient|1;
                result=pack(sign_out,quotient,xa-xb-(F+5));
            end
        end
    end
endmodule

module fp16_adder_helper_fp_stream #(parameter E=5,F=10,OP=0,W=1+E+F)(
    input clk,rst_n,in_valid,output in_ready,input [W-1:0] a,b,
    output reg out_valid,input out_ready,output reg [W-1:0] result
);
    wire [W-1:0] computed;
    fp16_adder_helper_fp_arithmetic #(E,F,OP) arithmetic(a,b,computed);
    assign in_ready=!out_valid || out_ready;
    always @(posedge clk)
        if(!rst_n) begin out_valid<=0;result<=0;end
        else if(in_ready) begin out_valid<=in_valid;if(in_valid) result<=computed;end
endmodule

Testbenchتست‌بنچ

No dedicated testbench is committed for this module.تست‌بنچ اختصاصی برای این ماژول در مخزن موجود نیست.

FP16 Subtractorتفریق‌کنندهٔ FP16 fp16_subtractor

Binary16 OP=1 wrapper with ready/valid stream interfaceپوشش Binary16 با OP=1 و رابط ready/valid.

rtl/fp16_subtractor.v

Explanationتوضیح

Binary16 OP=1 wrapper with ready/valid stream interface

IEEE-754-style floating-point datapath with parameterized exponent/fraction widths; special values are handled explicitly.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus 2 helper module(s): fp16_subtractor_helper_fp_arithmetic, fp16_subtractor_helper_fp_stream.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Use the in_valid/in_ready input handshake and the out_valid/out_ready output handshake.
  • Parameters: None (fixed widths).

پوشش Binary16 با OP=1 و رابط ready/valid.

دیتاپدیت ممیز شناور به سبک IEEE-754 با عرض‌های پارامتریک توان/کسر؛ مقادیر خاص به‌صورت صریح مدیریت می‌شوند.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه 2 ماژول کمکی است: fp16_subtractor_helper_fp_arithmetic, fp16_subtractor_helper_fp_stream.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: از handshake ورودی in_valid/in_ready و خروجی out_valid/out_ready استفاده کنید.
  • پارامترها: ندارد (عرض‌ها ثابت).

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: a, b (+1)"] --> M["fp16_subtractor"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+1)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: result"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: in_ready, out_valid"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The wrapper uses an elastic ready/valid handshake: accept input when in_valid and in_ready are both high, and present the result when out_valid and out_ready are both high.
پوشش از handshake کشسان ready/valid استفاده می‌کند: ورودی را وقتی in_valid و in_ready هر دو بالا هستند بپذیر و نتیجه را وقتی out_valid و out_ready هر دو بالا هستند ارائه کن.
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> EMPTY
    EMPTY --> FULL: in_valid and in_ready
    FULL --> EMPTY: out_valid and out_ready

    output out_valid,input out_ready,output [15:0] result);
    fp16_subtractor_helper_fp_stream #(5,10,1) core(clk,rst_n,in_valid,in_ready,a,b,out_valid,out_ready,result);
endmodule


// ---------------- PRIVATE HELPERS ----------------
module fp16_subtractor_helper_fp_arithmetic #(parameter E=5,F=10,OP=0,W=1+E+F)(
    input [W-1:0] a,b,output reg [W-1:0] result
);
    localparam BIAS=(1<<(E-1))-1;
    localparam EMAX=(1<<E)-1;
    localparam [W-1:0] QNAN=((64'd1*((1<<E)-1))<<F) | (64'd1<<(F-1));
    reg sa,sb,sign_out,na,nb,ia,ib,za,zb;
    integer ea,eb,xa,xb,common_exp,diff,n;
    reg [63:0] ma,mb,va,vb,magnitude,quotient,remainder;

    // by the final rounding operation when at least three guard bits remain.

        reg sticky;

            else if(amount>=64) shift_jam=(value!=0);
            else begin sticky=((value & ((64'd1<<amount)-1))!=0);
                shift_jam=(value>>amount)|sticky;
            end
        end
    endfunction

    // Pack integer magnitude * 2**exp2, including subnormal rounding.
    function [W-1:0] pack;
        input sign;input [63:0] mag;input integer exp2;
        integer msb,i,unbiased,shift,target_exp;
        reg [63:0] rounded,discarded,half;
        reg [E-1:0] encoded;

                target_exp=(unbiased<1-BIAS)?(1-BIAS):unbiased;
                shift=target_exp-F-exp2;
                if(shift>64) rounded=0;
                else if(shift==64) begin
                    rounded=(mag>64'h8000000000000000)?1:0;
                end else if(shift>0) begin
                    rounded=mag>>shift;
                    discarded=mag & ((64'd1<<shift)-1);
                    half=64'd1<<(shift-1);
                    if(discarded>half || (discarded==half && rounded[0])) rounded=rounded+1;
                end else rounded=mag<<(-shift);
                if(rounded>=(64'd1<<(F+1))) begin rounded=rounded>>1;target_exp=target_exp+1;end
                if(target_exp>BIAS) pack={sign,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
                else if(rounded<(64'd1<<F)) pack={sign,{E{1'b0}},rounded[F-1:0]};
                else begin encoded=target_exp+BIAS;pack={sign,encoded,rounded[F-1:0]};end
            end
        end
    endfunction

    always @* begin
        sa=a[W-1];sb=b[W-1]^(OP==1);
        ea=a[F +: E];eb=b[F +: E];
        ma={1'b0,a[F-1:0]};mb={1'b0,b[F-1:0]};
        na=(ea==EMAX && ma!=0);nb=(eb==EMAX && mb!=0);
        ia=(ea==EMAX && ma==0);ib=(eb==EMAX && mb==0);
        za=(ea==0 && ma==0);zb=(eb==0 && mb==0);
        if(ea!=0) ma=ma|(64'd1<<F);
        if(eb!=0) mb=mb|(64'd1<<F);
        xa=(ea==0?1:ea)-BIAS-F;xb=(eb==0?1:eb)-BIAS-F;
        result=0;va=0;vb=0;magnitude=0;quotient=0;remainder=0;
        common_exp=0;diff=0;sign_out=sa^sb;
        if(na || nb) result=QNAN;
        else if(OP==0 || OP==1) begin
            if(ia && ib && sa!=sb) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia) result={sa,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else if(ib) result={sb,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else begin

                common_exp=(xa>xb?xa:xb);
                va=shift_jam(ma<<(F+4),common_exp-xa);
                vb=shift_jam(mb<<(F+4),common_exp-xb);

                else if(va>=vb) begin magnitude=va-vb;sign_out=sa;end
                else begin magnitude=vb-va;sign_out=sb;end
                // Exact cancellation rounds to +0, except -0 + -0.
                if(magnitude==0) sign_out=sa & sb;
                result=pack(sign_out,magnitude,common_exp-(F+4));

            if((ia&&zb)||(ib&&za)) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia||ib) result={sign_out,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else result=pack(sign_out,ma*mb,xa+xb);
        end else begin
            if((ia&&ib)||(za&&zb)) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia||zb) result={sign_out,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else if(ib||za) result={sign_out,{(E+F){1'b0}}};
            else begin

                // guard/round/sticky information even for subnormal operands.
                for(n=0;n<F;n=n+1) begin

                    if(!mb[F]) begin mb=mb<<1;xb=xb-1;end

                if(remainder!=0) quotient=quotient|1;
                result=pack(sign_out,quotient,xa-xb-(F+5));
            end
        end
    end
endmodule

    input clk,rst_n,in_valid,output in_ready,input [W-1:0] a,b,
    output reg out_valid,input out_ready,output reg [W-1:0] result

// Module: fp16_multiplier. Original group: floating_point.
module fp16_multiplier(input clk,rst_n,in_valid,output in_ready,input [15:0] a,b,
    output out_valid,input out_ready,output [15:0] result);
    fp16_multiplier_helper_fp_stream #(5,10,2) core(clk,rst_n,in_valid,in_ready,a,b,out_valid,out_ready,result);
endmodule


// ---------------- PRIVATE HELPERS ----------------
module fp16_multiplier_helper_fp_arithmetic #(parameter E=5,F=10,OP=0,W=1+E+F)(
    input [W-1:0] a,b,output reg [W-1:0] result
);
    localparam BIAS=(1<<(E-1))-1;
    localparam EMAX=(1<<E)-1;
    localparam [W-1:0] QNAN=((64'd1*((1<<E)-1))<<F) | (64'd1<<(F-1));
    reg sa,sb,sign_out,na,nb,ia,ib,za,zb;
    integer ea,eb,xa,xb,common_exp,diff,n;
    reg [63:0] ma,mb,va,vb,magnitude,quotient,remainder;

    // Right shift with sticky bit; preserves all discarded information needed
    // by the final rounding operation when at least three guard bits remain.
    function [63:0] shift_jam;
        input [63:0] value;input integer amount;
        reg sticky;
        begin
            if(amount<=0) shift_jam=value;
            else if(amount>=64) shift_jam=(value!=0);
            else begin sticky=((value & ((64'd1<<amount)-1))!=0);
                shift_jam=(value>>amount)|sticky;
            end
        end
    endfunction

    // Pack integer magnitude * 2**exp2, including subnormal rounding.
    function [W-1:0] pack;
        input sign;input [63:0] mag;input integer exp2;
        integer msb,i,unbiased,shift,target_exp;
        reg [63:0] rounded,discarded,half;
        reg [E-1:0] encoded;
        begin
            pack={sign,{(E+F){1'b0}}}; msb=-1;rounded=0;discarded=0;half=0;encoded=0;
            for(i=0;i<64;i=i+1) if(mag[i]) msb=i;
            if(msb>=0) begin
                unbiased=msb+exp2;
                // Subnormal values use a fixed quantum 2**(1-BIAS-F).
                target_exp=(unbiased<1-BIAS)?(1-BIAS):unbiased;
                shift=target_exp-F-exp2;
                if(shift>64) rounded=0;
                else if(shift==64) begin
                    rounded=(mag>64'h8000000000000000)?1:0;
                end else if(shift>0) begin
                    rounded=mag>>shift;
                    discarded=mag & ((64'd1<<shift)-1);
                    half=64'd1<<(shift-1);
                    if(discarded>half || (discarded==half && rounded[0])) rounded=rounded+1;
                end else rounded=mag<<(-shift);
                if(rounded>=(64'd1<<(F+1))) begin rounded=rounded>>1;target_exp=target_exp+1;end
                if(target_exp>BIAS) pack={sign,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
                else if(rounded<(64'd1<<F)) pack={sign,{E{1'b0}},rounded[F-1:0]};
                else begin encoded=target_exp+BIAS;pack={sign,encoded,rounded[F-1:0]};end
            end
        end
    endfunction

    always @* begin
        sa=a[W-1];sb=b[W-1]^(OP==1);
        ea=a[F +: E];eb=b[F +: E];
        ma={1'b0,a[F-1:0]};mb={1'b0,b[F-1:0]};
        na=(ea==EMAX && ma!=0);nb=(eb==EMAX && mb!=0);
        ia=(ea==EMAX && ma==0);ib=(eb==EMAX && mb==0);
        za=(ea==0 && ma==0);zb=(eb==0 && mb==0);
        if(ea!=0) ma=ma|(64'd1<<F);
        if(eb!=0) mb=mb|(64'd1<<F);
        xa=(ea==0?1:ea)-BIAS-F;xb=(eb==0?1:eb)-BIAS-F;
        result=0;va=0;vb=0;magnitude=0;quotient=0;remainder=0;
        common_exp=0;diff=0;sign_out=sa^sb;
        if(na || nb) result=QNAN;
        else if(OP==0 || OP==1) begin
            if(ia && ib && sa!=sb) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia) result={sa,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else if(ib) result={sb,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else begin
                // Align around the larger exponent with F+4 low guard bits.
                common_exp=(xa>xb?xa:xb);
                va=shift_jam(ma<<(F+4),common_exp-xa);
                vb=shift_jam(mb<<(F+4),common_exp-xb);
                if(sa==sb) begin magnitude=va+vb;sign_out=sa;end
                else if(va>=vb) begin magnitude=va-vb;sign_out=sa;end
                else begin magnitude=vb-va;sign_out=sb;end
                // Exact cancellation rounds to +0, except -0 + -0.
                if(magnitude==0) sign_out=sa & sb;
                result=pack(sign_out,magnitude,common_exp-(F+4));
            end
        end else if(OP==2) begin
            if((ia&&zb)||(ib&&za)) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia||ib) result={sign_out,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else result=pack(sign_out,ma*mb,xa+xb);
        end else begin
            if((ia&&ib)||(za&&zb)) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia||zb) result={sign_out,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else if(ib||za) result={sign_out,{(E+F){1'b0}}};
            else begin
                // F+5 fractional quotient bits plus jammed remainder provide
                // guard/round/sticky information even for subnormal operands.
                for(n=0;n<F;n=n+1) begin
                    if(!ma[F]) begin ma=ma<<1;xa=xa-1;end
                    if(!mb[F]) begin mb=mb<<1;xb=xb-1;end
                end
                va=ma<<(F+5);quotient=va/mb;remainder=va%mb;
                if(remainder!=0) quotient=quotient|1;
                result=pack(sign_out,quotient,xa-xb-(F+5));
            end
        end
    end
endmodule

module fp16_multiplier_helper_fp_stream #(parameter E=5,F=10,OP=0,W=1+E+F)(
    input clk,rst_n,in_valid,output in_ready,input [W-1:0] a,b,
    output reg out_valid,input out_ready,output reg [W-1:0] result
);
    wire [W-1:0] computed;
    fp16_multiplier_helper_fp_arithmetic #(E,F,OP) arithmetic(a,b,computed);
    assign in_ready=!out_valid || out_ready;
    always @(posedge clk)
        if(!rst_n) begin out_valid<=0;result<=0;end
        else if(in_ready) begin out_valid<=in_valid;if(in_valid) result<=computed;end
endmodule

Floating Pointممیز شناور

FP16 Dividerتقسیم‌کنندهٔ FP16 fp16_divider

Binary16 OP=3 wrapper with ready/valid stream interfaceپوشش Binary16 با OP=3 و رابط ready/valid.

rtl/fp16_divider.v

Explanationتوضیح

Binary16 OP=3 wrapper with ready/valid stream interface

IEEE-754-style floating-point datapath with parameterized exponent/fraction widths; special values are handled explicitly.

  • Source structure: This file contains the public top plus 2 helper module(s): fp16_divider_helper_fp_arithmetic, fp16_divider_helper_fp_stream.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: Use the in_valid/in_ready input handshake and the out_valid/out_ready output handshake.
  • Parameters: None (fixed widths).

پوشش Binary16 با OP=3 و رابط ready/valid.

دیتاپدیت ممیز شناور به سبک IEEE-754 با عرض‌های پارامتریک توان/کسر؛ مقادیر خاص به‌صورت صریح مدیریت می‌شوند.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل شامل top عمومی به‌همراه 2 ماژول کمکی است: fp16_divider_helper_fp_arithmetic, fp16_divider_helper_fp_stream.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: از handshake ورودی in_valid/in_ready و خروجی out_valid/out_ready استفاده کنید.
  • پارامترها: ندارد (عرض‌ها ثابت).

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

The wrapper uses an elastic ready/valid handshake: accept input when in_valid and in_ready are both high, and present the result when out_valid and out_ready are both high.
پوشش از handshake کشسان ready/valid استفاده می‌کند: ورودی را وقتی in_valid و in_ready هر دو بالا هستند بپذیر و نتیجه را وقتی out_valid و out_ready هر دو بالا هستند ارائه کن.

    EMPTY --> FULL: in_valid and in_ready
    FULL --> EMPTY: out_valid and out_ready

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/fp16_divider.v
// Module: fp16_divider. Original group: floating_point.
module fp16_divider(input clk,rst_n,in_valid,output in_ready,input [15:0] a,b,
    output out_valid,input out_ready,output [15:0] result);
    fp16_divider_helper_fp_stream #(5,10,3) core(clk,rst_n,in_valid,in_ready,a,b,out_valid,out_ready,result);
endmodule


// ---------------- PRIVATE HELPERS ----------------
module fp16_divider_helper_fp_arithmetic #(parameter E=5,F=10,OP=0,W=1+E+F)(
    input [W-1:0] a,b,output reg [W-1:0] result
);
    localparam BIAS=(1<<(E-1))-1;
    localparam EMAX=(1<<E)-1;
    localparam [W-1:0] QNAN=((64'd1*((1<<E)-1))<<F) | (64'd1<<(F-1));
    reg sa,sb,sign_out,na,nb,ia,ib,za,zb;
    integer ea,eb,xa,xb,common_exp,diff,n;
    reg [63:0] ma,mb,va,vb,magnitude,quotient,remainder;

    // Right shift with sticky bit; preserves all discarded information needed
    // by the final rounding operation when at least three guard bits remain.
    function [63:0] shift_jam;
        input [63:0] value;input integer amount;
        reg sticky;
        begin
            if(amount<=0) shift_jam=value;
            else if(amount>=64) shift_jam=(value!=0);
            else begin sticky=((value & ((64'd1<<amount)-1))!=0);
                shift_jam=(value>>amount)|sticky;
            end
        end
    endfunction

    // Pack integer magnitude * 2**exp2, including subnormal rounding.
    function [W-1:0] pack;
        input sign;input [63:0] mag;input integer exp2;
        integer msb,i,unbiased,shift,target_exp;
        reg [63:0] rounded,discarded,half;
        reg [E-1:0] encoded;
        begin
            pack={sign,{(E+F){1'b0}}}; msb=-1;rounded=0;discarded=0;half=0;encoded=0;
            for(i=0;i<64;i=i+1) if(mag[i]) msb=i;
            if(msb>=0) begin
                unbiased=msb+exp2;
                // Subnormal values use a fixed quantum 2**(1-BIAS-F).
                target_exp=(unbiased<1-BIAS)?(1-BIAS):unbiased;
                shift=target_exp-F-exp2;
                if(shift>64) rounded=0;
                else if(shift==64) begin
                    rounded=(mag>64'h8000000000000000)?1:0;
                end else if(shift>0) begin
                    rounded=mag>>shift;
                    discarded=mag & ((64'd1<<shift)-1);
                    half=64'd1<<(shift-1);
                    if(discarded>half || (discarded==half && rounded[0])) rounded=rounded+1;
                end else rounded=mag<<(-shift);
                if(rounded>=(64'd1<<(F+1))) begin rounded=rounded>>1;target_exp=target_exp+1;end
                if(target_exp>BIAS) pack={sign,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
                else if(rounded<(64'd1<<F)) pack={sign,{E{1'b0}},rounded[F-1:0]};
                else begin encoded=target_exp+BIAS;pack={sign,encoded,rounded[F-1:0]};end
            end
        end
    endfunction

    always @* begin
        sa=a[W-1];sb=b[W-1]^(OP==1);
        ea=a[F +: E];eb=b[F +: E];
        ma={1'b0,a[F-1:0]};mb={1'b0,b[F-1:0]};
        na=(ea==EMAX && ma!=0);nb=(eb==EMAX && mb!=0);
        ia=(ea==EMAX && ma==0);ib=(eb==EMAX && mb==0);
        za=(ea==0 && ma==0);zb=(eb==0 && mb==0);
        if(ea!=0) ma=ma|(64'd1<<F);
        if(eb!=0) mb=mb|(64'd1<<F);
        xa=(ea==0?1:ea)-BIAS-F;xb=(eb==0?1:eb)-BIAS-F;
        result=0;va=0;vb=0;magnitude=0;quotient=0;remainder=0;
        common_exp=0;diff=0;sign_out=sa^sb;
        if(na || nb) result=QNAN;
        else if(OP==0 || OP==1) begin
            if(ia && ib && sa!=sb) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia) result={sa,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else if(ib) result={sb,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else begin
                // Align around the larger exponent with F+4 low guard bits.
                common_exp=(xa>xb?xa:xb);
                va=shift_jam(ma<<(F+4),common_exp-xa);
                vb=shift_jam(mb<<(F+4),common_exp-xb);
                if(sa==sb) begin magnitude=va+vb;sign_out=sa;end
                else if(va>=vb) begin magnitude=va-vb;sign_out=sa;end
                else begin magnitude=vb-va;sign_out=sb;end
                // Exact cancellation rounds to +0, except -0 + -0.
                if(magnitude==0) sign_out=sa & sb;
                result=pack(sign_out,magnitude,common_exp-(F+4));
            end
        end else if(OP==2) begin
            if((ia&&zb)||(ib&&za)) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia||ib) result={sign_out,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else result=pack(sign_out,ma*mb,xa+xb);
        end else begin
            if((ia&&ib)||(za&&zb)) result=QNAN;
            else if(ia||zb) result={sign_out,{E{1'b1}},{F{1'b0}}};
            else if(ib||za) result={sign_out,{(E+F){1'b0}}};
            else begin
                // F+5 fractional quotient bits plus jammed remainder provide
                // guard/round/sticky information even for subnormal operands.
                for(n=0;n<F;n=n+1) begin
                    if(!ma[F]) begin ma=ma<<1;xa=xa-1;end
                    if(!mb[F]) begin mb=mb<<1;xb=xb-1;end
                end
                va=ma<<(F+5);quotient=va/mb;remainder=va%mb;
                if(remainder!=0) quotient=quotient|1;
                result=pack(sign_out,quotient,xa-xb-(F+5));
            end
        end
    end
endmodule

module fp16_divider_helper_fp_stream #(parameter E=5,F=10,OP=0,W=1+E+F)(
    input clk,rst_n,in_valid,output in_ready,input [W-1:0] a,b,
    output reg out_valid,input out_ready,output reg [W-1:0] result
);
    wire [W-1:0] computed;
    fp16_divider_helper_fp_arithmetic #(E,F,OP) arithmetic(a,b,computed);
    assign in_ready=!out_valid || out_ready;
    always @(posedge clk)
        if(!rst_n) begin out_valid<=0;result<=0;end
        else if(in_ready) begin out_valid<=in_valid;if(in_valid) result<=computed;end
endmodule

Sequence Detectorتشخیص‌دهندهٔ الگو sequence_detector

MSB-first pattern detector with Moore and Mealy outputs; OVERLAP selects overlap behaviorتشخیص الگو MSB-first با خروجی Moore و Mealy؛ OVERLAP رفتار هم‌پوشانی را انتخاب می‌کند.

rtl/sequence_detector.v

Explanationتوضیح

MSB-first pattern detector with Moore and Mealy outputs; OVERLAP selects overlap behavior

The FSM updates on clk; reset returns it to the idle state.

  • Source structure: This file contains a single module.
  • Reset: Synchronous active-low reset
  • Handshake: No start/busy/done handshake; drive the control inputs directly.
  • Parameters: P, PATTERN, OVERLAP

تشخیص الگو MSB-first با خروجی Moore و Mealy؛ OVERLAP رفتار هم‌پوشانی را انتخاب می‌کند.

FSM در clk به‌روز می‌شود؛ ریست آن را به حالت idle برمی‌گرداند.

  • ساختار منبع: این فایل فقط یک ماژول دارد.
  • ریست: ریست همگام فعال پایین
  • Handshake: handshake start/busy/done ندارد؛ ورودی‌های کنترل را مستقیم بدهید.
  • پارامترها: P, PATTERN, OVERLAP

Visual diagramنمودار تصویری

flowchart LR
    IN["data in: none"] --> M["sequence_detector"]
    CTL["control: clk, rst_n (+2)"] -. "control" .-> M
    M --> OUT["data out: none"]
    M -. "status" .-> ST["status: hit_mealy, hit_moore"]

Control / FSMکنترل / FSM

This is not a classical state-machine FSM. A P-bit history shift register plus a valid-bit count and a comparator detect the pattern. hit_mealy is combinational; hit_moore is the registered hit pulse.

این ماژول یک FSM کلاسیک حالت‌محور نیست. یک شیفت رجیستر تاریخچهٔ P بیتی به‌همراه شمارندهٔ بیت‌های معتبر و یک مقایسه‌کننده الگو را تشخیص می‌دهند. hit_mealy ترکیبی و hit_moore پالس ثبات‌دار است.

Parametersپارامترها

NameنامDefaultپیش‌فرضDescriptionتوضیح
P4Pattern widthعرض الگو
PATTERN4'b1011Pattern valueمقدار الگو
OVERLAP11 = allow overlapping matches۱ = اجازهٔ تطابق هم‌پوشان

Portsپورت‌ها

RTL sourceکد RTL

rtl/sequence_detector.v
// Module: sequence_detector. Original group: fsm.
// Pattern arrives MSB first. A hit consumes the final bit on the next clock edge.
// hit_mealy depends on bit_in/valid; hit_moore is the registered hit pulse.
// OVERLAP=0 discards all history following a match. P >= 1.
module sequence_detector #(parameter P=4,parameter [P-1:0] PATTERN=4'b1011,parameter OVERLAP=1)(
    input clk,rst_n,valid,bit_in,output hit_mealy,output reg hit_moore
);
    reg [P-1:0] history;integer count;
    wire [P-1:0] candidate=(history<<1)|bit_in;
    assign hit_mealy=valid && (count>=P-1) && (candidate==PATTERN);
    always @(posedge clk)
        if(!rst_n) begin history<=0;count<=0;hit_moore<=0;end
        else begin
            hit_moore<=hit_mealy;
            if(valid) begin
                if(hit_mealy&&!OVERLAP) begin history<=0;count<=0;end
                else begin history<=candidate;if(count<P) count<=count+1;end
            end
        end
endmodule
